### [1] [On-Shell Bootstrap of Loop Inflation Correlators with Spectral Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02686)
*Haoyuan Liu,Zhehan Qin,Jiayi Wu,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper introduces a novel Cosmological Bootstrap technique called spectral dispersion, which reconstructs loop-level cosmological correlators from their on-shell (nonlocal) data using analyticity and dS spectral decomposition. It yields compact expressions for 1-loop 3- and 4-point functions with massive scalar/vector exchanges and extends naturally to higher spins and banana graphs.
\n\n
本文提出了一种新的Cosmological Bootstrap技术——spectral dispersion，该方法利用解析性（analyticity）和dS谱分解（dS spectral decomposition），从在壳（on-shell）数据（即非局域信号）重构圈图级别的宇宙学关联函数。该方法得到了包含大质量标量/矢量玻色子交换的单圈3点和4点函数的简洁表达式，并可自然推广至高自旋和banana图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard perturbative computations of loop correlators in de Sitter (dS) space are technically cumbersome due to time integrals and divergences requiring renormalization. The authors aim to bypass these difficulties by leveraging the analytic structure of correlators and the fact that their nonlocal content is fully encoded in on-shell data, specifically as sums over quasinormal modes. This motivates a bootstrap approach rooted in physical principles rather than Feynman diagrams.
\n\n
在de Sitter（dS）空间中，标准微扰方法计算圈图关联函数因时间积分和需重整化的发散而技术上繁琐。作者旨在通过利用关联函数的解析结构及其非局域内容完全编码于在壳数据（具体表现为quasinormal modes的离散和）这一事实，绕过这些困难。这促使他们发展一种基于物理原理而非费曼图的bootstrap方法。

Method: The method, termed spectral dispersion, merges two key ideas: (1) dispersion relations that reconstruct a correlator from its discontinuities (on-shell data), up to local contact terms; and (2) the dS spectral decomposition, which expresses loop-level on-shell data as a discrete sum over quasinormal mode frequencies $\\nu$. Mathematically, this involves writing the correlator as an integral over spectral parameters, where the kernel is fixed by symmetries and the spectral density encodes the particle content. The approach handles both minimal and derivative couplings systematically.
\n\n
该方法称为spectral dispersion，融合了两个关键思想：(1) 利用dispersion relations从关联函数的不连续性（即在壳数据）重构关联函数，最多相差局域接触项；(2) dS spectral decomposition将圈图级别的在壳数据表示为quasinormal mode频率$\\nu$的离散和。数学上，该方法将关联函数写为对谱参数的积分，其中核由对称性固定，而谱密度编码粒子内容。该方法可系统处理最小耦合与导数耦合。

Result: The authors derive explicit, compact analytic expressions for 1-loop 3-point and 4-point cosmological correlators involving massive scalar and vector boson exchanges, for both direct and derivative couplings. These results are obtained without performing traditional time integrals, showcasing the efficiency of the spectral dispersion method. The framework also readily accommodates higher-spin exchanges and multi-loop banana topologies with dS-covariant dispersions.
\n\n
作者推导出了包含大质量标量和矢量玻色子交换（包括直接耦合与导数耦合）的单圈3点和4点宇宙学关联函数的显式、简洁解析表达式。这些结果无需进行传统的时间积分，展示了spectral dispersion方法的高效性。该框架还可方便地处理高自旋交换以及具有dS协变色散关系的多圈banana拓扑结构。

Conclusion: Spectral dispersion provides a powerful, intuitive, and computationally efficient framework for computing loop-level cosmological correlators in de Sitter space. By focusing on physical on-shell data and leveraging analyticity and dS representation theory, it avoids the technical complexities of standard perturbation theory and opens new avenues for exploring quantum effects in inflationary cosmology.
\n\n
Spectral dispersion为计算de Sitter空间中的圈图级别宇宙学关联函数提供了一个强大、直观且计算高效的框架。通过聚焦于物理的在壳数据，并利用解析性和dS表示论，该方法规避了标准微扰论的技术复杂性，为探索暴胀宇宙学中的量子效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We develop a new bootstrap strategy for cosmological correlators at loop level, which we call spectral dispersion. It is based on two conceptual observations that a correlator can be recovered from its on-shell data, also known as nonlocal signals, by analyticity up to local counterterms, and that the on-shell data for a loop process take the form of a discrete sum over quasinormal modes. Technically, our method combines the dS spectral decomposition with dispersion relations. Using this technique, we bootstrap new results in a simple and intuitive form for 3-point and 4-point correlators with 1-loop massive exchanges of scalar and vector bosons, either directly or derivatively coupled. Applications of this bootstrap technique to higher spins and higher-loop banana graphs with dS covariant dispersions but noncovariant couplings are also straightforward.

</details>


### [2] [Quintessential $α$-attractors fit DESI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02701)
*Alessandro Borys,Joaquin Masias,Marco Scalisi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that $\\alpha$-attractor quintessence models can effectively mimic the axion-like dark energy benchmark favored by DESI data, establishing a mapping between the axion decay constant $f_a$ and the attractor parameter $\\alpha$, with observations preferring $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$. Numerical solutions confirm agreement with the observed equation-of-state evolution $w(z)$ up to redshift $z \\sim \\mathcal{O}(1)$, and the authors argue that the required initial conditions are naturally realized in multifield attractor frameworks.
\n\n
本文表明，$\\alpha$-attractor quintessence 模型能够有效模拟 DESI 数据所偏好的 axion-like dark energy 基准模型，建立了 axion decay constant $f_a$ 与 attractor 参数 $\\alpha$ 之间的映射关系，观测数据偏好 $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$。数值解显示其与观测到的状态方程演化 $w(z)$ 在红移 $z \\sim \\mathcal{O}(1)$ 范围内高度一致，作者指出所需初始条件可在 multifield attractor 场景中自然实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent DESI results suggest evidence for dynamical dark energy, motivating the exploration of theoretically well-motivated scalar field models such as quintessence. The axion-like potential used in DESI analyses lacks a direct embedding in high-energy theory, whereas $\\alpha$-attractor models arise naturally from supergravity and string theory. The paper aims to bridge this gap by showing that $\\alpha$-attractors can reproduce the phenomenology of the DESI benchmark while offering a UV-complete theoretical foundation.
\n\n
近期 DESI 结果暗示 dynamical dark energy 的存在，促使人们探索理论上动机充分的标量场模型（如 quintessence）。DESI 分析中使用的 axion-like 势缺乏直接的高能理论嵌入，而 $\\alpha$-attractor 模型自然源于 supergravity 和 string theory。本文旨在弥合这一差距，证明 $\\alpha$-attractor 能复现 DESI 基准模型的现象学，同时提供 UV-complete 的理论基础。

Method: The authors compare the shape of the $\\alpha$-attractor potential near its \\emph{knee} (the transition region between plateau and steep descent) to the axion-like potential $V(\\phi) \\propto 1 + \\cos(\\phi/f_a)$. They derive an approximate equivalence by matching the curvature and slope at the present epoch, yielding a relation $f_a \\sim \\sqrt{\\alpha}\\, M_{\\rm Pl}$. They then numerically solve the Friedmann and Klein-Gordon equations for the background cosmology to compute $w(z)$ and compare it with DESI constraints. Finally, they discuss how suitable initial conditions—namely, the field starting near the top of the potential—are naturally achieved in multifield $\\alpha$-attractor scenarios through inflationary dynamics.
\n\n
作者将 $\\alpha$-attractor 势在其 \\emph{knee}（平台与陡降区之间的过渡区域）附近的形状与 axion-like 势 $V(\\phi) \\propto 1 + \\cos(\\phi/f_a)$ 进行比较。通过匹配当前时代的曲率与斜率，导出近似等价关系 $f_a \\sim \\sqrt{\\alpha}\\, M_{\\rm Pl}$。随后数值求解 Friedmann 方程和 Klein-Gordon 方程以计算背景宇宙学中的 $w(z)$，并与 DESI 约束进行对比。最后，他们讨论了合适的初始条件（即场初始位于势能顶部附近）如何通过 inflationary dynamics 在 multifield $\\alpha$-attractor 场景中自然实现。

Result: The $\\alpha$-attractor model with $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$ reproduces the DESI-preferred evolution of the dark energy equation of state $w(z)$ up to $z \\sim \\mathcal{O}(1)$. The mapping $f_a \\sim \\sqrt{\\alpha}\\, M_{\\rm Pl}$ translates observational constraints on $f_a$ into a preference for $\\alpha \\sim 1$, consistent with expectations from string compactifications. The required initial displacement of the quintessence field is shown to be naturally generated in multifield inflationary attractor models.
\n\n
具有 $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$ 的 $\\alpha$-attractor 模型成功复现了 DESI 所偏好的 dark energy 状态方程 $w(z)$ 的演化，直至红移 $z \\sim \\mathcal{O}(1)$。映射关系 $f_a \\sim \\sqrt{\\alpha}\\, M_{\\rm Pl}$ 将对 $f_a$ 的观测约束转化为对 $\\alpha \\sim 1$ 的偏好，这与 string compactifications 的预期一致。quintessence 场所需的初始位移被证明可在 multifield inflationary attractor 模型中自然产生。

Conclusion: The agreement between axion-like quintessence and $\\alpha$-attractor models is not coincidental but stems from a shared phenomenological requirement: that both the potential energy $V$ and its derivative $|V'|$ today are of order $H_0^2 M_{\\rm Pl}^2$. This condition ensures slow-roll evolution compatible with late-time cosmic acceleration. The $\\alpha$-attractor framework provides a theoretically robust realization of this condition, with $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$ favored by data and motivated by string theory, while also offering a natural mechanism for setting initial conditions via multifield dynamics.
\n\n
axion-like quintessence 与 $\\alpha$-attractor 模型之间的一致性并非偶然，而是源于共同的现象学要求：即今日的势能 $V$ 及其导数 $|V'|$ 均为 $H_0^2 M_{\\rm Pl}^2$ 量级。该条件确保了与晚期宇宙加速相容的 slow-roll 演化。$\\alpha$-attractor 框架为此条件提供了理论稳健的实现方式，其中数据偏好 $\\alpha = \\mathcal{O}(1)$，且该取值受 string theory 动机支持，同时通过 multifield dynamics 自然提供了初始条件的设定机制。

Abstract: We study quintessence in $α$-attractor models in light of recent DESI indications for dynamical dark energy. We show that the \emph{knee} of the attractor potential provides an excellent approximation to the axion-like quintessence model used as a DESI benchmark. This leads to a simple relation between the axion decay constant $f_a$ and the attractor parameter $α$, allowing the experimental constraints to be translated into a preference for $α=\mathcal{O}(1)$, in agreement with string-motivated expectations. We solve the background dynamics numerically and find good agreement with the DESI-preferred evolution of $w(z)$ up to $z\sim\mathcal{O}(1)$. More generally, we point out that the agreement between axion-like and attractor potentials reflects a common requirement imposed by the data: today's potential energy and slope are both of order the Hubble scale in Planck units. We finally comment on the origin of the required initial conditions, which can naturally arise in multifield attractor scenarios.

</details>


### [3] [Perturbative construction of amplitudes from on-shell trees with vacuum pairs: the all-plus four-gluon amplitude through order $\boldsymbol{g}^{\boldsymbol{6}}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02702)
*M. Maniatis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper introduces a fixed-order perturbative on-shell method to construct scattering amplitudes using only physical particle spectra and three-point on-shell data. Tree-level amplitudes from BCFW recursion are combined with unobservable "vacuum pairs," integrated over Lorentz-invariant phase space, with inclusion-exclusion signs handling overlapping regions. Applied to the all-plus four-gluon amplitude at $g^4$ and $g^6$, it reproduces known one- and two-loop results via polygon-based bookkeeping.
\n\n
本文提出了一种固定阶微扰的壳上（on-shell）振幅构造方法，仅依赖物理粒子谱和允许的三重点壳上振幅作为输入。该方法基于BCFW递归生成的树图振幅，并引入不可观测的“真空对”（vacuum pairs），在洛伦兹不变相空间中对这些对进行积分，并通过包含-排除原理（inclusion-exclusion）分配符号以处理相空间区域的重叠。作为检验，作者研究了全正螺旋度四胶子振幅在 $g^4$ 和 $g^6$ 阶的行为，利用多边形（polygon）分类组织树图贡献，成功复现了已知的一圈和两圈结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard loop-amplitude computations rely on off-shell virtual particles and gauge-dependent intermediate steps, obscuring physical intuition. The authors aim to develop a purely on-shell, gauge-invariant formulation of perturbative amplitudes that avoids unphysical degrees of freedom and directly uses observable data—namely, the spectrum and three-point amplitudes—as input, aligning with modern amplitude paradigms like the S-matrix program and on-shell methods.
\n\n
标准圈图振幅计算依赖于壳外（off-shell）虚粒子和规范依赖的中间步骤，掩盖了物理图像。作者旨在发展一种纯壳上、规范不变的微扰振幅表述，避免非物理自由度，直接以可观测量——即粒子谱和三重点振幅——作为输入，与S矩阵纲领（S-matrix program）及现代壳上方法（on-shell methods）的理念一致。

Method: The method constructs fixed-order amplitudes by summing over tree-level diagrams generated via BCFW recursion, augmented by additional internal on-shell pairs called "vacuum pairs" that do not correspond to asymptotic states. These pairs are integrated over the Lorentz-invariant phase space $d\Pi = \\frac{d^D k}{(2\\pi)^D} 2\\pi \\delta(k^2 - m^2) \\theta(k^0)$, and overlapping integration domains are assigned relative signs via inclusion-exclusion principles. The combinatorial structure is organized into polygon sectors (e.g., octagon, hexagon-quadrilateral), reflecting the topology of internal on-shell propagators.
\n\n
该方法通过求和由BCFW递归生成的树图振幅来构造固定阶振幅，并引入额外的内部壳上对——称为“真空对”（vacuum pairs）——这些对不对应渐近态。这些对在洛伦兹不变相空间 $d\\Pi = \\frac{d^D k}{(2\\pi)^D} 2\\pi \\delta(k^2 - m^2) \\theta(k^0)$ 中积分，重叠的积分区域通过包含-排除原理（inclusion-exclusion）分配相对符号。组合结构被组织为多边形扇区（如八边形、六边形-四边形等），反映内部壳上传播子的拓扑结构。

Result: At order $g^4$, the construction exactly reproduces the finite rational part of the one-loop all-plus four-gluon amplitude. At order $g^6$, the non-vanishing contributions come from four polygon sectors: octagon, hexagon-quadrilateral, two-pentagon, and three-quadrilateral. Their signed phase-space integrals collectively reproduce the full two-loop result, including planar, non-planar, and bow-tie topologies, without invoking traditional Feynman diagrams or virtual loops.
\n\n
在 $g^4$ 阶，该构造精确复现了一圈全正螺旋度四胶子振幅的有限有理部分。在 $g^6$ 阶，非零贡献来自四个多边形扇区：八边形、六边形-四边形、双五边形和三个四边形。它们带符号的相空间积分共同复现了完整的两圈结果，包括平面、非平面和bow-tie拓扑结构，而无需引入传统的费曼图或虚圈。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that loop-level amplitudes can be reconstructed entirely from on-shell data and tree-level building blocks, supplemented by vacuum pairs and phase-space integration with inclusion-exclusion signs. This provides a novel, manifestly gauge-invariant, and unitary framework for perturbative quantum field theory that bypasses off-shell formalism and aligns with the philosophy of the modern amplitude program.
\n\n
本工作表明，圈图振幅可以完全从壳上数据和树图构件出发重构，辅以真空对、相空间积分以及包含-排除符号分配。这为微扰量子场论提供了一个新颖的、明显规范不变且满足幺正性的框架，绕过了壳外形式体系，契合现代振幅纲领（modern amplitude program）的核心理念。

Abstract: We formulate a fixed-order perturbative on-shell construction of amplitudes. The basic input is the particle spectrum together with the allowed on-shell three-point amplitudes. The construction is formulated in terms of tree amplitudes generated by BCFW recursion, supplemented by additional unobservable state-conjugate on-shell pairs, called vacuum pairs, and integrated over the Lorentz-invariant phase space of these pairs. The relative signs are assigned as inclusion-exclusion signs for repeated phase-space ranges in the on-shell construction.
  As a test case, we study the color-ordered four-gluon all-plus amplitude through orders $g^4$ and $g^6$, and compare the resulting signed phase-space sums with the standard one- and two-loop contributions. The fixed-order bookkeeping of the tree amplitudes is organized in terms of polygons. At order $g^4$ the construction reproduces the finite rational one-loop result. At order $g^6$ the non-vanishing polygon sectors are the octagon, hexagon-quadrilateral, two-pentagon, and three-quadrilateral sectors. Taken together, they reproduce the known planar, non-planar, and bow-tie expressions.

</details>


### [4] [Gravitational index, black hole saddle degeneracy, and one-form symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02707)
*Davide Cassani,Gianmarco Esposto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates the degeneracy of the saddle-point contribution of supersymmetric black holes to the superconformal index in 4D SCFTs, linking it to a one-form symmetry. It explores the gravitational dual realization of this symmetry and its spontaneous breaking in the Cardy-like limit, where the black hole becomes a decompactified black brane.
\n\n
本文研究了四维超共形场论（SCFT）中超对称黑洞对超共形指数的鞍点贡献的简并性，并将其归因于理论中的一形式对称性（one-form symmetry）。文章探讨了该对称性及其在Cardy类极限下的自发破缺的引力对偶实现，在此极限下黑洞退紧致化为黑膜（black brane）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the observed degeneracy in the saddle-point approximation for the superconformal index contributed by supersymmetric black holes in 4D $\\mathcal{N}=1$ SCFTs. This degeneracy manifests as a logarithmic correction to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy and is tied to the presence of a global one-form symmetry in the boundary theory. Understanding its bulk gravitational origin and behavior under symmetry breaking is essential for clarifying the holographic dictionary beyond leading order.
\n\n
动机源于在四维 $\\mathcal{N}=1$ 超共形场论中，超对称黑洞对超共形指数的鞍点近似存在简并性。这种简并性表现为对贝肯斯坦-霍金熵的对数修正，并与边界理论中的全局一形式对称性相关。理解其体引力起源及对称性破缺下的行为，对于澄清全息字典在领头阶之外的结构至关重要。

Method: The authors analyze various holographic setups (e.g., AdS$_5 \\times S^5$ and related gauged supergravity truncations) to identify the bulk manifestation of the boundary one-form symmetry. They study the on-shell supergravity action near the index saddle and examine how the degeneracy arises from flat directions associated with gauge field holonomies. To probe spontaneous symmetry breaking, they take the Cardy-like limit ($\\omega_i \\to 0$), where the black hole horizon decompactifies into an infinite black brane, effectively sending the spatial volume to infinity.
\n\n
作者分析多种全息设定（如 AdS$_5 \\times S^5$ 及其相关的规范超引力截断），以识别边界一形式对称性在体中的体现。他们研究超引力作用量在指数鞍点附近的壳上行为，并考察简并性如何源于与规范场整体相位（holonomies）相关的平坦方向。为探究自发对称性破缺，他们采用 Cardy 类极限（$\\omega_i \\to 0$），在此极限下黑洞视界退紧致化为无限大黑膜，等效地将空间体积趋于无穷。

Result: The paper demonstrates that the one-form symmetry is realized gravitationally through large gauge transformations of the bulk $U(1)$ gauge fields, whose holonomies around non-contractible cycles correspond to conserved topological charges. The degeneracy of the index saddle is shown to originate from the invariance of the on-shell action under these transformations. In the Cardy-like limit, the symmetry is spontaneously broken due to the decompactification, lifting the degeneracy and yielding a unique dominant saddle corresponding to the black brane.
\n\n
论文表明，一形式对称性通过体中 $U(1)$ 规范场的大规范变换（large gauge transformations）在引力中实现，其在非可缩循环上的整体相位对应守恒的拓扑荷。指数鞍点的简并性被证明源于壳上作用量对此类变换的不变性。在 Cardy 类极限下，由于退紧致化导致对称性自发破缺，简并性被解除，得到唯一主导的鞍点，即黑膜解。

Conclusion: The degeneracy of the supersymmetric black hole saddle in the superconformal index is a direct consequence of a boundary one-form symmetry, with a precise gravitational dual realization in terms of bulk gauge field holonomies. The spontaneous breaking of this symmetry in the infinite-volume (Cardy-like) limit resolves the degeneracy and connects the finite-$N$ black hole physics to the planar black brane description, enriching the understanding of quantum gravity corrections in holography.
\n\n
超共形指数中超对称黑洞鞍点的简并性直接源于边界的一形式对称性，其在引力对偶中有精确实现，即体规范场的整体相位。在无限体积（Cardy 类）极限下，该对称性的自发破缺解除了简并性，将有限 $N$ 的黑洞物理与平面黑膜描述联系起来，深化了对全息中量子引力修正的理解。

Abstract: It is known that the saddle describing the contribution of supersymmetric black holes to the index of four-dimensional superconformal field theories is degenerate. This degeneracy is a consequence of the one-form symmetry of the theory, and can also be seen as a specific logarithmic correction to the saddle-point action. We discuss the gravitational realization of the one-form symmetry and of the index saddle degeneracy in different holographic setups. In order to illustrate the spontaneous breaking of the one-form symmetry at infinite volume, we employ a gravitational realization of the Cardy-like limit of small chemical potentials where the black hole decompactifies into a black brane.

</details>


### [5] [Supersymmetry, Large Extra Dimensions and the Gravitino Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02708)
*Leonardo Bersigotti,Dieter Lust,Marco Scalisi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper explores a possible correlation between the non-observation of supersymmetry and extra dimensions by linking the gravitino mass $m_{3/2}$ to the Kaluza-Klein (KK) scale $M_{\\text{KK}}$ in 4D $\\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravity from Type II string compactifications. Using an anisotropy exponent $\\alpha$ that parametrizes the internal volume scaling, the authors derive constraints on the scaling relation $m_{3/2} \\sim M_{\\text{KK}}^n$, finding that $n=1$ is viable only for one or two large extra dimensions—precisely those testable at micron scales—and implies gauge-mediated SUSY breaking with a light gravitino.
\n\n
本文探讨了超对称性和额外维度未被观测到的现象之间可能存在的关联，通过在源自II型弦紧致化的四维$\\mathcal{N}=1$超引力中将引力微子质量$m_{3/2}$与Kaluza-Klein（KK）能标$M_{\\text{KK}}$联系起来。作者引入一个各向异性指数$\\alpha$来参数化内空间体积的缩放行为，并由此导出Kähler势中的体积贡献，进而对缩放关系$m_{3/2} \\sim M_{\\text{KK}}^n$中的指数$n$施加约束。研究发现，仅当存在一维或二维大额外维度时（即当前微米尺度实验可探测的情形），$n=1$才成立，且对应规范介导的超对称破缺和轻引力微子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The absence of experimental evidence for both supersymmetry (SUSY) and large extra dimensions motivates a unified theoretical explanation. Rather than treating these null results independently, the paper posits that they may be correlated through UV-complete frameworks like string theory. Specifically, assuming the Gravitino Conjecture—which relates the gravitino mass to quantum gravity constraints—the authors aim to determine whether consistency conditions from Type II compactifications naturally suppress both SUSY-breaking scales and KK scales below current collider and short-distance gravity experiment sensitivities.
\n\n
超对称（SUSY）和大额外维度均缺乏实验证据，这促使作者寻求一个统一的理论解释。本文不将这些零结果视为独立现象，而是假设它们可能通过弦论等紫外完备框架相互关联。具体而言，在Gravitino Conjecture（将引力微子质量与量子引力约束联系起来）的假设下，作者旨在探究II型弦紧致化的一致性条件是否自然地将超对称破缺能标和KK能标压制在当前对撞机及短程引力实验的灵敏度以下。

Method: The authors consider four-dimensional $\\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravity arising from Type II string compactifications on anisotropic Calabi–Yau manifolds with a large $p$-cycle. They introduce an anisotropy exponent $\\alpha$ such that the total internal volume $\\mathcal{V} \\sim (\\rho_p)^\\alpha$, where $\\rho_p$ is the volume modulus of the large $p$-cycle. Using this parametrization, they compute the leading volume contributions to the Kähler potential $K = -2 \\ln \\mathcal{V}$, which governs the kinetic terms and scalar potential in supergravity. The gravitino mass is given by $m_{3/2} = e^{K/2} |W| / M_{\\text{Pl}}^2$, with $W$ the superpotential. Assuming $W$ is stabilized at order one in Planck units, they relate $m_{3/2}$ to $\\mathcal{V}$, and hence to the KK scale $M_{\\text{KK}} \\sim \\mathcal{V}^{-1/(2p)} M_s$, where $M_s$ is the string scale. This yields a power-law relation $m_{3/2} \\sim M_{\\text{KK}}^n$, with $n$ depending on $\\alpha$ and $p$. Consistency with phenomenology and the Gravitino Conjecture then constrains allowed $(\\alpha, p)$ pairs.

Result: The analysis shows that the scaling exponent $n$ in the relation $m_{3/2} \\sim M_{\\text{KK}}^n$ is determined by $n = \\frac{\\alpha}{\\alpha - 1}$. Imposing the Gravitino Conjecture and requiring compatibility with current experimental bounds (e.g., fifth-force experiments at micron scales), the authors find that the linear scaling $n=1$ is only possible if $\\alpha \\to \\infty$, which corresponds to effectively one or two large extra dimensions ($p=1$ or $p=2$). For these cases, $M_{\\text{KK}}$ lies in the meV–eV range, accessible to short-range gravity tests. Moreover, such a light KK scale implies a light gravitino ($m_{3/2} \\lesssim \\text{eV}$), favoring gauge-mediated supersymmetry breaking. In contrast, gravity-mediated scenarios require $M_{\\text{KK}} \\gg \\text{meV}$, placing them beyond current experimental reach.
\n\n
分析表明，关系式$m_{3/2} \\sim M_{\\text{KK}}^n$中的缩放指数$n$由$n = \\frac{\\alpha}{\\alpha - 1}$决定。在施加Gravitino Conjecture并要求与当前实验限制（如微米尺度的第五力实验）相容的前提下，作者发现线性缩放$n=1$仅在$\\alpha \\to \\infty$时成立，这对应于有效的一维或二维大额外维度（$p=1$或$p=2$）。在这些情形中，$M_{\\text{KK}}$位于meV–eV能区，可被短程引力实验探测。此外，如此低的KK能标意味着轻引力微子（$m_{3/2} \\lesssim \\text{eV}$），支持规范介导的超对称破缺。相比之下，引力介导的情形要求$M_{\\text{KK}} \\gg \\text{meV}$，超出当前实验可达范围。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that the joint absence of signals for supersymmetry and large extra dimensions may not be coincidental but instead arises from geometric and quantum-gravity constraints in string compactifications. Specifically, under the Gravitino Conjecture, only models with one or two large extra dimensions can simultaneously accommodate a light gravitino and a KK scale within experimental reach, and these correspond precisely to gauge-mediated SUSY breaking scenarios. This provides a predictive framework linking UV physics (string theory) to IR observables (gravitino mass, extra dimensions), suggesting that future improvements in short-distance gravity experiments could indirectly probe supersymmetry-breaking mechanisms.
\n\n
本文得出结论：超对称性和大额外维度信号的同时缺失可能并非巧合，而是源于弦紧致化中的几何与量子引力约束。具体而言，在Gravitino Conjecture下，仅有一维或二维大额外维度的模型能同时容纳轻引力微子和实验可及的KK能标，而这恰好对应规范介导的超对称破缺情形。这提供了一个将紫外物理（弦论）与红外可观测量（引力微子质量、额外维度）联系起来的预言性框架，表明未来短程引力实验的改进可能间接探测超对称破缺机制。

Abstract: We investigate whether the absence of experimental signals for supersymmetry and extra dimensions can be understood as a correlated phenomenon. Assuming the Gravitino Conjecture, we study the relation between the gravitino mass and the Kaluza-Klein scale in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravity from Type II compactifications with large extra dimensions. We parametrize the scaling of the full internal volume with respect to that of a large $p$-cycle through an anisotropy exponent $α$, and derive the corresponding volume contributions to the Kähler potential. This leads to constraints on the scaling exponent $n$, linking the gravitino mass to the KK scale, and on the effective number $αp$ of large dimensions. We find that the linear relation $n=1$ is compatible only with one or two large extra dimensions, precisely the cases that can still be probed at micron distances. In such scenarios, micron-sized extra dimensions imply a light gravitino and gauge-mediated supersymmetry breaking, whereas gravity mediation corresponds to compactification scales beyond current experimental reach.

### [6] [Probabilistic Microcausality in a Thermal Bath of Gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02729)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: We compute the operator-valued commutator $[φ(t, \vec x), φ(0)]$ of a massless scalar field coupled to gravity in transverse-traceless gauge at leading order in Newton's constant $G_N$. The commutator includes lightcone-supported delta-function derivatives that encode gravitational backreaction. On classical graviton states, this reproduces standard microcausality; on thermal graviton states at temperature $T$, it yields a Gaussian probability distribution for non-vanishing commutators with variance ${\rm Var}(\vec x^{\,2}) = \frac{16 G_N T t^3}{3}$, after subtracting a universal but UV log-divergent vacuum contribution.

我们计算了在横向无迹规范下、以牛顿常数 $G_N$ 的最低阶耦合引力的质量less标量场 $φ$ 的算符值对易子 $[φ(t, \vec x), φ(0)]$。该对易子包含位于光锥上的狄拉克δ函数导数项，这些项编码了引力反作用效应。在经典/相干引力子态上，这恢复了标准的微观因果性；而在温度为 $T$ 的热引力子态上，非零对易子的概率呈高斯分布，其方差为 ${\rm Var}(\vec x^{\,2}) = \\frac{16 G_N T t^3}{3}$，该结果是在减去一个普适但紫外对数发散的真空贡献后得到的。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to understand how quantum gravitational fluctuations affect causality in effective field theory. While classical general relativity enforces strict lightcone structure, quantum gravity may induce an intrinsic uncertainty in causal relations. By studying the commutator of a matter field coupled to dynamical gravity, the authors probe whether microcausality is preserved or modified by quantum metric fluctuations—especially in non-vacuum gravitational states like thermal ensembles.

本文旨在理解量子引力涨落如何影响有效场论中的因果性。尽管经典广义相对论强制严格的光锥结构，但量子引力可能在因果关系中引入内在不确定性。通过研究与动力学引力耦合的物质场的对易子，作者探究微观因果性是否被量子度规涨落所保持或修改——尤其是在热系综等非真空引力态中。

Method: The authors work in perturbative quantum gravity around Minkowski spacetime, using transverse-traceless (TT) gauge and expanding to leading order in $G_N$. They compute the operator-valued commutator $[φ(x), φ(y)]$ including gravitational loop corrections mediated by virtual gravitons. The result contains distributions supported on the lightcone, such as derivatives of Dirac deltas. To assess causal uncertainty, they evaluate the expectation value of the squared commutator on a thermal state of gravitons at temperature $T$, interpreting $\langle [φ, φ]^2 \rangle \neq 0$ as a measure of causal violation probability. A universal vacuum contribution—logarithmically UV divergent—is subtracted to isolate the thermal effect.
\n\n
作者在闵可夫斯基时空附近使用微扰量子引力，在横向无迹（TT）规范下展开至 $G_N$ 的最低阶。他们计算了算符值对易子 $[φ(x), φ(y)]$，包含由虚引力子介导的引力圈图修正。结果包含支撑在光锥上的分布，如狄拉克δ函数的导数。为评估因果不确定性，他们在温度为 $T$ 的引力子热态上计算对易子平方的期望值，将 $\langle [φ, φ]^2 \rangle \\neq 0$ 解释为因果破坏概率的度量。一个普适但紫外对数发散的真空贡献被减去，以分离出热效应。

Result: The key result is that in a thermal state of gravitons at temperature $T$, the probability that the commutator $[φ(t, \vec x), φ(0)]$ is non-zero follows a Gaussian distribution in $\vec x^{\,2}$, centered on the classical lightcone ($|\vec x| = t$) with a time-dependent variance: $$ {\rm Var}( \vec x^{\, 2}) = \frac{16 \, G_N T t^3}{3}\,. $$ This implies a growing “fuzziness” of the lightcone due to thermal metric fluctuations. The vacuum contribution is shown to be universally present but subleading at late times and UV log-divergent; physical regularization may arise from finite source size, suggesting vacuum lightcone spreading is source-dependent.

主要结果是：在温度为 $T$ 的引力子热态中，对易子 $[φ(t, \vec x), φ(0)]$ 非零的概率在 $\vec x^{\,2}$ 上呈高斯分布，中心位于经典光锥（$|\\vec x| = t$）处，其时间依赖的方差为：$$ {\\rm Var}( \\vec x^{\, 2}) = \\frac{16 \\, G_N T t^3}{3}\\,. $$ 这表明由于热度规涨落，光锥出现随时间增长的“模糊性”。真空贡献被证明是普适存在的，但在晚期时间次主导且紫外对数发散；物理上的正规化可能来自有限大小的源，暗示真空中的光锥弥散依赖于源。

Conclusion: Quantum gravitational fluctuations induce an intrinsic uncertainty in spacetime causal structure. While classical or coherent gravitational backgrounds preserve microcausality via lightcone “bending,” generic quantum states—such as thermal ensembles—lead to a probabilistic smearing of the lightcone. The observed $t^3$ growth of variance suggests a cumulative effect of stochastic metric fluctuations over time. The UV sensitivity of the vacuum contribution highlights the need for a UV-complete theory or physical regulator (e.g., finite source size) to define causal structure unambiguously in quantum gravity.

量子引力涨落在时空因果结构中引入了内在不确定性。虽然经典或相干引力背景通过光锥“弯曲”保持微观因果性，但一般的量子态（如热系综）会导致光锥的概率性弥散。观测到的方差 $t^3$ 增长表明度规随机涨落具有时间累积效应。真空贡献的紫外敏感性突显了需要一个紫外完备理论或物理正规化方案（如有限源尺寸）才能在量子引力中明确地定义因果结构。

Abstract: We compute the (operator-valued) commutator of a massless scalar field $φ$ coupled to gravity. We work in perturbations around Minkowski space, in transverse-traceless gauge at the lowest order in $G_N$. The commutator is composed of different operators, including terms with Dirac delta derivatives supported on the lightcone. These are responsible for ``bending" the Minkowski lightcone when evaluated on a classical/coherent state of gravitons, which allows to recover standard microcausality in the fixed-background limit. On more general gravitational states, metric fluctuations induce an uncertainty in the causal structure. We compute this effect on a thermal state of gravitons at temperature $T$ by evaluating the probability that $[φ(t, \vec x), φ(0)] \neq 0$. We find that the probability is Gaussian in $\vec x^{\, 2}$, centered on the lightcone and with time-growing variance $$ {\rm Var}( \vec x^{\, 2}) = \frac{16 \, G_N T t^3}{3}\, .$$ This result is obtained by subtracting a universal vacuum contribution, which is log-divergent in the UV and subleading in the large-time limit. As a source of finite size can effectively serve as a regulator in this case, the lightcone spread in the vacuum appears to be source-dependent.

Abstract: In this work we discuss in detail a global analytic expansion scheme for the solutions of the `t~Hooft-Polyakov monopole profile equations for arbitrary $λ/e^2>0$ based on the findings presented in a recent resurgence-oriented letter arXiv:2602.14620 [hep-th], which the present study significantly expands upon. ### [8] [Kinematical correlations via $κ$-Poincaré coproducts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03004)
*Mohammad Ali Gorji,Babak Vakili*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates how the choice of momentum-space coordinates in $κ$-Minkowski spacetime—specifically the classical versus bicrossproduct bases—affects the physical interpretation of multi-particle states due to nontrivial Hopf-algebraic momentum composition. 本文研究了在$κ$-Minkowski时空下，由于Hopf代数动量合成律所导致的运动学后果，特别关注经典基与bicrossproduct基之间动量空间坐标选择对多粒子态物理解释的影响。在经典基中，辅助平面波标签$p_μ$到物理平移生成元本征值$P_μ$的映射在高动量区域可能变为多值，当施加总动量为零约束时，会导致依赖分支的$κ$-变形背对背动量关联。量$P_+ = κ e^{p_0/κ}$用于区分不同分支。若将$P_μ$视为直接物理动量，则表现为变形关联；若赋予$p_μ$操作意义，则同一约束态可解释为不同辅助分支的叠加。标准最小长度模型中不存在类似的光滑局部双实分支反演结构。

  <summary>Details</summary>

They identify regions where this map fails to be globally invertible, leading to multiple real preimages $p_μ$ for a single $P_μ$. Using the branch-sensitive quantity $P_+ = P_0 + P_4 = κ e^{p_0/κ}$, they resolve ambiguities in two-particle states under the constraint $P_μ^{(1)} + P_μ^{(2)} = 0$. They contrast this with standard nonrelativistic minimal-length models by checking for analogous multibranch inversion properties.

作者分析了$κ$-Poincaré对称性的Hopf代数结构，重点关注不同基底下平移生成元的余积（coproduct）。他们研究经典基中从辅助变量$p_μ$（有序平面波标签）到物理动量$P_μ$（平移生成元本征值）的映射，并识别出该映射非全局可逆的区域，即单个$P_μ$对应多个实数预像$p_μ$。利用分支敏感量$P_+ = P_0 + P_4 = κ e^{p_0/κ}$，他们在总动量约束$P_μ^{(1)} + P_μ^{(2)} = 0$下解析两粒子态的分支模糊性。并通过检验是否存在类似的多分支反演性质，将此结构与标准非相对论最小长度模型进行对比。

Result: In the classical basis of $κ$-Minkowski spacetime, the relation between auxiliary momenta $p_μ$ and physical momenta $P_μ$ becomes non-injective at high energies, admitting multiple real solutions for $p_μ$ given $P_μ$. Imposing total-momentum conservation in two-particle states leads to branch-dependent $κ$-deformed back-to-back correlations. The quantity $P_+ = κ e^{p_0/κ}$ distinguishes between branches and enters the coproduct structure. If $P_μ$ are physical, the state exhibits deformed correlations; if $p_μ$ are physical, the state is a superposition over branches. In contrast, standard self-adjoint minimal-length models lack such smooth local two-real-branch inversions within their physical domains.

在$κ$-Minkowski时空的经典基中，辅助动量$p_μ$与物理动量$P_μ$之间的关系在高能区变为非单射，即给定$P_μ$存在多个实数解$p_μ$。在两粒子态中施加总动量守恒会导致依赖分支的$κ$-变形背对背关联。量$P_+ = κ e^{p_0/κ}$用于区分不同分支，并进入余积结构。若$P_μ$为物理量，则态呈现变形关联；若$p_μ$为物理量，则态是不同分支的叠加。相比之下，标准自伴最小长度模型在其物理定义域内不存在此类光滑局部双实分支反演。

Conclusion: The physical interpretation of multi-particle states in $κ$-Minkowski spacetime is inherently tied to the choice of momentum-space coordinates and the operational meaning assigned to momenta. The classical basis introduces branch ambiguities absent in the bicrossproduct basis, leading to novel $κ$-deformed correlation patterns or quantum superpositions over auxiliary branches, depending on interpretation. This structure has no counterpart in conventional minimal-length models, highlighting a distinctive feature of Hopf-algebraic quantum spacetimes with curved momentum space.

$κ$-Minkowski时空中多粒子态的物理解释本质上依赖于动量空间坐标的选取以及对动量赋予的操作意义。经典基引入了bicrossproduct基中不存在的分支模糊性，根据解释方式不同，导致新颖的$κ$-变形关联模式或辅助分支上的量子叠加。这种结构在传统最小长度模型中并无对应，凸显了具有弯曲动量空间的Hopf代数量子时空的独特性。

Abstract: We study a kinematical consequence of the Hopf-algebraic momentum composition law in $κ$-Minkowski spacetime. The same curved momentum space can be described in different coordinates. In the bicrossproduct basis the ordered-plane-wave labels are the translation-generator eigenvalues, so the relevant map is one-to-one. In the classical basis, instead, the translation eigenvalues $P_μ$ are nonlinearly related to the ordered-plane-wave labels $p_μ$. This relation can fail to be globally one-to-one in a high-momentum region. When a given classical-basis four-momentum admits more than one real auxiliary preimage, the branch-sensitive quantity $P_+\equiv P_0+P_4=κe^{p_0/κ}$ enters the coproduct and resolves the branches in two-particle states. Imposing the vanishing total-momentum constraint therefore gives branch-dependent $κ$-deformed back-to-back momentum correlations. In a single-branch regime this is just a deformed correlated product, while in a multibranch regime a state specified only by $P_μ$ can be expanded into distinct auxiliary branches. If $P_μ$ are taken as the directly meaningful momenta, the physical content is the resulting deformed correlation pattern. If the auxiliary variables $p_μ$ are assigned operational meaning, the same constrained state can be interpreted as a superposition over different auxiliary branches. We also compare this structure with standard regular self-adjoint nonrelativistic minimal-length models and find no analogous smooth local two-real-branch inversion on their physical domains.

For CV and CV2.0, they evaluate the maximal spatial volume and spacetime four-volume within the WDW patch; for CA, they compute the gravitational action (including boundary, joint, and counterterms) over the same region. Late-time limits (for static patch) and large spacelike boundary coordinate limits (for dS/CFT) are taken to extract asymptotic complexity growth rates.

作者构建了Schwarzschild-de Sitter黑洞的Wheeler-DeWitt (WDW) patch，并基于三种proposal计算holographic complexity：Complexity equals Volume (CV)、Complexity equals Spacetime Volume (CV2.0) 和 Complexity equals Action (CA)。他们分别分析两种全息设定：(i) static patch holography，其中边界置于stretched cosmological horizon处；(ii) dS/CFT correspondence，其对偶CFT位于$\\mathcal{I}^+$与$\\mathcal{I}^-$。对于CV和CV2.0，他们在WDW patch内计算最大空间体积与四维spacetime volume；对于CA，则计算同一区域上的引力作用量（包括boundary项、joint项及counterterms）。通过对static patch取late-time极限、对dS/CFT取大spacelike boundary coordinate极限，提取asymptotic complexity增长率。

Crucially, the complexity growth rates computed in the static patch and dS/CFT frameworks are numerically identical for each conjecture, suggesting scheme independence.

在static patch与dS/CFT holography下，CV与CV2.0猜想均预言holographic complexity在late time（或大boundary坐标）呈线性增长：$\\frac{d\\mathcal{C}_{\\text{V}}}{dt} \\propto M$ 与 $\\frac{d\\mathcal{C}_{\\text{V2.0}}}{dt} \\propto M$，其中$M$为黑洞质量。相比之下，CA猜想在两种方案中均给出消失的增长率（$\\frac{d\\mathcal{C}_{\\text{A}}}{dt} = 0$），这是由于受限于horizon之间的紧致WDW patch内(regularized)引力作用量有限所致。关键的是，每种猜想下static patch与dS/CFT框架计算出的complexity增长率数值完全相同，表明其方案无关性。

Abstract: We investigate holographic complexity within the Schwarzschild-de Sitter (SdS) black hole spacetime. Two distinct de Sitter holography prescriptions are examined: the static patch scheme restricted to the stretched horizon and the de Sitter/Conformal Field Theory (dS/CFT) correspondence scheme defined at asymptotic future and past infinities. We evaluate the Complexity equals Volume (CV) conjecture and extend the analysis to codimension-zero proposals, specifically Complexity equals Spacetime Volume (CV2.0) and Complexity equals Action (CA), through the Wheeler-DeWitt (WDW) patch we construct. Conversely, regarding the CA conjecture, the holographic complexity growth rates for both the static patch and the dS/CFT correspondence vanish. This behavior is attributed to the finiteness of the (regularized) action within the restricted WDW region. Furthermore, it is demonstrated that the complexity growth rate of the static patch scheme is identical to that in the dS/CFT scheme. They analyze how these symmetries act on charged operators and constrain their correlation functions. The factorization of correlators into a Schwinger phase and a reduced function is derived from the cocycle structure of magnetic translations. The spectral representation of two-point functions is constructed by decomposing them into irreducible representations of the magnetic translation algebra, revealing gauge choices as basis selections.

作者采用一种与模型无关的方法，基于磁平移群的射影表示（projective representation）及磁旋转对称性的群论分析。他们研究这些对称性如何作用于带电算符并约束其关联函数。关联函数分解为Schwinger相位与约化函数的形式，源于磁平移的上循环（cocycle）结构。两点函数的谱表示通过将其分解为磁平移代数的不可约表示而构建，揭示了规范选择本质上是基的选择。

Result: The key results are: (1) Charged operator correlation functions must contain the Schwinger phase $e^{i q \Phi(x,y)}$, where $\Phi(x,y)$ is the line integral of the vector potential, ensuring gauge covariance; (2) These correlators factorize as $\langle \mathcal{O}(x) \mathcal{O}^\dagger(y) \rangle = e^{i q \Phi(x,y)} F(x - y)$, with $F$ depending only on relative coordinates; (3) The two-point function admits a spectral representation in terms of eigenstates of the magnetic translation algebra, with Landau and symmetric gauges corresponding to distinct bases in this representation space.

主要结果包括：(1) 带电算符的关联函数必须包含Schwinger相位 $e^{i q \Phi(x,y)}$，其中 $\Phi(x,y)$ 是矢势的线积分，以保证规范协变性；(2) 这些关联函数可分解为 $\langle \mathcal{O}(x) \mathcal{O}^\dagger(y) \rangle = e^{i q \Phi(x,y)} F(x - y)$，其中 $F$ 仅依赖于相对坐标；(3) 两点函数具有基于磁平移代数本征态的谱表示，Landau规范与对称规范对应于该表示空间中的不同基。

Abstract: Quantum field theories in the presence of a static and uniform external magnetic field possess two characteristic spatial symmetries: magnetic translations and magnetic rotation. We investigate general consequences of these symmetries on correlation functions from a model-independent perspective, without relying on specific models or perturbative expansions. Our results provide a unified symmetry-based framework for quantum field theories in external magnetic fields.

Abstract: We recently constructed a two-field Degenerate Higher-Order Scalar-Tensor (DHOST) bouncing model which is fully viable at the linear level [1]. This model is completely free of Belinski-Khalatnikov-Lifshitz (BKL) instability, ghost instability, gradient instability and superluminality. It also predicts the scalar spectral index and tensor-toscalar ratio consistent with observations. The aim of this paper is to extend the viability of the model to the non-linear level. To this end, we first refine the original model such that its prediction on the (local) non-Gaussianity parameter fNL agrees with observations, leaving the viability of the model at the linear level intact. We furthermore demonstrate that the strong-coupling scale is well above the characteristic background energy scale all the time. Our model indeed exemplifies the fully viable two-field DHOST bouncing model, in the sense that it is weakly-coupled, stable and non-superluminal as well as consistent with observations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors employ a brane engineering setup: D4-branes suspended between NS5-branes arranged on a circle yield the $A_{K-1}$ circular quiver gauge theory. They adopt a single-pole ansatz for the Higgs field profile near the boundary, which reduces the differential BPS equations to a rigid algebraic problem involving eigenvalues and residues. This algebraic system is then analyzed for its solution space structure, revealing a winding number associated with the cyclic nature of the quiver. Two explicit cases are solved in closed form. Brane duality (relating different D-brane configurations under S-duality) is used to conjecture the S-dual of the Neumann boundary condition.

Result: The key results are: (1) the reduction of $\tfrac12$-BPS boundary condition equations to an algebraic system via a single-pole ansatz; (2) the discovery of a winding phenomenon in the solution space, which has no counterpart in linear quiver theories due to the periodicity of the circular quiver; (3) explicit closed-form solutions for two representative cases; and (4) a proposal—supported by brane duality—that the maximal-winding solution corresponds to the S-dual of the pure Neumann boundary condition.

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that circular quiver gauge theories exhibit richer boundary dynamics than their linear counterparts, manifested through a topological winding feature in $\tfrac12$-BPS boundary conditions. Abstract: We study a string-motivated class of $\tfrac12$-BPS boundary conditions for 4d $\mathcal N=2$ $A_{K-1}$ circular quiver gauge theories, engineered by D4-branes suspended between NS5-branes on a circle. *Pavel Galashin*

TL;DR: This paper establishes that BCFW cells provide a complete triangulation of the $m=4$ amplituhedron at all loop orders, leveraging the origami-amplituhedron correspondence. It also introduces two $L$-punctured extensions of the positive Grassmannian and connects them through T-duality.

  <summary>Details</summary>

主要结果包括：(1) 严格证明了BCFW胞腔在动量空间和动量扭量空间中于所有圈图阶数下对$m=4$振幅hedron构成三角剖分；(2) 定义了两种$L$-穿孔正Grassmannian，分别记为动量空间中的$G_{+}(k,n;L)$和动量扭量空间中的$\\widetilde{G}_{+}(k,n;L)$；(3) 证明了这两个空间是T对偶的，即通过一个保持正性但交换其边界结构的变换相互关联。

Conclusion: This work completes the geometric interpretation of BCFW recursion within the amplituhedron framework by proving its validity to all loop orders for $m=4$. Abstract: Building on the recently discovered origami-amplituhedron correspondence, we prove that the BCFW (Britto-Cachazo-Feng-Witten) cells triangulate the $m=4$ amplituhedron in full generality at all loop orders, both in momentum and momentum-twistor space. Along the way, we develop two natural "$L$-punctured" extensions of the positive Grassmannian and relate them via T-duality.

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates renormalization in 4D quantum Yang-Mills theory using cutoff regularization in coordinate space via the background field method, introducing two types of quasi-local probabilistic averaging (strong and weak deformation). 本文研究了四维量子Yang-Mills理论在坐标空间中通过背景场方法引入截断正规化下的重整化问题，提出了两种准局域概率平均方案（强形变与弱形变）。文章分析了一圈和二圈修正中的奇异性部分，将其与维度正规化情形进行详细比较，并考察了新反项顶点的局域性及其对正规化参数的依赖。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand non-perturbative and regularization-scheme-dependent aspects of 4D Yang-Mills theory, particularly how gauge symmetry and locality are preserved under cutoff-based regularization. Method: The authors employ the background field method to construct the generating functional for quantum fluctuations around a classical background. Regularization is implemented via quasi-local probabilistic averaging: strong deformation averages fluctuation fields directly, while weak deformation generalizes this covariantly under background gauge transformations. They compute the divergent (singular) parts of the first two quantum corrections (one- and two-loop) and perform renormalization by introducing counter-vertices. The analysis includes verifying consistency of the renormalized action and equations of motion, and studying the locality and regulator dependence ($\\Lambda$ or analogous parameter) of new counterterms.

Conclusion: The proposed quasi-local cutoff regularization within the background field formalism provides a viable alternative to dimensional regularization for 4D Yang-Mills theory, offering clearer insight into locality and gauge structure of counterterms. Abstract: The paper discusses renormalization aspects of the quantum four-dimensional Yang-Mills theory with a cutoff regularization in the coordinate representation. The background field method is used to formulate a generating functional, and the regularization is introduced through quasi-local probabilistic averaging. Two main types of regularization are proposed: strong deformation, which consists in averaging fluctuation fields, and weak deformation, which is a covariant generalization of the first case with respect to gauge transformations of the background field. We study singular contributions for the first two quantum corrections in this paper and compare them in detail with the case of dimensional regularization. The consistency of the action and the equation of motion after introducing the regularization and making a renormalization procedure is analyzed. New counter-vertices are studied, in particular their locality properties and dependence on the regularization parameter.

</details>

*Tetsuyuki Muramatsu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates whether emergent spacetime in the Lorentzian IIB matrix model can be algebraically constrained by off-shell supersymmetry closure, without assuming classical solutions. Imposing spatial isotropy yields a $κ$-Minkowski-like algebra identifying time, but nontrivial spatial geometry requires an infinite-dimensional or unbounded-operator limit, leading to kinematic compactification of internal directions.
\n\n
本文研究Lorentzian IIB矩阵模型中的涌现时空能否通过离壳超对称闭合的代数一致性条件加以约束，而不依赖经典解的假设。结果表明，一致性条件强制宏观与内禀方向之间呈现块对角分离，消除内禀非阿贝尔通量，并通过四维epsilon张量唯一固定变换结构。施加空间各向同性后得到类似$κ$-Minkowski的代数并识别出时间方向，但非平凡空间几何需取$N\to\infty$或无界算符极限，从而在运动学上导致内禀方向的有效紧致化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how spacetime geometry emerges from the fundamental matrix degrees of freedom in the Lorentzian IIB matrix model without relying on dynamical equations or classical background solutions. Instead, the authors seek purely algebraic constraints—specifically, off-shell supersymmetry closure—as a guiding principle for viable effective structures.

Method: The authors consider a CPT-even low-order effective-action ansatz in Minkowski signature and impose restricted off-shell supersymmetry closure on anisotropic background fields without using their equations of motion. They analyze the resulting Ward identities order by order: at zeroth order, they constrain the scalar potential; at second order, they examine transformation coefficients and use Clifford-algebra identities to restrict internal fluxes. Spatial isotropy is then imposed on the macroscopic sector, and finite-dimensional Hermitian representations are analyzed to assess geometric nontriviality.

作者采用Minkowski符号下CPT偶性的低阶有效作用量ansatz，在不使用运动方程的前提下，对各向异性背景场施加受限的离壳超对称闭合条件。他们逐阶分析所得Ward恒等式：零阶约束标量势；二阶考察变换系数，并利用Clifford代数恒等式限制内禀通量。随后对宏观部分施加空间各向同性，并分析有限维Hermitian表示以判断几何是否非平凡。

Result: Key results include: (1) the scalar ansatz must be constant due to the zeroth-order Ward identity; (2) second-order closure enforces a block-diagonal separation between macroscopic and internal directions; (3) internal non-Abelian flux vanishes algebraically; (4) in 4D, closure obstruction is absorbed via a Lorentz-type rotation with coefficient fixed (up to a function) by the epsilon tensor $\\epsilon_{\\mu\\nu\\rho\\sigma}$; (5) spatial isotropy selects a $κ$-Minkowski-like algebra and identifies time; (6) nontrivial spatial geometry requires $N\\to\\infty$ or unbounded operators; (7) this yields kinematic compactification: macroscopic space expands while internal sector remains static.

主要结果包括：(1) 零阶Ward恒等式迫使标量ansatz为常数；(2) 二阶闭合强制宏观与内禀方向呈块对角分离；(3) 内禀非阿贝尔通量代数性地消失；(4) 在四维中，闭合障碍可通过Lorentz型旋转吸收，其系数由epsilon张量$\\epsilon_{\\mu\\nu\\rho\\sigma}$（至多一个整体函数）唯一确定；(5) 空间各向同性选出类似$κ$-Minkowski的代数并识别出时间方向；(6) 非平凡空间几何需取$N\\to\\infty$或无界算符极限；(7) 这导致运动学紧致化：宏观空间膨胀而内禀部分保持静态。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that algebraic consistency—specifically off-shell supersymmetry closure—can strongly constrain emergent spacetime structures in the Lorentzian IIB matrix model, even without dynamical input. The resulting framework naturally separates observable (macroscopic) and hidden (internal) sectors, eliminates internal dynamics algebraically, and provides a kinematic origin for effective dimensional reduction through representation-theoretic constraints.
\n\n
本文表明，代数一致性——特别是离壳超对称闭合——即使在没有动力学输入的情况下，也能强力约束Lorentzian IIB矩阵模型中的涌现时空结构。所得框架自然分离可观测（宏观）与隐藏（内禀）部分，代数性地消除内禀动力学，并通过表示论约束为有效维度约化提供运动学起源。

Abstract: The Lorentzian IIB matrix model provides a non-perturbative framework for studying emergent spacetime from microscopic matrix degrees of freedom. In this paper we ask whether such emergent structures can be constrained by algebraic consistency, rather than by specifying a classical or dynamical solution. We analyze a CPT-even low-order effective-action ansatz in Minkowski signature and impose restricted off-shell supersymmetry closure on anisotropic background fields, without imposing their equations of motion. The zeroth-order Ward identity forces the scalar ansatz to be constant. Within the order-two truncation, closure constrains the effective transformation coefficients and selects a block-diagonal separation between macroscopic and internal directions. Clifford-algebra identities then require the internal non-Abelian flux to vanish, giving an algebraic decoupling of the internal sector. In the four-dimensional sector, the closure obstruction can be absorbed into a Lorentz-type rotation when the macroscopic matrices form a non-degenerate coordinate sector. Within a linear absorption ansatz, the coefficient structure is fixed, up to an overall function, by the four-dimensional epsilon tensor. Imposing macroscopic spatial isotropy selects a $κ$-Minkowski-like algebra and identifies the macroscopic time direction. Finite-dimensional Hermitian representations make this spatial sector trivial, so a nontrivial realization requires an $N\to\infty$ or unbounded-operator limit. 作者通过计算引力路径积分来构建空间子区域的纯态，其中包含该子区域的时空区域被固定（“冻结”），而对该区域外部的场构型和几何进行求和。在半经典极限下，他们为该纯态的双划分提出了一种全息纠缠熵公式，该公式涉及极值曲面，并受与冻结区域相关的修正同调约束限制。

Result: The proposed holographic entropy prescription satisfies strong subadditivity, complementarity, and entanglement wedge nesting. 所提出的全息熵处方满足强次可加性、互补性以及纠缠楔嵌套。它在特定极限下复现了已知结果，如Ryu–Takayanagi公式、岛屿（island）公式以及边缘模（edge-mode）贡献。该构造暗示了一种依赖于观测者的纠缠楔，其由冻结子区域的选择所标记。

Abstract: It is proposed that spatial subregions in quantum gravity can be assigned pure states, rather than mixed reduced density matrices. In the semiclassical regime, we further propose a holographic prescription for the entanglement entropy of bipartitions of this state, with a frozen-region analogue of the homology constraint. The prescription satisfies nontrivial self-consistency conditions, including strong subadditivity, complementarity, and entanglement wedge nesting, and reproduces several known entropy formulas in holography and gravity as special cases. The construction suggests an observer-dependent entanglement wedge labeled by the frozen subregion.

Result: The calculation yields magnetic moments for all Standard Model fermions and the $W^\pm$ boson with respect to each gauge boson’s magnetic field. 计算得到了所有标准模型费米子及 $W^\pm$ 玻色子相对于各规范玻色子磁場的磁矩。引入与运动相关的赝矢量磁場后，在 $W^\pm$ 相互作用中，左、右手费米子的耦合结构呈现不对称性，从而为带电弱过程中宇称破坏提供了几何诠释。

Conclusion: The complex quaternion formulation provides a unified algebraic setting where magnetic moments arise naturally for both fermions and charged gauge bosons across all interactions. The observed parity asymmetry in weak interactions may stem from the interplay between charged currents and neutral pseudovector magnetic fields inherent in this geometric framework.

复四元数表述提供了一个统一的代数框架，在其中磁矩可自然地出现在所有相互作用中的费米子与带电规范玻色子上。弱相互作用中观测到的宇称不对称性可能源于该几何框架内带电流与中性赝矢量磁場之间的内在耦合。

Abstract: As has been shown by multiple authors in recent decades, it is possible to reformulate various portions of the standard model over the ring of complex quaternions. In this paper, we utilize a complex quaternion spin representation of the spacetime algebra to derive the magnetic moments of standard model fermions and the $W^\pm$ boson. The moments calculated are not limited to those with the photon. We account for coupling to the magnetic fields of each standard model gauge boson. We naively assume that fermions coupled to weak isospin have magnetic moments with the charged bosons. Upon assuming these charged moments exist, we realize that they are to be influenced by the neutral, pseudovector-valued magnetic fields that are observed when a charged particle is moving. Visualizing the derived moments of fermions and charged bosons together, we find a possible explanation for the parity asymmetry observed in charged weak interactions.

*Arturo de Giorgi,James Ingoldby,Valentin V. Khoze,Jessica Turner*

来自最小暗区 $\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$ 规范理论的亚稳宇宙弦可解释PTA观测到的纳赫兹引力波背景；热效应通过热诱导磁单极子成核主导弦衰变，将可行参数空间推向更低的暗精细结构常数 $α'$ 和更高的磁单极子与弦张力比 $κ$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The recent detection of a stochastic gravitational wave background in the nanohertz band by Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) collaborations motivates concrete particle physics models that can produce such a signal. Metastable cosmic strings—topological defects that decay slowly enough to generate observable gravitational waves—are promising candidates. The paper aims to explore this scenario within a minimal dark-sector gauge theory with a single symmetry-breaking scale, where previous analyses relied on zero-temperature decay rates, potentially missing crucial thermal effects during the early universe phase transition.

脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）合作组近期在纳赫兹波段探测到随机引力波背景，这激发了对能够产生此类信号的具体粒子物理模型的研究。亚稳宇宙弦——衰变足够缓慢以产生可观测引力波的拓扑缺陷——是极具前景的候选者。本文旨在一个具有单一对称性破缺能标的最小暗区规范理论中探讨此情景，而此前分析依赖于零温衰变率，可能忽略了早期宇宙相变期间关键的热效应。

Method: The authors consider a dark-sector gauge theory with gauge group $\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$ spontaneously broken to $\mathrm{U}(1)$ by a complex Higgs doublet at a single scale. They compute the finite-temperature one-loop effective potential with daisy resummation to account for thermal corrections. Using this, they determine the nucleation temperature $T_{\rm nuc}$ at which thermally induced monopole-antimonopole pair production on the string worldsheet becomes the dominant decay channel. They then map out the string formation temperature across the $(\sin^2θ_w, \sqrtβ)$ parameter space, where $θ_w$ is the dark weak mixing angle and $β = M_Φ^2/M_Z^2$. Gravitational wave spectra from decaying string networks are computed under the assumption of a first-order phase transition, and compared with PTA observations.

作者考虑一个暗区规范理论，其规范群 $\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)$ 通过一个复标量希格斯二重态在单一能标下自发破缺至 $\mathrm{U}(1)$。他们计算包含 Daisy 重求和的一圈有限温度有效势以计入热修正。基于此，他们确定成核温度 $T_{\rm nuc}$，在此温度下弦世界面上的热诱导磁单极子–反磁单极子对产生成为主导衰变道。随后，他们在 $(\sin^2θ_w, \sqrtβ)$ 参数空间中映射出弦形成温度，其中 $θ_w$ 是暗区弱混合角，$β = M_Φ^2/M_Z^2$。在假设一级相变的前提下，计算衰变弦网络产生的引力波谱，并与PTA观测结果进行比较。

Result: Thermal effects drastically alter the decay dynamics: instead of zero-temperature quantum tunneling (monopole nucleation), thermally assisted nucleation dominates after the phase transition. This region corresponds to smaller values of the dark fine-structure constant $α' = g'^2/(4π)$ and larger values of the monopole-to-string-tension ratio $κ = M_{\rm mono}/(πμ)$, where $μ$ is the string tension. 本文表明，热效应对于准确建模早期宇宙中亚稳宇宙弦的衰变至关重要。忽略热效应会导致与PTA观测兼容的参数空间预测错误。在所研究的最小暗区模型中，与纳赫兹引力波信号的一致性要求热成核占主导地位，这选定了暗弱混合角和希格斯与规范玻色子质量比的特定范围，从而为超出标准模型的新物理提供了可检验的预言。

Abstract: Metastable cosmic strings provide a cosmological interpretation of the nanohertz stochastic gravitational wave background reported by Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) experiments. We revisit this scenario in a minimal dark-sector gauge theory, in which a complex Higgs doublet breaks $\mathrm{SU}(2)\times\mathrm{U}(1)\to\mathrm{U}(1)$ at a single symmetry-breaking scale. This one-scale setup predicts metastable $Z$-strings whose endpoints are monopole-like defects, and whose zero-temperature decay rate is controlled by the gauge couplings and mass ratios. We show that, once the string-forming transition occurs in a thermal plasma, the dominant decay channel is not the zero-temperature monopole nucleation but thermally induced nucleation on the string worldsheet. We determine the nucleation temperature, $T_{\rm nuc}$, from the one-loop finite-temperature effective potential with daisy resummation, and use it to compute the string formation temperature throughout the model parameter space. Requiring both a viable first-order transition and a PTA-compatible gravitational wave signal selects a narrow region in the model parameter space, in the $(\sin^2θ_w,\sqrtβ)$ plane, where $θ_w$ is the dark-sector weak mixing angle and $β\equiv M_Φ^2/M_{Z}^2$ is the squared Higgs-to-$Z$ mass ratio. Thermal effects modify the zero-temperature picture significantly, shifting the PTA-compatible region towards lower values of the dark fine-structure constant $α'$ and larger values of the monopole-to-string-tension ratio $κ$.

### [23] [Stochastic Dynamics of Heavy Quarks in Strongly Coupled Plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02693)
*Krishna Rajagopal,Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld,Urs Achim Wiedemann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper studies the stochastic motion of heavy quarks in a strongly coupled $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM plasma using the full Kolmogorov equation, valid across relativistic and non-relativistic regimes. By exploiting the heavy quark limit, the momentum-space evolution is recast as a Hamilton-Jacobi problem solvable via first-order ODEs. Comparing with Fokker-Planck dynamics, the authors show that equilibration at large momentum is significantly slower due to enhanced probability of rare, small-momentum-loss events—similar to jet quenching effects—highlighting limitations of Gaussian approximations.

本文利用完整的Kolmogorov方程研究了重夸克在强耦合$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM等离子体中的随机动力学，该方法适用于从极端相对论到非相对论的全速度范围。通过重夸克极限，动量空间分布函数的演化被重构为Hamilton-Jacobi问题，并可通过一阶常微分方程求解。与Fokker-Planck动力学对比表明，在大动量区域，由于罕见的小动量损失事件（类似于jet quenching现象）的概率显著更高，热化过程明显延迟，揭示了高斯近似的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard approaches to heavy quark energy loss in quark-gluon plasma often rely on the Fokker-Planck equation, which assumes Gaussian fluctuations and is typically restricted to the non-relativistic regime. However, in realistic high-energy collisions, heavy quarks can be ultra-relativistic, and non-Gaussian fluctuations become important. The authors aim to develop a more systematic and relativistically consistent description of kinetic equilibration using the exact Kolmogorov equation derived from holographic methods in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theory, thereby capturing both drag and non-Gaussian noise without ad hoc assumptions.

标准处理夸克-胶子等离子体中重夸克能量损失的方法通常依赖于Fokker-Planck方程，该方程假设高斯涨落且通常局限于非相对论区域。然而，在真实高能碰撞中，重夸克可能处于极端相对论状态，此时非高斯涨落变得重要。作者旨在利用从$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM理论全息方法导出的精确Kolmogorov方程，构建一个更系统且相对论自洽的动力学热化描述，从而无需人为假设即可同时捕捉拖曳力和非高斯噪声效应。

Method: The authors employ the heavy quark limit in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM at finite temperature, where the dual gravitational description provides an exact Langevin-like process with non-Gaussian noise. They formulate the time evolution of the momentum distribution $f(\vec{p}, t)$ via the Kolmogorov forward equation. In the heavy quark mass limit ($M \to \infty$), they perform a WKB-like ansatz $f \sim e^{-M S(\vec{p}, t)}$, reducing the Kolmogorov PDE to a Hamilton-Jacobi equation: $\partial_t S + H(\nabla_p S, \vec{p}) = 0$, where the Hamiltonian $H$ encodes the stochastic dynamics. This yields characteristic equations—first-order ODEs—that are numerically integrated for spherically symmetric initial conditions with steeply falling tails (e.g., exponential or power-law). Results are contrasted with a Fokker-Planck model sharing the same drag coefficient but Gaussian noise tuned to reproduce equilibrium.
\n\n
作者在有限温度的$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM理论中采用重夸克极限，其对偶引力描述提供了具有非高斯噪声的精确Langevin型过程。他们通过Kolmogorov前向方程描述动量分布函数$f(\vec{p}, t)$的时间演化。在重夸克质量极限（$M \to \infty$）下，采用WKB型试探解$f \sim e^{-M S(\vec{p}, t)}$，将Kolmogorov偏微分方程约化为Hamilton-Jacobi方程：$\partial_t S + H(\nabla_p S, \vec{p}) = 0$，其中哈密顿量$H$编码了随机动力学。由此得到特征线方程——一组一阶常微分方程——并对具有陡峭衰减尾部（如指数或幂律）的球对称初始条件进行数值积分。结果与具有相同拖曳系数但噪声为高斯型（人为调节以保证热化）的Fokker-Planck模型进行对比。

Result: The Kolmogorov-based evolution shows that while low-momentum dynamics closely resemble those of the Fokker-Planck approximation (due to dominance of typical drag), high-momentum equilibration is markedly slower. This delay arises because the exact stochastic process assigns higher probability to trajectories with anomalously small momentum loss per unit time—events that are exponentially suppressed in the Gaussian Fokker-Planck treatment. Quantitatively, the tail of the distribution decays more slowly in the Kolmogorov case, implying that heavy quarks retain high momentum longer than predicted by standard models. This effect mirrors mechanisms in jet quenching where rare fluctuations dominate energy loss statistics.
\n\n
基于Kolmogorov方程的演化表明：尽管低动量区域的动力学与Fokker-Planck近似高度相似（因典型拖曳主导），但在高动量区域热化显著变慢。这种延迟源于精确随机过程赋予单位时间内异常小动量损失轨迹更高的概率——这类事件在高斯型Fokker-Planck处理中呈指数压低。定量而言，Kolmogorov情形下的分布尾部衰减更慢，意味着重夸克比标准模型预测的更长时间保持高动量。该效应与jet quenching中罕见涨落主导能量损失统计的机制类似。

Conclusion: The work establishes a robust, relativistic framework for modeling heavy quark thermalization in strongly coupled plasmas beyond the Gaussian approximation. By solving the full Kolmogorov equation via Hamilton-Jacobi methods, it reveals the critical role of non-Gaussian fluctuations in delaying equilibration at high momentum—a feature missed by conventional Fokker-Planck treatments. This approach bridges the ultra-relativistic and non-relativistic regimes and offers a foundation for improved phenomenological implementations in heavy-ion collision analyses.
\n\n
本工作建立了一个超越高斯近似的、适用于强耦合等离子体中重夸克热化的稳健相对论框架。通过Hamilton-Jacobi方法求解完整的Kolmogorov方程，揭示了非高斯涨落在延迟高动量热化过程中的关键作用——这是传统Fokker-Planck方法所遗漏的。该方法贯通了极端相对论与非相对论区域，为重离子碰撞现象学分析提供了改进的基础。

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of heavy quarks propagating through the strongly coupled plasma of $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills (SYM) theory at nonzero temperature in terms of the corresponding Kolmogorov equation, which correctly describes their kinetic equilibration and the non-Gaussian fluctuations in their momenta without having to restrict their velocity to the non-relativistic regime. Leveraging the heavy quark limit, we show that the evolution of the momentum space distribution function can be reformulated as a Hamilton-Jacobi problem, and therefore can be solved in terms of first-order ordinary differential equations. We solve these evolution equations in an infinite thermal plasma with a constant temperature for initial conditions specified by spherically symmetric heavy quark momentum distributions with a phenomenologically motivated shape that is steeply falling at large momentum. To highlight the distinctive features of the kinetic equilibration process, we compare their solutions with Fokker-Planck dynamics with the same drag coefficient and fluctuations chosen by hand to guarantee equilibration. We find qualitatively similar dynamics at small momentum, and very different dynamics at large momentum, where, much like in jet quenching phenomena, the steepness of the momentum distribution gives a larger relevance to unlikely events in which a heavy quark loses little momentum in a given time step. Such events are much less unlikely in the Kolmogorov evolution than in Fokker-Planck evolution with the same mean energy loss, meaning that equilibration at large momentum is significantly delayed. Our results provide a systematic description of heavy quarks propagating through strongly coupled plasma from the ultra-relativistic to the non-relativistic regime and point the way towards implementation in phenomenological studies.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neutrino self-interactions via a light neutrinophilic scalar are theoretically well-motivated and can address anomalies in cosmological observations (e.g., $H_0$ tension). This work fills that gap to provide robust, up-to-date constraints.

通过轻的neutrinophilic标量实现的中微子自相互作用在理论上具有充分动机，并可能解释宇宙学观测中的异常（例如$H_0$张力）。然而，这类模型必须满足严格的电弱精密测试。以往研究忽略了某些规范不变的辐射修正——尤其是修改中微子带电流耦合的修正——这些修正显著影响费米常数（$G_F$）和$Δr$参数等可观测量。本文填补了这一空白，提供了稳健且最新的约束。

Method: The authors compute finite one-loop radiative corrections in a simplified model featuring a neutrinophilic scalar coupled only to active neutrinos, ensuring gauge invariance via the pinch technique or background field method. They focus on the correction to the $W$-boson–neutrino–charged-lepton vertex, which shifts the effective Fermi constant and modifies the $Δr$ parameter. Constraints are derived by comparing theoretical predictions with high-precision measurements of $G_F$ and $M_W$. The analysis is then extended to a UV-complete model containing a TeV-scale $SU(2)_L$ triplet scalar, where they assess when heavy-particle loop contributions become non-negligible by varying mass splittings and couplings.
\n\n
作者在一个仅将neutrinophilic标量与活跃中微子耦合的简化模型中计算有限的一圈辐射修正，通过pinch technique或background field method确保规范不变性。他们聚焦于$W$玻色子–中微子–带电轻子顶点的修正，该修正会移动有效费米常数并修改$Δr$参数。通过将理论预言与$G_F$和$M_W$的高精度测量进行比较，导出约束。随后将分析扩展到一个包含TeV尺度$SU(2)_L$三重态标量的UV完备模型中，通过改变质量劈裂和耦合强度，评估重粒子圈图贡献何时变得不可忽略。

Result: For mediator masses $m_\phi \gtrsim 300~\text{MeV}$, the dominant constraint arises from the logarithmically enhanced correction to the neutrino charged-current coupling, yielding upper limits on the neutrinophilic Yukawa coupling $y_\nu \lesssim 10^{-3} - 10^{-2}$ (depending on $m_\phi$). In the UV-complete model with an $SU(2)_L$ triplet, these simplified-model bounds remain valid provided the mass splitting between neutral and charged components of the triplet is small ($\Delta m \ll m_{\text{triplet}}$); otherwise, additional loop contributions from the triplet modify the constraints.
\n\n
对于媒介粒子质量$m_\phi \gtrsim 300~\text{MeV}$，主导约束来自对中微子带电流耦合的对数增强修正，由此得到neutrinophilic Yukawa耦合的上限$y_\nu \lesssim 10^{-3} - 10^{-2}$（依赖于$m_\phi$）。在包含$SU(2)_L$三重态的UV完备模型中，只要三重态的中性与带电组分之间的质量劈裂较小（$\Delta m \ll m_{\text{triplet}}$），简化模型的约束仍然成立；否则，三重态带来的额外圈图贡献会修改这些限制。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that electroweak precision data—particularly through the $Δr$ parameter and Fermi constant—provide the strongest current constraints on neutrinophilic self-interacting neutrino models for mediator masses above a few hundred MeV. It highlights the necessity of including gauge-invariant radiative corrections to neutrino interactions, which were previously omitted. The results are robust across a broad class of UV completions, offering a reliable benchmark for future theoretical and experimental studies of self-interacting neutrinos.

本文表明，电弱精密数据——特别是通过$Δr$参数和费米常数——对媒介粒子质量高于几百MeV的neutrinophilic中微子自相互作用模型提供了当前最强的约束。文章强调了必须包含此前被忽略的、规范不变的中微子相互作用辐射修正。这些结果在一大类UV完备模型中具有鲁棒性，为未来关于自相互作用中微子的理论与实验研究提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: Strong self-interaction among the active neutrinos mediated by a neutrinophilic scalar is a well-motivated target of particle physics and cosmological probes. In this article, we present precision electroweak constraints on models for neutrino self-interaction. We first work in the simplified model where the finite radiative corrections are obtained with the guidance of gauge invariance. These corrections are logarithmically enhanced for small mediator masses. We point out the importance of neutrino charged-current coupling correction and its impact on the $Δr$ parameter and Fermi constant measurements. This effect was overlooked previously and allows us to derive the leading constraint on the neutrinophilic couplings for mediator mass above a few hundred MeV. We investigate the robustness of the result in a concrete UV completion which further includes a TeV-scale $SU(2)_L$ triplet scalar and find the simplified model constraints continue to hold for a wide range of parameter space. We pin down moving parts in the UV complete model and conditions when the contributions from heavy particle loops are no longer negligible. Our results serve as a useful road map for future explorations of the self-interacting neutrino paradigm.

### [25] [Majoron Dark Matter, High-Scale Seesaw, and Leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02706)
*Brian Batell,Arnab Dasgupta,Swapnil Dutta,Akshay Ghalsasi*

Main category: hep-ph

本文研究了在高标度seesaw模型中伴随轻子数自发破缺产生的majoron暗物质，表明其可作为亚MeV暗物质候选者，通过misalignment机制和拓扑缺陷（如宇宙弦）产生，并在CMB等曲率扰动、引力波背景、X射线/软伽马射线衰变信号、黑洞超辐射及Lyman-$\\alpha$森林观测中留下可观测印记。

Abstract: We study the cosmology and observational probes of majoron dark matter in a high-scale seesaw framework with spontaneously broken lepton number. 当磁场强度满足 $|eB| \\lesssim Q_s^2$ 时，胶子分裂为夸克-反夸克对在 bottom-up 演化的最早阶段变得高效。这导致硬夸克的早期产生，改变预平衡等离子体的化学组成，并引入一条新的化学平衡路径。反作用效应还可能进一步修改胶子动力学并增强介质的电磁电导率。

The qualitative outcomes are highly sensitive to the spacetime profile and lifetime of the magnetic field, suggesting that future studies incorporating realistic $B$-field evolution could refine predictions for quark production and transport properties in heavy-ion collisions.
\n\n
本文提出了一个磁辅助的 bottom-up 热化变体，在该图像中，强电磁场显著改变了早期部分子动力学。定性结果对磁场的时空分布和寿命高度敏感，表明未来结合更真实的 $B$ 场演化的研究可改进对重离子碰撞中夸克产生和输运性质的预测。

Abstract: We investigate how a strong magnetic field generated in noncentral heavy-ion collisions may modify the bottom-up equilibration scenario. In the conventional weak-coupling picture, the earliest stages of the evolution are dominated by overoccupied gluons, while quark production is parametrically delayed. In a background magnetic field, however, additional inelastic channels become kinematically allowed or enhanced, most notably gluon decay into quark-antiquark pairs, $g\to q+\bar q$. Using parametric estimates, we show that for sufficiently strong fields, with $|eB|$ approaching the saturation scale squared, $Q_s^2$, magnetic-field-induced quark production can become important during the earliest stages of bottom-up evolution. This mechanism can populate the hard quark sector, modify the chemical composition of the pre-equilibrium matter, and provide an additional pathway toward chemical equilibration. We also discuss possible back-reaction effects, including quark-antiquark annihilation, depletion of the hard-gluon sector, and the potential feedback of early quark production on the electromagnetic conductivity of the medium. This exploratory study of a magnetically assisted bottom-up scenario provides a natural extension of the standard framework, with qualitative predictions that depend sensitively on the lifetime and spacetime profile of the magnetic field.

  <summary>Details</summary>

本文旨在为传统上通过高维规范理论中的Wilson线实现的额外维度axion机制提供一个完全四维且可重整化的描述，以澄清axion shift对称性的微观起源及其对量子修正的保护机制。这一动机源于理解axion质量（即其shift对称性的鲁棒性）如何在紫外完备的设定中产生，以及非微扰效应如何在5D与4D图像之间对应。

Method: The authors employ dimensional deconstruction to replace the fifth dimension with a 4D quiver (moose) gauge theory consisting of multiple $U(1)$ nodes connected by link scalar fields. The axion is identified as the phase of the product of these link fields, emerging as a collective pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson. The 5D Chern-Simons term responsible for the axion–gluon coupling is mapped to a 4D gauged Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) term. Non-perturbative effects are analyzed through both zero-mode and fractional instantons, and axion potentials from bulk matter and boundary operators are computed to exhibit nonlocal exponential suppression $\sim e^{-m L}$, where $L$ is the effective size of the extra dimension.

作者采用dimensional deconstruction方法，将第五维度替换为由多个$U(1)$节点通过link标量场连接而成的4D quiver（moose）规范理论。Axion被识别为这些link场乘积的相位，作为集体pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone玻色子出现。负责axion–胶子耦合的5D Chern-Simons项被映射为4D gauged Wess-Zumino-Witten（WZW）项。通过零模和“fractional” instanton分析非微扰效应，并计算来自体物质场和边界局域对称性破缺算符的axion势能，展现出非局域指数压低$\sim e^{-m L}$，其中$L$为额外维度的有效尺度。

Result: The 4D moose model successfully reproduces key features of the 5D axion: (i) the axion arises as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson protected by an emergent shift symmetry; (ii) its coupling to QCD is encoded in a gauged WZW term, matching the 5D CS term; (iii) axion potentials from bulk or boundary sources exhibit nonlocal suppression consistent with extra-dimensional propagation; and (iv) while fractional instantons are exponentially suppressed in the 5D regime, their contribution can become significant when instanton size falls below the 5D cutoff scale ($\\rho \\lesssim 1/\\Lambda_{5D}$), potentially affecting axion phenomenology.

该4D moose模型成功复现了5D axion的关键特征：(i) axion作为受涌现shift对称性保护的pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone玻色子出现；(ii) 其与QCD的耦合由gauged WZW项编码，与5D CS项一致；(iii) 来自体或边界源的axion势能展现出与额外维度传播一致的非局域压低；(iv) 虽然fractional instanton在5D区域呈指数压低，但当instanton尺度小于5D截断尺度（$\\rho \\lesssim 1/\\Lambda_{5D}$）时，其贡献可能变得显著，从而影响axion现象学。

Conclusion: This work establishes a consistent, renormalizable 4D framework that captures the essential physics of extra-dimensional axions, offering a transparent realization of the axion shift symmetry and its quality. It highlights the interplay between UV sensitivity and non-perturbative effects, showing that the validity of the 5D effective description breaks down for sufficiently small instantons, which must be treated within the full 4D deconstructed theory.

Abstract: We present a four-dimensional deconstruction of the extra-dimensional axion arising from a $U(1)$ gauge theory in a five-dimensional orbifold, where the axion is identified with the Wilson line of the $U(1)$ gauge field and its coupling to QCD is generated by a 5D Chern-Simons (CS) term. We construct the corresponding 4D moose (quiver) gauge theory with link scalar fields, in which the axion emerges as a collective pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson. The axion-gluon coupling is described by a gauged Wess-Zumino-Witten term, providing the 4D counterpart of the 5D CS term. We further analyze non-perturbative effects from zero-mode and ``fractional'' instanton configurations. While the latter is exponentially suppressed in the regime corresponding to the 5D description, ensuring consistency with the higher-dimensional picture, we point out that this suppression can break down for smaller instantons whose inverse size exceeds the 5D cutoff scale, leading to a potentially significant effect. We also study axion potentials induced by bulk matter fields and boundary-localized symmetry-breaking operators, reproducing the characteristic nonlocal suppression associated with propagation in the extra dimension.
