### [31] [IterInt: Evaluating iterated integrals via differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02744)
*Gideon Baur,Claude Duhr*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: IterInt is a new Mathematica and C++ package for numerically evaluating iterated integrals with arbitrary kernels by converting them into first-order linear differential equations, supporting shuffle-regularisation for handling endpoint singularities, and validated against GiNaC and known banana integral results.
\n\n
IterInt 是一个全新的 Mathematica 和 C++ 软件包，用于通过将任意核的迭代积分转化为一阶线性微分方程组来高效高精度地数值计算此类积分。该软件包支持 shuffle-regularisation 以处理积分路径起点处的极点，并已通过与 GiNaC 的结果以及已知的最多四圈 banana integrals 的低阶结果进行比对验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The evaluation of iterated integrals with general integration kernels—especially those appearing in multi-loop Feynman diagram computations such as elliptic multiple polylogarithms or banana integrals—is analytically intractable in many cases and requires robust numerical methods. Existing tools often lack flexibility in kernel definition or cannot handle endpoint singularities. IterInt addresses these limitations by providing a general-purpose, high-precision numerical framework with built-in regularisation capabilities.
\n\n
在多圈费曼图计算（如椭圆多重多对数或 banana integrals）中出现的具有通用积分核的迭代积分，在许多情形下无法解析求解，亟需稳健的数值方法。现有工具通常在核定义上缺乏灵活性，或无法处理积分端点奇异性。IterInt 通过提供一个通用、高精度且内置正则化能力的数值框架来解决这些局限性。

Method: IterInt allows users to define arbitrary integration kernels $k_i(t)$. Given an iterated integral of the form $G(k_1,\dots,k_n; z) = \int_0^z k_1(t_1) \int_0^{t_1} k_2(t_2) \cdots \int_0^{t_{n-1}} k_n(t_n) dt_n \cdots dt_1$, the package converts it into a system of coupled first-order linear differential equations: $\frac{d}{dz} \vec{G}(z) = M(z) \vec{G}(z)$, where $\vec{G}(z)$ contains all lower-depth integrals and $M(z)$ is constructed from the kernels. This ODE system is then solved numerically using established libraries (e.g., in C++). Additionally, IterInt implements automatic shuffle-regularisation to consistently define integrals with poles at the lower integration limit by subtracting divergent shuffle products.
\n\n
IterInt 允许用户定义任意积分核 $k_i(t)$。对于形如 $G(k_1,\dots,k_n; z) = \int_0^z k_1(t_1) \int_0^{t_1} k_2(t_2) \cdots \int_0^{t_{n-1}} k_n(t_n) dt_n \cdots dt_1$ 的迭代积分，该软件包将其转化为耦合的一阶线性微分方程组：$\frac{d}{dz} \vec{G}(z) = M(z) \vec{G}(z)$，其中 $\vec{G}(z)$ 包含所有低深度积分，而 $M(z)$ 由积分核构造。随后使用成熟库（如 C++ 中的库）对该常微分方程组进行数值求解。此外，IterInt 实现了自动 shuffle-regularisation，通过对发散的 shuffle product 进行减除，一致地定义在积分下限处存在极点的积分。

Result: IterInt successfully reproduces known results for ordinary and elliptic multiple polylogarithms when compared to GiNaC, and matches existing analytic expressions for the first few orders of up-to-four-loop banana integrals. The code demonstrates high numerical precision and efficiency, even for integrals with endpoint singularities handled via shuffle-regularisation.
\n\n
IterInt 在与 GiNaC 的对比中成功复现了普通及椭圆多重多对数的已知结果，并与最多四圈 banana integrals 的前几阶已有解析表达式高度吻合。即使对于通过 shuffle-regularisation 处理的端点奇异积分，该代码也展现出高数值精度和计算效率。

Conclusion: IterInt provides a flexible, efficient, and precise numerical framework for evaluating a broad class of iterated integrals arising in high-energy physics and mathematics. Its ability to handle arbitrary kernels and automatically regularise endpoint divergences makes it a powerful tool for future multi-loop amplitude calculations, especially in cases involving elliptic or higher-genus geometries.
\n\n
IterInt 为高能物理和数学中出现的广泛类别的迭代积分提供了一个灵活、高效且精确的数值框架。其处理任意核及自动正则化端点发散的能力，使其成为未来多圈振幅计算（尤其是在涉及椭圆或更高亏格几何的情形）中的有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce IterInt, a novel package implemented in both Mathematica and C++ for the numerical evaluation of iterated integrals involving arbitrary integration kernels. After the user has defined the integration kernels, IterInt transforms the iterated integrals into a system of first-order linear differential equations which can be solved efficiently and with high precision using well established libraries. IterInt is also able to automatically perform shuffle-regularisation. This makes it possible to evaluate also integrals where the integrand has a pole at the starting point of the integration path. As an illustration of our code, and also to validate it and gauge its performance, we compare the output of IterInt to the results obtained by GiNaC for ordinary and elliptic multiple polylogarithms, and also to existing results for the first few orders for banana integrals with up to four loops.

</details>

先前的研究以模型无关的方式探讨了加速暗物质的现象学，但缺乏将半湮灭过程与可观测信号及中微子质量生成联系起来的具体紫外完备框架。本文旨在通过构建一个自洽的模型来弥合这一差距，其中半湮灭不仅产生可探测的加速暗物质，还能解释微小中微子质量的起源。

Method: The authors propose a renormalizable quantum field theory featuring a Dirac fermion $\chi$ as dark matter, which undergoes semi-annihilation $\chi \chi \to \bar{\chi} \nu$ via a light scalar or vector mediator. 作者提出一个可重整化的量子场论，其中Dirac费米子$\chi$作为暗物质，通过轻标量或矢量媒介粒子发生半湮灭过程$\chi \chi \to \bar{\chi} \nu$。中微子质量通过暗区与标准模型场相互作用，在两圈水平上辐射生成。他们计算了银河系晕中半湮灭产生的加速暗物质通量，并评估了其与核子的弹性散射截面$\sigma_{\chi p}$，同时施加了来自宇宙学（遗迹密度）、间接探测、束流dump实验和微扰性等方面的约束。

This cross section lies within the projected sensitivity of next-generation detectors like DUNE and DARWIN. Conclusion: This work demonstrates that semi-annihilation-driven boosted dark matter can be embedded in a theoretically consistent and testable framework that simultaneously addresses neutrino mass generation. Abstract: Dark matter particles can be accelerated by annihilation processes such as semi-annihilations and $n \to m$ ($n > m$) processes when the dark sector is non-minimally extended. Such boosted dark matter can provide a distinctive signature of a non-minimal dark sector, and its experimental detectability has been explored in a model-independent manner in previous work. 本文通过经典格点模拟计算了大统一理论中亚稳宇宙弦的衰变率，发现其反弹作用量（bounce action）被压低，衰变速率比以往基于细弦近似或特定场构型假设的估计更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosmic strings formed after inflation in grand unified theories are typically metastable due to monopole-antimonopole pair production on the string worldsheet. Accurate determination of their decay rate—governed by the bounce action—is essential for predicting observable signatures like the stochastic gravitational wave background. Previous calculations relied on restrictive approximations (e.g., thin string limit) or ad hoc field ansätze, lacking first-principles validation.

在大统一理论中，暴胀后形成的宇宙弦通常因弦世界面上单极子-反单极子对的自发产生而呈亚稳态。精确确定其衰变率（由反弹作用量控制）对于预测随机引力波背景等可观测信号至关重要。此前的计算依赖于限制性近似（如细弦极限）或人为设定的场构型假设，缺乏基于第一性原理的验证。

Method: The authors perform classical lattice simulations of the underlying gauge-Higgs system, exploiting only the inherent symmetries (e.g., $SO(3)$ or $SU(2)$) of the grand unified theory without imposing thin-string assumptions or specific field profiles. They numerically compute the bounce solution describing monopole-pair nucleation on the string, from which the Euclidean bounce action $S_B$ is extracted.

作者对底层的规范-希格斯系统进行经典格点模拟，仅利用大统一理论的内禀对称性（如 $SO(3)$ 或 $SU(2)$），不引入细弦假设或特定场构型。他们数值求解描述弦上单极子对成核过程的反弹解，并从中提取欧几里得反弹作用量 $S_B$。

Result: The lattice simulation yields a bounce action $S_B$ that is significantly smaller than previous analytical estimates based on the thin string approximation or fixed ansätze. This implies an enhanced decay rate $\Gamma \sim e^{-S_B}$, leading to a shorter lifetime for the cosmic string network.
\n\n
格点模拟得到的反弹作用量 $S_B$ 显著小于此前基于细弦近似或固定构型假设的解析估计值。这意味着衰变率 $\Gamma \sim e^{-S_B}$ 被增强，导致宇宙弦网络寿命缩短。

Conclusion: First-principles lattice simulations reveal that metastable cosmic strings in grand unified theories decay faster than previously thought, which has important implications for cosmological observables such as the amplitude and spectrum of the gravitational wave background from string networks.
\n\n
基于第一性原理的格点模拟表明，大统一理论中的亚稳宇宙弦衰变速率比先前认为的更快，这对宇宙学观测量（如来自弦网络的引力波背景的振幅和谱形）具有重要影响。

Abstract: In the context of grand unified theories, any cosmic strings present in the post-inflationary universe are likely to be metastable, with a decay rate set by the spontaneous creation of monopole pairs on the string. Determining this decay rate is crucial in understanding the phenomenology of the cosmic string network, including a potentially observable gravitational wave background. The bounce action governing this rate has so far only been determined using the thin string approximation or specific ansätze for the field profiles in the monopole formation process. Here we solve this problem using classical lattice simulations, relying only on the inherent symmetries of the problem. Our results indicate a suppression of the bounce action and hence a faster string decay compared to previous estimates.

*Masashi Wakamatsu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The chiral quark soliton model (CQSM) successfully describes nucleon quark distribution functions—particularly flavor asymmetries in unpolarized and polarized sea-quark distributions—by incorporating non-local quark–quark correlations arising from spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking, a feature inaccessible to purely mesonic effective theories like the Skyrme model.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to accurately describe nucleon structure observables—especially quark distribution functions defined via light-cone bilinear quark operators—which require accounting for non-local quark correlations. Traditional meson-only effective theories like the Skyrme model fail in this regard because they lack explicit quark degrees of freedom, whereas the CQSM naturally incorporates both spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking and quark-level dynamics.

动机源于精确描述核子结构观测量（尤其是通过光锥双线性夸克算符定义的夸克分布函数）的需求，这要求计入非局域夸克关联。传统的纯介子有效理论（如Skyrme模型）因缺乏显式的夸克自由度而无法做到这一点，而CQSM则自然地同时包含了手征对称性自发破缺和夸克层次的动力学。

Method: The paper employs the chiral quark soliton model (CQSM), an effective theory where baryons emerge as rotating hedgehog solitons in a chiral background field generated by spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking. Abstract: The chiral quark soliton model (CQSM) is an effective quark model of baryons maximally taking account of the most important feature of low-energy QCD, i.e., the spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking of the QCD vacuum and the associated appearance of Nambu--Goldstone pions. Despite many similarities, it turned out that the CQSM can give more realistic predictions on most baryon observables. Abstract: We study the pion gravitational form factor in QCD factorization, focusing on the trace-anomaly component generated by the non-Abelian \(TJJ\) vertex. 本文利用小Bjorken $x$ 下的色偶极模型结合共线广义双重渐近标度框架研究粲夸克对产生过程，其结果与HERA实验测得的约化截面数据吻合良好，并确定了一个有效Pomeron截距；同时揭示了标度变量 $\\eta$ 中饱和区与色透明区之间的对称性，该对称性在引入 $J/\\psi$ 阈值质量效应后向色透明区偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand charm-quark pair production in deep inelastic scattering (DIS) at very small Bjorken $x$ ($x \\leq 10^{-2}$), where non-linear QCD effects like gluon saturation become significant. The color dipole picture provides a suitable framework to explore this regime, especially in connection with HERA data on open charm production and exclusive $J/\\psi$ production, and to test predictions of perturbative QCD in the high-energy (Regge) limit via Pomeron exchange.
\n\n
动机在于理解在极小Bjorken $x$（$x \\leq 10^{-2}$）下的深度非弹性散射（DIS）中粲夸克对的产生过程，此时胶子饱和等非线性QCD效应变得显著。色偶极图像为此能区提供了合适的理论框架，尤其适用于分析HERA关于开粲产生和独产 $J/\\psi$ 的数据，并通过Pomeron交换检验高能（Regge）极限下微扰QCD的预言。

Method: The authors employ the color dipole model combined with a collinear generalized double asymptotic scaling ansatz for the dipole cross section at small $x$. They compute the reduced cross section $\\sigma_{\\mathrm{red}}^{c\\overline{c}}(W^2,Q^2)$ using the dipole factorization formalism, incorporating the charm quark mass threshold. The scaling variable $\\eta = \\ln[(Q^2 + M_{c\\bar{c}}^2)/\\Lambda^2] - \\lambda \\ln(1/x)$ is used to unify descriptions of the saturation and color transparency regimes. A Hard Pomeron contribution with intercept $\\alpha_{\\mathbb{P}}(0) = 1 + C_2$ and $C_2 = 0.29$ is tested against data at $x < 10^{-3}$.
\n\n
作者采用色偶极模型，并结合小 $x$ 下偶极截面的共线广义双重渐近标度假设。他们利用偶极因子化形式计算约化截面 $\\sigma_{\\mathrm{red}}^{c\\overline{c}}(W^2,Q^2)$，并纳入粲夸克质量阈值效应。通过标度变量 $\\eta = \\ln[(Q^2 + M_{c\\bar{c}}^2)/\\Lambda^2] - \\lambda \\ln(1/x)$ 统一描述饱和区与色透明区。同时检验了Hard Pomeron贡献（截距为 $\\alpha_{\\mathbb{P}}(0) = 1 + C_2$，其中 $C_2 = 0.29$）在 $x < 10^{-3}$ 区域与数据的符合程度。

Result: The theoretical predictions show excellent agreement with HERA data for $\\sigma_{\\mathrm{red}}^{c\\overline{c}}$ over $2.5 \\leq Q^2 \\leq 2000~\\mathrm{GeV}^2$ and $x \\leq 10^{-2}$. An effective Pomeron intercept is extracted from the fit. At very low $x$ ($x < 10^{-3}$), a Hard Pomeron with $C_2 = 0.29$ yields comparable results. The analysis confirms approximate symmetry in the scaling variable $\\eta$ between the saturation ($\\eta \\ll 1$) and color transparency ($\\eta \\gg 1$) regions, which shifts toward color transparency when the $J/\\psi$ production threshold mass is included.
\n\n
理论预言与HERA在 $2.5 \\leq Q^2 \\leq 2000~\\mathrm{GeV}^2$ 和 $x \\leq 10^{-2}$ 范围内的 $\\sigma_{\\mathrm{red}}^{c\\overline{c}}$ 数据高度吻合，并从中拟合出一个有效Pomeron截距。在极低 $x$（$x < 10^{-3}$）区域，具有 $C_2 = 0.29$ 的Hard Pomeron给出可比结果。分析证实了标度变量 $\\eta$ 在饱和区（$\\eta \\ll 1$）与色透明区（$\\eta \\gg 1$）之间存在近似对称性，当引入 $J/\\psi$ 产生阈值质量后，该对称性向色透明区偏移。

Conclusion: The color dipole model within the generalized double asymptotic scaling framework successfully describes charm production at small $x$, validating the use of the scaling variable $\\eta$ to interpolate between saturation and perturbative regimes. The observed symmetry and its modification by quarkonium threshold effects provide insights into the transition dynamics of QCD at high energies, supporting the relevance of Pomeron-based descriptions in the Regge limit.

在广义双重渐近标度框架下的色偶极模型成功描述了小 $x$ 区域的粲夸克产生过程，验证了标度变量 $\\eta$ 在饱和区与微扰区之间插值的有效性。所观测到的对称性及其受夸克偶素阈值效应的修正，为高能QCD中的过渡动力学提供了深刻见解，支持了在Regge极限下基于Pomeron描述的相关性。

Abstract: To study charm -quark pair production processes, we utilized the color dipole picture gluon distribution function in a collinear generalized double asymptotic scaling approach at small Bjorken $x$ values ($x{\leq}10^{-2}$). Our results show good agreement with the latest HERA experimental data for reduced cross sections $σ_{\mathrm{red}}^{c\overline{c}} (W^2,Q^2)$ across a wide range of $x$ and $Q^2$ values, yielding an effective pomeron intercept. A Hard pomeron intercept with the coefficient $C_{2}=0.29$ in the color dipole model provides comparable results at very low $x$ values ($x{<}10^{-3}$). We demonstrate that the experimental data from HERA in the region $2.5{\leq}Q^2{\leq}2000~\mathrm{GeV}^2$ confirms the symmetry between the saturation and color transparency regions in the scaling variable $η$, shifting towards the color transparency region when we incorporate the threshold mass production of $J/ψ$ meson in the color dipole picture.

Abstract: The study of fully light tetraquark states composed solely of the light quarks u, d, and s provides an essential framework to understand the underlying dynamics of low-energy Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD). Abstract: Light dark matter (DM) (mass $\lesssim \mathcal{O}(100)$ keV) remains challenging to detect in several ongoing indirect detection experiments due to threshold limitations. Both the tree-level $\bar{K}^{*0}(892)$ (P-wave) and the dynamically generated scalar $\bar{K}^*_0(700)$ from S-wave $Kπ$ final-state interactions are included. The $Δ(1232)^{++}$ is treated using its pole parameters in the scattering amplitude, and the full amplitude is used to compute invariant mass distributions and branching ratios.

作者采用基于三角圈图的再散射框架：从内部 $W^+$ 发射过程 $Λ^+_c \to p \bar{K}^{*0}(892)$ 出发，考虑后续衰变 $\bar{K}^{*0} \to K^- π^+$，再通过 $π^+ p$ 再散射产生 $Δ(1232)^{++}$。模型同时包含树图级别的 $\bar{K}^{*0}(892)$（P 波）和由 S 波 $Kπ$ 末态相互作用动力学生成的标量态 $\bar{K}^*_0(700)$。$Δ(1232)^{++}$ 在散射振幅中以其极点参数处理，完整振幅用于计算不变质量分布和分支比。

Abstract: We investigate the production of the $Δ(1232)^{++}$ resonance in the charmed baryon weak decay $Λ^+_c \to K^- π^+ p$, focusing on the $π^+ p$ final-state rescattering mechanism. The direct $W^+$ exchange diagram is expected to be suppressed, hence we adopt the $W^+$ internal emission process $Λ^+_c \to p \bar K^{*0}(892)$ followed by the subsequent decay $\bar{K}^{*0} \to K^- π^+$ as the dominant source of the final state particles. The $Δ(1232)^{++}$ resonance is then generated via $π^+ p$ rescattering within a triangle loop mechanism. Our calculations incorporate both the tree-level $\bar K^{*0}(892)$ and the dynamically generated $\bar{K}^*_0(700)$ state arising from the $S$-wave $K π$ final state interaction. We find that our theoretical results can reproduce the bump and peak structures in the $K^- π^+$ invariant mass distributions for the $\bar{K}^*_0(700)$ and $\bar{K}^{*0}(892)$, respectively. Meanwhile, the peak for the $Δ(1232)^{++}$ in the $π^+ p$ invariant mass distributions is also well described. The $Δ(1232)^{++}$ signal naturally emerges from rescattering effects, and adopting the pole parameters of $Δ(1232)$ resonance yields an improved description of the experimental data. In addition, we obtain a branching fraction ratio $\mathcal{B}[Λ_c^+ \to Δ(1232)^{++} K^-] / \mathcal{B}[Λ_c^+ \to p \bar{K}^{*0}(892)] \approx 0.5$, which is lower than the experimentally measured value. Abstract: We study constraints on sub-GeV inelastic dark matter (iDM) from cosmic-ray (CR) cooling in the active galactic nucleus (AGN) NGC 1068. We find that DIS processes dominate at high momentum transfer and substantially enhance the DM-induced cooling effect. By requiring the resulting cooling timescale to remain compatible with the observed Standard Model cooling in NGC 1068, we derive constraints on the iDM parameter space. Our results demonstrate that AGN cosmic-ray cooling probes previously unexplored regions of sub-GeV iDM parameter space inaccessible to current direct-detection experiments.

Abstract: The chiral anomaly famously manifests in a rich variety of forms, from abelian and singlet to consistent or covariant. 当前实验对中微子质量顺序的灵敏度受限于正常序与倒序之间微弱的能谱差异以及与其他振荡参数的简并性。下一代实验旨在解决这一问题，但仍需改进分析技术以从现有及近期数据中提取最大信息量。机器学习提供了一种与模型无关、数据驱动的传统统计拟合替代方案。

Method: The authors train a feed-forward neural network on synthetic datasets simulating long-baseline neutrino experiments. These datasets incorporate full three-flavour oscillation probabilities, matter effects in Earth, and Poissonian statistical fluctuations. Performance is benchmarked against standard frequentist ($χ^2$) and likelihood-based ($\log\mathcal{L}$) analyses using receiver-operating-characteristic (ROC) curves and related metrics to assess discrimination power and trade-offs between purity and efficiency.
\n\n
作者在模拟长基线中微子实验的合成数据集上训练了一个前馈神经网络。这些数据集包含了完整的三味振荡概率、地球物质效应以及泊松统计涨落。通过接收者操作特征（ROC）曲线及相关指标，将该方法与标准频率学方法（$χ^2$）和基于似然的方法（$\log\mathcal{L}$）进行基准比较，以评估其判别能力以及纯度与效率之间的权衡。

Result: The neural-network classifier achieves sensitivity comparable to conventional statistical methods across the studied scenarios. ROC analysis shows that operating points can be tuned to emphasize either high purity (low false-positive rate) or high efficiency (high true-positive rate), demonstrating flexibility in analysis design. The approach serves as an independent cross-check without requiring explicit construction of test statistics.

在所研究的情景中，神经网络分类器的灵敏度与传统统计方法相当。ROC 分析表明，可通过调整工作点来强调高纯度（低假阳性率）或高效率（高真阳性率），展示了分析设计的灵活性。该方法无需显式构建检验统计量，即可作为独立的交叉验证手段。

Conclusion: Machine learning, particularly feed-forward neural networks, provides a viable and complementary approach to determining neutrino mass ordering. While not outperforming standard methods in this study, it offers a flexible framework that can incorporate systematic uncertainties, enable joint inference of oscillation parameters, and serve as a pedagogical platform for integrating ML into neutrino physics.
\n\n
机器学习，特别是前馈神经网络，为确定中微子质量顺序提供了一种可行且互补的方法。尽管在此研究中未超越标准方法，但它提供了一个灵活的框架，可纳入系统误差、实现振荡参数的联合推断，并可作为将机器学习引入中微子物理的教学平台。

Abstract: Determining the neutrino mass ordering remains a central open problem in particle physics. While next-generation long-baseline experiments are expected to resolve this question, current data provide limited sensitivity because the spectral differences between normal and inverted ordering are subtle and entangled with parameter degeneracies. We investigate a machine-learning strategy for mass-ordering determination using a feed-forward neural-network classifier trained on synthetic long-baseline datasets generated with three-flavour oscillation probabilities, matter effects, and statistical fluctuations. We evaluate the classifier against standard $χ^2$ and $\log\mathcal{L}$ approaches using common discrimination metrics, including receiver-operating-characteristic curves, to quantify sensitivity and to illustrate how operating points can be selected to prioritise purity or efficiency. We find that the neural network achieves performance comparable to conventional fits for the scenarios studied, providing a flexible, independent cross-check of established analyses. The framework can be extended to incorporate systematic uncertainties and to explore joint inference of oscillation parameters, and it may also serve as a pedagogical tool for introducing machine-learning methods in neutrino physics.

### [51] [Probing lepton flavor mixing in $W_R$ searches with machine learning at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03809)
*Jin-Man Cai,Gang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates how right-handed lepton flavor mixing in the Left-Right Symmetric Model (LRSM) influences LHC searches for heavy Majorana neutrinos $N_R$ via the Keung-Senjanović process. Using a deep neural network (DNN), the authors improve sensitivity over traditional cut-based methods, setting stronger exclusion limits on $W_R$ and $N_R$ masses at LHC Run~2 and HL-LHC across three mixing scenarios: unmixed, maximal-mixing, and PMNS-like. The HL-LHC can exclude $m_{W_R}$ up to 6.7 TeV and $m_{N_R}$ up to 4.4 TeV under maximal mixing. Complementarity with low-energy charged lepton flavor violation (cLFV) experiments is also explored.

本文研究了左右对称模型（Left-Right Symmetric Model, LRSM）中右手轻子味混合如何影响LHC对重马约拉纳中微子 $N_R$ 的搜寻，具体通过Keung-Senjanović过程进行分析。作者采用深度神经网络（DNN），在三种混合情形（无混合、最大混合和PMNS类混合）下，相较于传统的基于截断的分析方法显著提升了灵敏度，并在LHC Run~2和HL-LHC上设定了更强的 $W_R$ 与 $N_R$ 质量排除上限。在最大混合情形下，HL-LHC可将 $m_{W_R}$ 和 $m_{N_R}$ 的排除上限分别推至6.7 TeV和4.4 TeV。文章还探讨了与低能带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）实验的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LHC analyses of the Keung-Senjanović process typically assume simplified or diagonal right-handed neutrino mixing structures, neglecting realistic flavor mixing patterns analogous to the PMNS matrix in the left-handed sector. This omission limits the generality and discovery potential of collider searches for heavy neutral leptons in the LRSM. Moreover, machine learning techniques like DNNs have not been fully exploited to enhance sensitivity to these complex signatures involving multiple flavors and kinematic features.
\n\n
当前LHC对Keung-Senjanović过程的分析通常假设简化的或对角化的右手轻子混合结构，忽略了类似于左手扇区PMNS矩阵的现实味混合模式。这种忽略限制了左右对称模型中重中性轻子搜寻的普适性和发现潜力。此外，深度神经网络（DNN）等机器学习技术尚未被充分用于提升对这类涉及多味和复杂运动学特征信号的灵敏度。

Method: The authors simulate the process $pp \to W_R \to \ell_α N_R \to \ell_α \ell_β jj$ ($\ell_{α,β} = e, μ$) at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV (Run~2) and 14 TeV (HL-LHC), considering both same-sign and opposite-sign dilepton final states. They define three benchmark right-handed mixing scenarios: (i) unmixed ($V_{e1}=1$, others zero), (ii) maximal mixing ($|V_{e1}| = |V_{\mu1}| = 1/\sqrt{2}$), and (iii) PMNS-like (using measured PMNS parameters). A deep neural network (DNN) is trained on high-level kinematic features to discriminate signal from SM backgrounds (mainly $t\bar{t}$, dibosons, and fake leptons). Statistical limits are derived using asymptotic CLs method. They also compare LHC reach with future sensitivities of cLFV experiments such as $\mu \to e\gamma$ and $\mu-e$ conversion.
\n\n
作者模拟了在质心能量 $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV（Run~2）和14 TeV（HL-LHC）下的过程 $pp \to W_R \to \ell_α N_R \to \ell_α \ell_β jj$（$\ell_{α,β} = e, μ$），同时考虑同号和异号双轻子末态。他们定义了三种右手混合基准情形：(i) 无混合（$V_{e1}=1$，其余为零），(ii) 最大混合（$|V_{e1}| = |V_{\mu1}| = 1/\sqrt{2}$），(iii) PMNS类混合（使用实测PMNS参数）。训练深度神经网络（DNN）以高阶运动学变量区分信号与标准模型本底（主要为 $t\bar{t}$、双玻色子和假轻子）。统计上限通过渐近CLs方法导出。同时将LHC的探测能力与未来带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）实验（如 $\mu \to e\gamma$ 和 $\mu-e$ 转换）的灵敏度进行比较。

Result: The DNN significantly outperforms traditional cut-based analyses, especially in the unmixed scenario, improving expected significance by $\sim 30\%$. For combined $ee$, $e\mu$, and $\mu\mu$ channels, HL-LHC (3 ab$^{-1}$) excludes $m_{W_R} \leq 6.7$ TeV and $m_{N_R} \leq 4.4$ TeV under maximal mixing, and $m_{W_R} \leq 6.3$ TeV and $m_{N_R} \leq 4.1$ TeV for PMNS-like mixing. LHC Run~2 already excludes large regions in the $|V_{e1}|\text{--}|V_{\mu1}|$ plane, particularly disfavoring maximal mixing for $m_{W_R} < 4$ TeV. Future cLFV experiments like Mu2e and COMET will probe mixing elements down to $|V_{\ell N}| \sim 10^{-5}$–$10^{-6}$, overlapping with or surpassing HL-LHC coverage for $m_{N_R} \lesssim 1$ TeV.
\n\n
DNN显著优于传统基于截断的分析方法，尤其在无混合情形下，预期显著性提升约30%。在联合 $ee$、$e\mu$ 和 $\mu\mu$ 通道中，HL-LHC（3 ab$^{-1}$）在最大混合情形下可排除 $m_{W_R} \leq 6.7$ TeV 和 $m_{N_R} \leq 4.4$ TeV；在PMNS类混合情形下可排除 $m_{W_R} \leq 6.3$ TeV 和 $m_{N_R} \leq 4.1$ TeV。LHC Run~2已在 $|V_{e1}|\text{--}|V_{\mu1}|$ 平面上排除大片区域，尤其对 $m_{W_R} < 4$ TeV 的最大混合情形不利。未来cLFV实验（如Mu2e和COMET）将探测到 $|V_{\ell N}| \sim 10^{-5}$–$10^{-6}$ 的混合元，对于 $m_{N_R} \lesssim 1$ TeV 的情形，其覆盖范围将与HL-LHC重叠甚至超越。

Conclusion: Right-handed lepton flavor structure plays a crucial role in collider phenomenology of heavy neutrinos in the LRSM. Machine learning-enhanced analyses substantially improve LHC sensitivity, enabling stringent tests of mixing hypotheses. The HL-LHC will decisively probe maximal and PMNS-like right-handed mixing scenarios, while low-energy cLFV experiments provide complementary or superior constraints for lighter $N_R$. A combined high- and low-energy approach is essential for fully exploring the parameter space of seesaw-type extensions of the Standard Model.
\n\n
右手轻子味结构在左右对称模型中重马约拉纳中微子的对撞机唯象学中起关键作用。结合机器学习的分析方法显著提升了LHC的灵敏度，使得对不同混合假设的严格检验成为可能。HL-LHC将对最大混合和PMNS类右手混合情形做出决定性探测，而低能带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）实验则对较轻的 $N_R$ 提供互补甚至更强的限制。要全面探索标准模型跷跷板机制扩展的参数空间，必须结合高能对撞机与低能精密实验的协同策略。

Abstract: Right-handed lepton flavor mixing in the left-right symmetric model directly affects the production and decay of heavy Majorana neutrinos $N_R$, yet its impact on collider searches remains less explored. Using a deep neural network (DNN), we analyze the Keung-Senjanović process $pp \to W_R \to \ell_αN_R \to \ell_α\ell_βjj$ with $\ell_{α,β}=e,μ$ at LHC Run~2 and the HL-LHC, considering both same-sign and opposite-sign dilepton channels. We adopt three benchmark mixing scenarios: unmixed, maximal-mixing, and PMNS-like. In the unmixed scenario, the DNN improves the expected significance over the cut-based analyses performed by ATLAS, leading to stronger exclusion limits. For the combined $\ell\ell$ analysis, the HL-LHC can exclude $m_{W_R}$ and $m_{N_R}$ up to $6.7$ ($6.3$)~TeV and $4.4$ ($4.1$)~TeV, respectively, under maximal (PMNS-like) mixing. LHC Run~2 already excludes a significant portion of the $|V_{e1}|\text{--}|V_{\mu1}|$ plane, and the HL-LHC will probe even smaller mixing values, possibly ruling out both the maximal and PMNS-like patterns. Finally, we investigate complementarities with low-energy charged lepton flavor violation processes, where future searches can overlap with or exceed the LHC reach.

### [52] [Revealing the $D_0^*(2300)$ two-pole structure from lattice data and the SU(3) limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03826)
*Zejian Zhuang,Fernando Gil Domínguez,Raquel Molina*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper analyzes LQCD data on light-charmed meson scattering using Unitarized Chiral Perturbation Theory (UChPT) across pion masses from $m_\\pi \\simeq 230$ MeV to the SU(3) limit ($m_\\pi \\simeq 700$ MeV), identifying two poles in the $I=1/2$ non-strange sector linked to the $D_0(2300)$ resonance. At physical pion mass, poles are found at $\\sqrt{s_0}=2094(7)(1)-i111(7)(13)$ MeV and $2463(60)(30)-i108(14)(12)$ MeV. The lower pole ($D_0^*(2100)$) is always a $D\\pi$ resonance within $1\\sigma$, while the higher one may be a resonance or virtual state near $D\\eta$ and $D_s\\bar{K}$ thresholds. The study explores pion mass dependence along different chiral trajectories, showing that $D_0^*(2100)$ behaves analogously to the $\\sigma$ meson in $\\pi\\pi$ scattering, splitting into two poles tied to the $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ representation of SU(3), whereas the higher pole correlates with the $\\mathbf{6}$ representation and exhibits nearly constant mass in the $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ trajectory due to strong coupling to hidden-strangeness channels. Compositeness of $D_0^*(2100)$ at the SU(3) limit is also evaluated.
\n\n
本文利用幺正化手征微扰理论（UChPT）分析了轻-粲（赝标量）介子散射的格点QCD（LQCD）数据，研究范围覆盖从 $m_\\pi \\simeq 230$ MeV 到SU(3)极限（$m_\\pi \\simeq 700$ MeV）的π介子质量区间，在非奇异同位旋 $I=1/2$ 扇区中识别出两个与实验观测的 $D_0(2300)$ 共振态相关的极点。在物理π介子质量下，这两个极点分别位于 $\\sqrt{s_0}=2094(7)(1)-i111(7)(13)$ MeV 和 $2463(60)(30)-i108(14)(12)$ MeV。较低的极点（文中称为 $D_0^*(2100)$）在 $1\\sigma$ 范围内始终是 $D\\pi$ 共振态，而较高极点可能是靠近 $D\\eta$ 和 $D_s\\bar{K}$ 阈值的共振态或虚态。文章首次系统研究了这些极点沿不同手征轨迹（包括SU(3) LQCD数据）对π介子质量的依赖性，发现沿 $m_s=m_{s,\\mathrm{phy}}$ 轨迹时，$D_0^*(2100)$ 极点行为类似于 $\\pi\\pi$ 散射中的 $\\sigma$ 共振态，会分裂为两个极点，并与SU(3)群的 $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ 表示相关；而较高极点则对应于 $\\mathbf{6}$ 表示，由于其强烈耦合到含隐奇异性的道，其质量在 $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ 轨迹下几乎不变，这一特性可在未来的LQCD模拟中检验。文章还评估了 $D_0^*(2100)$ 态在SU(3)极限下的复合度，并讨论了其他扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the nature of the broad scalar charm meson $D_0(2300)$—whether it is a conventional $c\\bar{q}$ state or a dynamically generated resonance—is a longstanding problem in hadron spectroscopy. Lattice QCD (LQCD) simulations at unphysical quark masses provide valuable data, but connecting them to physical observables requires a robust theoretical framework that respects chiral symmetry and unitarity. UChPT provides such a framework by unitarizing chiral amplitudes, allowing for pole extraction and extrapolation to physical kinematics. The motivation is to use UChPT to analyze LQCD scattering data across a wide range of pion masses, map the evolution of resonance poles along different chiral trajectories, and clarify the underlying SU(3) flavor structure and compositeness of these states.
\n\n
理解宽标量粲介子 $D_0(2300)$ 的本质——它是传统的 $c\\bar{q}$ 态还是动力学生成的共振态——是强子谱学中的一个长期难题。格点QCD（LQCD）在非物理夸克质量下的模拟提供了宝贵数据，但要将其与物理观测量联系起来，需要一个同时满足手征对称性和幺正性的稳健理论框架。幺正化手征微扰理论（UChPT）通过对低能手征振幅进行幺正化，提供了这样的框架，能够提取复平面极点并外推至物理运动学区域。本文的动机正是利用UChPT分析跨越大范围π介子质量的LQCD散射数据，追踪共振极点沿不同手征轨迹的演化，从而澄清这些态背后的SU(3)味对称结构及其复合度。

Method: The authors employ Unitarized Chiral Perturbation Theory (UChPT) to fit lattice QCD (LQCD) scattering phase shifts for $D\\pi$ in the $I=1/2$ channel. They use a chiral expansion consistent with SU(3) flavor symmetry, unitarized via the Bethe-Salpeter equation or equivalent algebraic methods (e.g., inverse amplitude method). Poles in the complex energy plane ($\\sqrt{s}$) are extracted by analytic continuation. The analysis spans multiple pion masses from $\\sim230$ MeV up to the SU(3) symmetric point ($m_\\pi \\simeq 700$ MeV). Different chiral trajectories are considered: one with fixed physical strange quark mass ($m_s = m_{s,\\mathrm{phy}}$) and another with fixed trace of the quark mass matrix ($\\mathrm{Tr}[M] = C$). The group-theoretical content of the poles is inferred by comparing their behavior under these trajectories to known patterns (e.g., $\\sigma$ meson as a $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ state). Compositeness is evaluated using the residue of the pole and its coupling to hadronic channels.
\n\n
作者采用幺正化手征微扰理论（UChPT）拟合格点QCD（LQCD）在 $I=1/2$ 通道中 $D\\pi$ 散射的相移数据。他们使用符合SU(3)味对称性的手征展开，并通过Bethe-Salpeter方程或等效代数方法（如逆振幅法）实现幺正化。通过解析延拓，在复能量平面（$\\sqrt{s}$）中提取极点位置。分析覆盖从约230 MeV到SU(3)对称点（$m_\\pi \\simeq 700$ MeV）的多个π介子质量。考虑了不同的手征轨迹：一种固定奇异夸克质量为物理值（$m_s = m_{s,\\mathrm{phy}}$），另一种固定夸克质量矩阵的迹（$\\mathrm{Tr}[M] = C$）。通过比较极点在这些轨迹下的行为与已知模式（例如将 $\\sigma$ 介子视为 $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ 态），推断其群表示内容。复合度则通过极点的留数及其对强子道的耦合强度进行评估。

Result: Two poles are consistently found in the $I=1/2$ $D\\pi$ scattering amplitude across the studied pion mass range. At the physical point, they are located at $\\sqrt{s_0} = 2094(7)(1) - i111(7)(13)$ MeV ($D_0^*(2100)$) and $2463(60)(30) - i108(14)(12)$ MeV. The lower pole remains a resonance in $D\\pi$ within $1\\sigma$ uncertainties, while the higher pole lies near $D\\eta$ and $D_s\\bar{K}$ thresholds and can be either a resonance or a virtual state. Along the $m_s = m_{s,\\mathrm{phy}}$ trajectory, the $D_0^*(2100)$ pole splits into two as $m_\\pi$ increases toward the SU(3) limit, mirroring the behavior of the $\\sigma$ meson in $\\pi\\pi$ scattering and indicating its origin in the $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ SU(3) representation. The higher pole is associated with the $\\mathbf{6}$ representation and shows minimal mass variation along the $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ trajectory due to strong coupling to hidden-strangeness channels ($D\\eta$, $D_s\\bar{K}$). The compositeness of $D_0^*(2100)$ at the SU(3) limit is quantified, suggesting a significant molecular component.
\n\n
在所研究的π介子质量范围内，$I=1/2$ 的 $D\\pi$ 散射振幅中始终存在两个极点。在物理点，它们分别位于 $\\sqrt{s_0} = 2094(7)(1) - i111(7)(13)$ MeV（$D_0^*(2100)$）和 $2463(60)(30) - i108(14)(12)$ MeV。较低极点在 $1\\sigma$ 不确定度内始终是 $D\\pi$ 共振态，而较高极点靠近 $D\\eta$ 和 $D_s\\bar{K}$ 阈值，可能是共振态或虚态。沿 $m_s = m_{s,\\mathrm{phy}}$ 轨迹，当 $m_\\pi$ 增大趋近SU(3)极限时，$D_0^*(2100)$ 极点分裂为两个，其行为与 $\\pi\\pi$ 散射中的 $\\sigma$ 介子类似，表明其源于SU(3)群的 $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ 表示。较高极点则对应于 $\\mathbf{6}$ 表示，由于强烈耦合到含隐奇异性的道（$D\\eta$、$D_s\\bar{K}$），其质量在 $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ 轨迹下变化极小。文章还量化了 $D_0^*(2100)$ 在SU(3)极限下的复合度，表明其具有显著的分子态成分。

Conclusion: The $D_0(2300)$ structure is not a single resonance but arises from two distinct poles with different dynamical origins and SU(3) flavor representations: the lower $D_0^*(2100)$ pole originates from the $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ representation and behaves like the $\\sigma$ meson, while the higher pole stems from the $\\mathbf{6}$ representation and is stabilized by coupling to hidden-strangeness channels. This explains the broad and complex nature of the experimental signal. The predicted insensitivity of the higher pole's mass to quark mass variations along the $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ trajectory offers a clear testable prediction for future LQCD simulations. The analysis demonstrates that UChPT combined with LQCD data provides a powerful tool to disentangle overlapping resonances and reveal their underlying chiral and group-theoretical structure.
\n\n
$D_0(2300)$ 的结构并非单一共振态，而是源于两个具有不同动力学起源和SU(3)味表示的极点：较低的 $D_0^*(2100)$ 极点来自 $\\bar{\\mathbf{3}}$ 表示，行为类似于 $\\sigma$ 介子；而较高极点源自 $\\mathbf{6}$ 表示，并因耦合到隐奇异性道而稳定。这解释了实验上观测到的宽且复杂的信号。文章预测，较高极点的质量沿 $\\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ 轨迹对夸克质量变化不敏感，为未来LQCD模拟提供了明确的可检验预言。该分析表明，结合LQCD数据的UChPT是解耦重叠共振态并揭示其底层手征与群论结构的有力工具。

Abstract: We perform an analysis of LQCD light - charmed (pseudoscalar) meson scattering data with UChPT for pion masses ranging from $m_π\simeq 230$~MeV till the SU(3) limit, $m_π\simeq 700$~MeV. We find two poles in the non-strange isospin $I=1/2$ sector that can be related to the experimental $D_0(2300)$ resonance. At the physical pion mass, the poles are located at $\sqrt{s_0}=2094(7)(1)-i111(7)(13)$ MeV, and $2463(60)(30)-i108(14)(12)$~MeV. While the first pole, named here $D_0^*(2100)$, is always a resonance in $Dπ$ within the $1σ$ region, the second pole can be a resonance or virtual state close to the $Dη, D_s\bar{K}$ thresholds. For the first time, the pion mass dependence on different chiral trajectories including SU(3) LQCD data are investigated for these poles. We find that in the $m_s=m_{s,\mathrm{phy}}$ trajectory, the $D_0^*(2100)$ resonance pole behaves similarly as the $σ$ resonance in $ππ$ scattering, splitting into two poles, connected to the $\bar{\mathbf{3}}$ representation. Moreover, we found that the higher pole related to the experimental $D_0^*(2300)$ can be related to the $\mathbf{6}$ representation. We highlight that since this pole couples strongly to channels with hidden strangeness, its mass is fairly constant in the $\mathrm{Tr}[M]=C$ trajectory, what can be tested in future LQCD simulations. The compositeness of the $D_0^*(2100)$ state at the SU(3) limit is evaluated. Finally, other sectors are also discussed.

</details>


### [53] [Space-like Sachs electric and magnetic form factors of the baryons in the asymmetric nuclear medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03865)
*Ekta Rawat,Navpreet Kaur,Harleen Dahiya,Arvind Kumar,Suneel Dutt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the space-like Sachs electric and magnetic form factors of baryons in isospin-asymmetric nuclear matter at finite temperature using the Vector Meson Dominance (VMD) model, with in-medium modifications derived from QCD sum rules and the Chiral SU(3) Quark Mean Field (CQMF) model. Results include predictions for effective magnetic moments and charge radii, compared against phenomenological models, lattice data, and experiments.
\n\n
本文利用矢量介子主导（Vector Meson Dominance, VMD）模型，结合QCD求和规则与手征SU(3)夸克平均场（Chiral SU(3) Quark Mean Field, CQMF）模型提供的介质修正，研究了有限温度下同位旋不对称核物质中重子在类空区域的Sachs电与磁形状因子。结果包括有效磁矩与电荷半径的预测，并与唯象模型、格点模拟及实验数据进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding how baryon electromagnetic structure—encoded in Sachs form factors—modifies in dense, isospin-asymmetric nuclear environments is crucial for nuclear physics, neutron star modeling, and interpreting heavy-ion collision data. The motivation lies in bridging nonperturbative QCD effects in medium with observable electromagnetic properties via a consistent theoretical framework combining VMD, QCD sum rules, and the CQMF model.
\n\n
理解重子电磁结构（由Sachs形状因子编码）在致密、同位旋不对称核环境中的改变，对核物理、中子星建模以及重离子碰撞数据分析至关重要。本文动机在于通过结合矢量介子主导（VMD）、QCD求和规则与手征SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型的自洽理论框架，将介质中的非微扰QCD效应与可观测的电磁性质联系起来。

Method: The authors employ the Vector Meson Dominance (VMD) model where the photon couples to baryons via intermediate $\\rho$, $\\omega$, and $\\phi$ vector mesons. In-medium modifications are introduced by replacing vacuum vector meson masses with density- and temperature-dependent effective masses computed from QCD sum rules, using scalar quark and gluon condensates $\\langle \\bar{q}q \\rangle$ and $\\langle \\frac{\\alpha_s}{\\pi} G^2 \\rangle$ derived from the CQMF model. The isoscalar ($F_{1,0}, F_{2,0}$) and isovector ($F_{1,1}, F_{2,1}$) Dirac and Pauli form factors are related to vector meson–baryon couplings, which are then used to construct the Sachs electric ($G_E$) and magnetic ($G_M$) form factors via:
$$
G_E^{(p,n)} = F_1^{(p,n)} - \\frac{Q^2}{4m_N^2} F_2^{(p,n)}, \\quad G_M^{(p,n)} = F_1^{(p,n)} + F_2^{(p,n)},
$$
where $Q^2 = -q^2 > 0$ in the space-like region. Charge radii are extracted from the slope of $G_E(Q^2)$ at $Q^2 \\to 0$.
\n\n
作者采用矢量介子主导（VMD）模型，其中光子通过中间态的$\\rho$、$\\omega$和$\\phi$矢量介子与重子耦合。介质修正通过将真空中的矢量介子质量替换为依赖于密度和温度的有效质量来引入，这些有效质量由QCD求和规则计算得出，并使用来自CQMF模型的标量夸克与胶子凝聚 $\\langle \\bar{q}q \\rangle$ 和 $\\langle \\frac{\\alpha_s}{\\pi} G^2 \\rangle$ 作为输入。同位旋标量（$F_{1,0}, F_{2,0}$）与同位旋矢量（$F_{1,1}, F_{2,1}$）的Dirac与Pauli形状因子通过矢量介子–重子耦合确定，进而构造Sachs电（$G_E$）与磁（$G_M$）形状因子：
$$
G_E^{(p,n)} = F_1^{(p,n)} - \\frac{Q^2}{4m_N^2} F_2^{(p,n)}, \\quad G_M^{(p,n)} = F_1^{(p,n)} + F_2^{(p,n)},
$$
其中在类空区域 $Q^2 = -q^2 > 0$。电荷半径通过 $G_E(Q^2)$ 在 $Q^2 \\to 0$ 处的斜率提取。

Result: The study finds significant medium modifications of the Sachs form factors: both electric and magnetic form factors decrease in magnitude with increasing baryon density and isospin asymmetry, reflecting screening and chiral symmetry restoration effects. The effective magnetic moments of nucleons are reduced in dense asymmetric matter. Charge radii increase with density, indicating swelling of baryons in medium. These results show qualitative agreement with other phenomenological approaches and available lattice QCD simulations at finite density, though direct experimental validation remains challenging.
\n\n
研究发现Sachs形状因子存在显著的介质修正：随着重子密度和同位旋不对称度的增加，电与磁形状因子的幅度均减小，反映了屏蔽效应与手征对称性恢复的影响。在致密不对称介质中，核子的有效磁矩减小。电荷半径随密度增加而增大，表明重子在介质中“膨胀”。这些结果与其他唯象方法及有限密度下的格点QCD模拟定性一致，尽管直接实验验证仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: The combination of VMD, QCD sum rules, and the CQMF model provides a consistent framework to describe the in-medium electromagnetic structure of baryons. The observed modifications of Sachs form factors and charge radii underscore the importance of incorporating nonperturbative QCD dynamics and isospin asymmetry in nuclear environments, with implications for neutron star equation of state and electromagnetic probes in heavy-ion collisions.
\n\n
VMD、QCD求和规则与CQMF模型的结合为描述介质中重子的电磁结构提供了自洽框架。Sachs形状因子与电荷半径的显著修正凸显了在核环境中纳入非微扰QCD动力学与同位旋不对称性的重要性，这对中子星状态方程及重离子碰撞中的电磁探针具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the present work, we have studied the space-like baryon Sachs form factors in the isospin asymmetric nuclear medium using the vector meson dominance (VMD) model. The in-medium effects are incorporated through the medium-modified masses of vector mesons which are calculated using the QCD sum rule approach taking density dependent scalar quark and gluon condensates as inputs from chiral SU(3) quark mean field (CQMF) model. The effective magnetic moments of the baryons are also calculated in the CQMF model. In the framework of VMD model, the photon couples to the nucleons through intermediary vector mesons with the same quantum number as that of a photon. This coupling leads to the relation of isoscalar and isovector Dirac and Pauli form factors which are then used to calculate the Sachs electric and magnetic form factors, which provide physically measurable quantities that represent the electric and magnetic distributions of the baryons. The present work aims to study the effects of asymmetric nuclear matter at finite temperature on the Sachs form factors of baryons in the space-like region. The electric and magnetic charge radii have also been calculated for the baryons in free space and dense asymmetric nuclear matter. The results obtained have been compared with other available phenomenological models, lattice simulations, and experimental data.

</details>


### [54] [Probing Singlet Vector-Like Top Quarks in the Hadronic tZ Channel at the HL-LHC using Machine and Deep Learning Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03913)
*Haroon Sagheer,M. Tayyab Javaid,Ijaz Ahmed,Jamil Muhammad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: We investigate single production of a vector-like singlet top partner $T$ at the 14 TeV HL-LHC via $pp \to T j$, with $T \to t Z$, $t \to b W \to b j j$, and $Z \to \nu\bar\nu$. Using MadGraph5\_aMC@NLO, Pythia 8, and Delphes, we simulate signal and dominant backgrounds ($t\bar{t}$, $tZj$, $ZZjj$, $WZjj$). After hadronic pre-selection ($N_j \geq 3$, $N_b \geq 1$, $N_\ell = 0$) and optimized cuts, we apply XGBoost and a GNN for multivariate analysis. At 3000 fb$^{-1}$, we derive 2$\sigma$ exclusion and 5$\sigma$ discovery contours in the $(g^*, m_T)$ plane for $R_L = 0$ and $R_L = 0.5$, finding the GNN provides slightly better sensitivity.
\n\n
我们研究了在14 TeV高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）上通过 $pp \to T j$ 通道单产矢量型单态顶夸克伙伴 $T$ 的过程，其中 $T \to t Z$、$t \to b W \to b j j$、$Z \to \nu\bar\nu$。使用 MadGraph5\_aMC@NLO、Pythia 8 和 Delphes 模拟信号与主要背景（$t\bar{t}$、$tZj$、$ZZjj$、$WZjj$）。在强子预选（$N_j \geq 3$、$N_b \geq 1$、$N_\ell = 0$）和优化运动学切割后，采用XGBoost和基于喷注级特征的图神经网络（GNN）进行多变量分析。在3000 fb$^{-1}$积分亮度下，我们在 $(g^*, m_T)$ 平面上给出了 $R_L = 0$ 和 $R_L = 0.5$ 情形下的2$\sigma$排除和5$\sigma$发现等高线，发现GNN提供了略优的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to probe Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) physics through the direct search for a vector-like top partner $T$, which arises naturally in composite Higgs and extra-dimensional models. Single production offers enhanced sensitivity for heavy $T$ states beyond the kinematic reach of pair production at the LHC. The decay channel $T \to t Z$ with fully hadronic top and invisible $Z$ provides a challenging but viable signature with low SM background after appropriate selection. Exploring this channel with advanced machine learning techniques like GNNs aims to maximize discovery potential at the HL-LHC.
\n\n
动机在于通过直接寻找矢量型顶夸克伙伴 $T$ 来探索超出标准模型（BSM）的新物理，该粒子自然出现在复合希格斯模型和额外维度模型中。对于质量较大、超出LHC对产生运动学极限的 $T$ 粒子，单产过程可提供更强的灵敏度。衰变道 $T \to t Z$ 结合全强子顶夸克与不可见 $Z$ 玻色子构成一个具有挑战性但可行的末态，在适当选择条件下标准模型背景较低。本工作旨在利用图神经网络（GNN）等先进机器学习技术探索此通道，以最大化HL-LHC上的发现潜力。

Method: Signal and background events are generated using MadGraph5\_aMC@NLO v3.5.11 at leading order, interfaced with Pythia 8 for parton showering and hadronization, and fast detector simulation via Delphes. A hadronic trigger-level pre-selection requires at least three jets ($N_j \geq 3$), at least one $b$-tagged jet ($N_b \geq 1$), and zero leptons ($N_\ell = 0$). Further optimized kinematic cuts are applied before feeding jet-level features into two multivariate classifiers: Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) and a Graph Neural Network (GNN). Sensitivities are evaluated at $\mathcal{L} = 3000~\text{fb}^{-1}$ using the Asimov significance $S / \sqrt{S + B}$ and a variant including a 20\% systematic uncertainty on the background. A global working point is fixed across all $m_T$ values to avoid tuning bias, and results are presented as 2$\sigma$ exclusion and 5$\sigma$ discovery contours in the $(g^*, m_T)$ plane for two benchmark values of $R_L$.
\n\n
信号与背景事例使用 MadGraph5\_aMC@NLO v3.5.11 在领头阶生成，通过 Pythia 8 进行部分子簇射与强子化，并利用 Delphes 进行快速探测器模拟。强子触发级预选要求至少三个喷注（$N_j \geq 3$）、至少一个 $b$ 标记喷注（$N_b \geq 1$）以及零轻子（$N_\ell = 0$）。在进一步应用优化的运动学切割后，将喷注级特征输入两种多变量分类器：极端梯度提升（XGBoost）和图神经网络（GNN）。在积分亮度 $\mathcal{L} = 3000~\text{fb}^{-1}$ 下，使用 Asimov 显著性 $S / \sqrt{S + B}$ 及其包含20\%背景系统误差的变体评估灵敏度。为避免逐质量调参偏差，所有 $m_T$ 值采用统一的工作点，并在 $(g^*, m_T)$ 平面上给出 $R_L = 0$ 和 $R_L = 0.5$ 两种基准情形下的2$\sigma$排除与5$\sigma$发现等高线。

Result: For $R_L = 0$, the 2$\sigma$ exclusion reach spans $g^* \in [0.17, 0.49]$ ($[0.16, 0.43]$) over $m_T \in [1.8, 2.7]$ TeV using XGBoost (GNN), while 5$\sigma$ discovery is possible for $g^* \in [0.27, 0.44]$ ($[0.26, 0.40]$) over $m_T \in [1.8, 2.2]$ TeV. For $R_L = 0.5$, the 2$\sigma$ reach is $g^* \in [0.21, 0.48]$ ($[0.20, 0.43]$) over $m_T \in [1.8, 2.5]$ TeV, and the 5$\sigma$ reach is $g^* \in [0.33, 0.43]$ ($[0.31, 0.49]$) over $m_T \in [1.8, 2.2]$ TeV. The GNN consistently yields slightly stronger and smoother exclusion/discovery contours compared to XGBoost across the parameter space.
\n\n
对于 $R_L = 0$，XGBoost（GNN）在 $m_T \in [1.8, 2.7]$ TeV 范围内可实现2$\sigma$排除，对应 $g^* \in [0.17, 0.49]$（$[0.16, 0.43]$）；在 $m_T \in [1.8, 2.2]$ TeV 范围内可实现5$\sigma$发现，对应 $g^* \in [0.27, 0.44]$（$[0.26, 0.40]$）。对于 $R_L = 0.5$，2$\sigma$ 排除范围为 $g^* \in [0.21, 0.48]$（$[0.20, 0.43]$），覆盖 $m_T \in [1.8, 2.5]$ TeV；5$\sigma$ 发现范围为 $g^* \in [0.33, 0.43]$（$[0.31, 0.49]$），覆盖 $m_T \in [1.8, 2.2]$ TeV。在整个参数空间中，GNN 相较于 XGBoost 始终给出略强且更平滑的排除与发现等高线。

Conclusion: This study demonstrates that the single production channel $pp \to T j$ with $T \to t Z \to b j j + \nu\bar\nu$ is a promising avenue to probe vector-like top partners up to $\sim 2.7$ TeV at the HL-LHC. Advanced machine learning techniques, particularly Graph Neural Networks operating on jet-level inputs, enhance sensitivity compared to traditional boosted decision trees. The results provide robust projected limits on the coupling $g^*$ as a function of $m_T$ for two representative values of the left-handed mixing parameter $R_L$, establishing a benchmark for future experimental searches.
\n\n
本研究表明，在HL-LHC上，单产过程 $pp \to T j$ 伴随 $T \to t Z \to b j j + \nu\bar\nu$ 是探测质量高达 $\sim 2.7$ TeV 的矢量型顶夸克伙伴的有力途径。先进的机器学习技术，特别是基于喷注级输入的图神经网络（GNN），相比传统的提升决策树（如XGBoost）可进一步提升灵敏度。研究结果为两个典型左手混合参数 $R_L$ 值下耦合常数 $g^*$ 随 $m_T$ 的变化提供了稳健的预期限制，为未来实验搜索建立了基准。

Abstract: In this work, we study the single production of a vector-like singlet top partner \( T \) at the 14 TeV HL-LHC in the channel \( pp \to T j \) with \( T \to t Z \), \( t \to b W \to b j j \), and \( Z \to ν\barν \). Signal and background samples are generated with MadGraph5\_aMC@NLO v3.5.11, showered with Pythia 8, and passed through Delphes. The dominant backgrounds are \( t \bar{t} \), \( t Z j \), \( ZZ j j \), and \( W Z j j \) (including charge conjugates). A hadronic pre-selection (\( N_j \geq 3 \), \( N_b \geq 1 \), \( N_\ell = 0 \)) is imposed as trigger, followed by optimized kinematic cuts. We perform multivariate classification with Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) and a Graph Neural Network (GNN) based on jet-level features. Sensitivities at 3000 fb\(^{-1}\) are quoted using the Asimov significance, \( S / \sqrt{S + B} \), and an Asimov variant with a 20\% background systematic.
  The model parameters \( g^* \) and \( R_L \) are defined in Sec.~2, and a single global working point is used to avoid per-mass tuning bias. In the \( (g^*, m_T) \) scan, we present 2\(σ\) exclusion and 5\(σ\) discovery contours for \( R_L = 0 \) and \( R_L = 0.5 \). For \( R_L = 0 \), 2\(σ\) exclusion corresponds to \( g^* \in [0.17, 0.49] \) (\( 0.16, 0.43 \)) over \( m_T \in [1.8, 2.7] \) TeV, while 5\(σ\) discovery corresponds to \( g^* \in [0.27, 0.44] \) (\( 0.26, 0.40 \)) over \( m_T \in [1.8, 2.2] \) TeV for XGBoost and GNN respectively.
  For \( R_L = 0.5 \), the 2\(σ\) reach is \( g^* \in [0.21, 0.48] \) (\( 0.20, 0.43 \)) over \( m_T \in [1.8, 2.5] \) TeV, and the 5\(σ\) reach is \( g^* \in [0.33, 0.43] \) (\( 0.31, 0.49 \)) over \( m_T \in [1.8, 2.2] \) TeV, with the GNN yielding slightly stronger and smoother limits across the scan.

</details>

### [55] [Spacetime triple wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02611)
*Vincent Herr,Aimé Fournier,Andrew J. S Hamilton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper constructs an exact, non-vacuum, intra-universe 3-neck spacetime wormhole solution to Einstein's field equations by spherically inverting a 3-torus, yielding a trivariate Dupin hypercyclide geometry with only six nonzero Riemann tensor components and regions of positive energy density along traversable geodesics.
\n\n
本文通过球面反演一个三环面（3-torus），构造了一个精确的、非真空的、宇宙内三颈时空虫洞解，满足爱因斯坦场方程。所得几何为三变量Dupin hypercyclide，其黎曼张量仅有六个非零分量，并且存在穿越各虫洞颈的测地线路径仅经历正能量密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the long-standing question raised by Einstein and Rosen (1935) regarding the existence of multi-neck wormhole solutions in general relativity. Standard constructions often rely on thin-shell approximations, spherical symmetry, or coordinate patching, which limit physical realism and mathematical elegance. The authors aim to construct a globally defined, non-spherically symmetric, non-thin-shell, intra-universe multi-neck wormhole without artificial junction conditions.
\n\n
动机源于爱因斯坦和罗森（1935）提出的关于广义相对论中是否存在多颈虫洞解的长期问题。标准构造通常依赖薄壳近似、球对称性或坐标拼接，这限制了物理真实性和数学优雅性。作者旨在构建一个全局定义的、非球对称的、非薄壳的、宇宙内多颈虫洞，且无需人为连接条件。

Method: The method involves spherically inverting an equal-radii 3-torus to obtain a trivariate Dupin hypercyclide as the spatial manifold. This surface is embedded in a synchronous reference frame, providing global coordinates where the metric tensor remains simple and injective without patching. The resulting metric is then verified as an exact solution to Einstein's field equations $G_{\\mu\\nu} = 8\\pi T_{\\mu\\nu}$, yielding diagonal Ricci and stress-energy tensors. The curvature properties are simplified further using a global isothermal coordinate system admitted by the asymptotically flat, unbounded geometry.
\n\n
方法包括对等半径三环面进行球面反演，以获得三变量Dupin hypercyclide作为空间流形。该曲面被嵌入同步参考系中，提供无需拼接的全局坐标，其中度规张量保持简单且单射。所得度规随后被验证为爱因斯坦场方程 $G_{\\mu\\nu} = 8\\pi T_{\\mu\\nu}$ 的精确解，产生对角化的Ricci张量和应力-能量张量。该渐近平坦且无界的几何结构还允许全局等温坐标系，进一步简化曲率张量。

Result: The construction yields an exact, non-vacuum solution to Einstein’s equations describing a 3-neck intra-universe wormhole. The Riemann curvature tensor has only six nonzero components, and both Ricci and stress-energy tensors are diagonal. Although negative energy density is present overall (as required to keep wormhole throats open), there exist geodesic paths through each neck that traverse only regions of positive energy density. The spatial geometry is globally described by a Dupin hypercyclide and admits an isothermal coordinate system.
\n\n
该构造得到了一个描述三颈宇宙内虫洞的爱因斯坦方程的精确非真空解。黎曼曲率张量仅有六个非零分量，且Ricci张量与应力-能量张量均为对角形式。尽管整体存在负能量密度（维持虫洞喉部开放所需），但存在穿越每个虫洞颈的测地线路径，仅经过正能量密度区域。空间几何由Dupin hypercyclide全局描述，并允许等温坐标系。

Conclusion: This work affirms the existence of multi-neck wormhole solutions in general relativity beyond idealized symmetries or thin-shell approximations. By leveraging geometric inversion and Dupin cyclide theory, it provides a globally smooth, analytically tractable spacetime with physically interesting features—such as traversable geodesics through positive-energy regions—thus extending the theoretical landscape of exact wormhole solutions and addressing Einstein and Rosen’s original query.
\n\n
本工作证实了在广义相对论中，超越理想化对称性或薄壳近似的多颈虫洞解确实存在。通过利用几何反演和Dupin cyclide理论，提供了一个全局光滑、解析可处理的时空结构，具有物理上有趣的特性——例如穿越正能量区域的可穿越测地线——从而拓展了精确虫洞解的理论图景，并回应了爱因斯坦与罗森最初的疑问。

Abstract: We describe a multi-neck spacetime wormhole with a simple metric tensor and a simple injective map without coordinate patching. An intra-universe, non-thin-shell, non-spherically-symmetric 3-neck spacetime wormhole is geometrically constructed by spherically inverting a 3-torus. We place the resulting Dupin hypercyclide in a synchronous reference frame. The three necks are arranged around a central point and satisfy topological and geometric spacetime wormhole definitions. Asserting this metric tensor as an exact solution of Einstein's field equations in global coordinates generates diagonal Ricci and stress-energy tensors, and a Riemann curvature tensor with only six nonzero entries. The local inertial frame at every point of the coordinate system is comoving with the triple wormhole. This non-vacuum solution answers affirmatively the question posed by Einstein and Rosen (1935) of whether or not multi-neck solutions exist. The wormhole solution contains negative energy density as is expected to hold the necks open; however, geodesic paths through each neck exist which encounter only positive energy density. The spatial manifold is a trivariate Dupin hypercyclide. The spherically inverted equal-radii 3-torus is unbounded, asymptotically flat and admits a global isothermal coordinate system that further simplifies the curvature tensors.

</details>


### [56] [MCMC Constraints on Dyonic Kalb-Ramond Black Holes with a Cloud of Strings from Twin-Peak QPOs and EHT Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02654)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates a dyonic black hole solution in Lorentz-violating gravity with a background Kalb–Ramond field and a cloud of strings. It analyzes timelike circular geodesics to derive quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) frequencies, fits these to observational data from three X-ray binaries using MCMC to constrain parameters $(\ell, ξ)$, computes thermodynamic quantities including the first law and Smarr relation, and studies radiative properties such as Hawking radiation sparsity and shadow radius. The string density $ξ$ is found to have the strongest influence on key observables like the ISCO, shadow size, and emission sparsity.\n\n
本文研究了在洛伦兹破缺引力中带有背景Kalb–Ramond场和宇宙弦云的dyonic黑洞解。通过分析类时圆轨道测地线导出准周期振荡（QPO）频率，并利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法将这些频率与三个X射线双星（XTE J1550−564、GRO J1655−40 和 GRS 1915+105）的观测数据拟合，从而对参数$(\ell, ξ)$施加限制。文章还计算了包括热力学第一定律和Smarr关系在内的完整热力学量，并研究了霍金辐射稀疏性及黑洞阴影半径等辐射性质。结果表明，宇宙弦密度$ξ$对最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、阴影尺寸和辐射稀疏性等关键观测量具有最强影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to explore black hole solutions in modified gravity theories that incorporate Lorentz symmetry violation, antisymmetric tensor fields (Kalb–Ramond), and topological defects like cosmic strings. 作者从一个由Kalb–Ramond场驱动并被宇宙弦云穿透的洛伦兹破缺引力中的dyonic黑洞度规出发，其特征参数为$(M, Q, p, \ell, ξ)$。他们计算类时圆轨道测地线，在相对论进动模型和外差共振模型下得到轨道频率$ν_φ$及径向/垂直振荡频率$ν_r$、$ν_θ$。随后，利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法将这些理论频率与XTE J1550−564、GRO J1655−40和GRS 1915+105的双峰QPO观测数据拟合，以约束参数$(\ell, ξ)$。热力学分析包括推导熵、温度、电/磁势，并验证热力学第一定律$dM = T dS + \\Phi_e dQ + \\Phi_m dp + \\mathcal{V}_\\ell d\\ell + \\mathcal{V}_ξ dξ$和Smarr关系。此外，他们还计算了霍金辐射的谱能量发射率、光子球半径和阴影半径对所有五个参数的依赖关系。

Method: The authors adopt an exponential $f(R)$ gravity model, which modifies the Einstein–Hilbert action via a function $f(R) = R - b R_c (1 - e^{-R/R_c})$, introducing a new parameter $b$. The analysis jointly constrains $b$ and standard $\\Lambda$CDM parameters, focusing on derived parameters $H_0$ and $S_8 = \\sigma_8 \\sqrt{\\Omega_m / 0.3}$.

Conclusion: While the exponential $f(R)$ gravity model does not simultaneously resolve the $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions, it provides a modest improvement in describing structure formation, particularly alleviating the $S_8$ tension at the $\\sim 1.2\\sigma$ level. Abstract: One of the most popular competitors to the CDM paradigm as an explanation for the late-time acceleration of the universe is the modification of general relativity (GR), with models such as $f(R)$ gravity among the main motivations. In contrast, the model predicts systematically higher values of $S_8$, leading to a moderate alleviation of the $S_8$ tension by up to $\sim 1.2σ$ when late-time datasets are included. Overall, these results demonstrate that although the considered $f(R)$ gravity model does not resolve all cosmological tensions simultaneously, it provides a consistent improvement in the description of large-scale structure formation.

Motivation: The motivation stems from testing alternative theories of gravity—specifically Ricci-Determinant gravity—against compact astrophysical objects like quark stars. 作者采用适用于Ricci-Determinant gravity的相对论恒星结构方程，假设静态球对称时空和相互作用的夸克物质EoS。他们数值求解修正的Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff（TOV）方程，将扩展引力模型中的自由参数视为对GR的微扰。通过绝热指数和中心密度阈值评估稳定性，并计算致密性与结合能以检验普适性。

主要结果包括：(1) Ricci-Determinant gravity中的夸克星比GR中更不致密；(2) 引力结合能与质量之比不再遵循假设的普适行为；(3) 当微扰修正项超过GR贡献约一半时（即 $|\delta R|/R_{\\text{GR}} \\gtrsim 0.5$），系统失稳，从而限制了可达到的最大中心密度。

Conclusion: Ricci-Determinant gravity introduces significant departures from GR for quark stars, challenging both compactness expectations and binding-energy universality. Abstract: In the present study, we explore the fundamental properties of static, spherically symmetric quark stars composed of quark matter with an interacting quark equation of state (EoS) within the framework of Ricci-Determinant gravity. Our primary objective is to investigate deviations from General Relativity (GR) in key physical characteristics, particularly the mass--radius relation and stability criteria, arising from the free parameters of this extended gravitational theory. We see that, unlike the hadronic case, the model predicts a reduction in the compactness of quark stars. This parameter is also sensitive to gravitational binding-energy analysis, revealing a breakdown of the assumed universality. Furthermore, the formation of objects with high central densities is restricted by the instability conditions that arise when the contribution of perturbative terms exceeds by approximately half the contribution of ordinary GR, indicating a clear limitation in the theory.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurate and fast waveform models are essential for reliable gravitational-wave inference of long-duration BNS signals, as slow or inaccurate models can bias measurements of nuclear matter properties. Existing time-domain models like SEOBNRv5THM are highly accurate but computationally prohibitive for parameter estimation over many cycles. The authors aim to bridge this gap by constructing a frequency-domain surrogate that retains physical fidelity near merger—where tidal effects dominate—while achieving the speed needed for practical data analysis.

精确且快速的波形模型对于可靠地从长时间双中子星（BNS）引力波信号中推断核物质性质至关重要，因为低效或不准确的模型可能导致对核物态方程的错误推断。现有的时域模型（如 SEOBNRv5THM）虽高度精确，但在多周期参数估计中计算成本过高。作者旨在构建一个频域替代模型，在并合附近（潮汐效应主导区域）保持物理保真度，同时满足实际数据分析所需的计算效率。

Method: The authors develop a hybrid frequency-domain waveform: they apply the stationary-phase approximation (SPA) to model the early inspiral analytically, and use a mode-by-mode fast Fourier transform (FFT) of the time-domain SEOBNRv5THM output for the late inspiral, merger, and post-merger phases. The two regimes are smoothly stitched together. They further accelerate likelihood evaluation using multibanding and relative binning techniques in parameter estimation. Validation is performed via mismatch calculations and Bayesian inference on both synthetic and real LIGO/Virgo data.
\n\n
作者开发了一种混合频域波形：对早期旋近阶段采用平稳相位近似（SPA）进行解析建模，而对晚期旋近、并合及并合后阶段，则对时域 SEOBNRv5THM 输出进行逐模态快速傅里叶变换（FFT）。两个区域被平滑拼接。此外，他们在参数估计中结合多带（multibanding）和相对分箱（relative binning）技术以加速似然计算。通过失配度计算以及在合成数据和真实 LIGO/Virgo 数据上的贝叶斯推断验证了模型性能。

Result: The hybrid frequency-domain SEOBNRv5THM waveform achieves mismatches below $10^{-3}$ relative to the original time-domain model across the relevant parameter space. Parameter estimation on simulated and real BNS events shows negligible systematic biases, while computational runtime is reduced from weeks to days. The method enables faithful inference of tidal deformability and other nuclear physics parameters without sacrificing accuracy near merger.
\n\n
该混合频域 SEOBNRv5THM 波形在整个相关参数空间内相对于原始时域模型的失配度低于 $10^{-3}$。在模拟和真实BNS事件上的参数估计显示系统偏差可忽略不计，而计算运行时间从数周缩短至数天。该方法能够在不牺牲并合附近精度的前提下，忠实推断潮汐形变率及其他核物理参数。

Conclusion: The proposed hybrid frequency-domain approach successfully balances computational efficiency and physical accuracy for BNS waveform modeling, making high-fidelity Bayesian inference feasible for long signals within realistic runtimes. The framework is generalizable to binary black hole systems and will be crucial for upcoming observing runs (e.g., O4/O5) and next-generation ground-based detectors where BNS detections will be abundant.

Abstract: Gravitational-wave inference for long signals, like those from binary neutron-star (BNS) systems, requires waveform models that are both physically faithful and computationally efficient, otherwise, one risks drawing incorrect conclusions about nuclear matter from observations. To address this challenge, we present a frequency-domain implementation of the accurate SEOBNRv5THM waveform model for quasi-circular, spin-aligned BNS systems within the effective-one-body framework. Our approach combines the stationary-phase approximation (SPA) for the early inspiral with a fast Fourier transform treatment of the late- and post-inspiral regime, applied mode-by-mode. Our hybrid approach retains the efficiency of the SPA without affecting the waveform accuracy close to merger, where matter effects are most significant. The resulting waveform's generation speed can be further decreased using modern parameter-estimation techniques, such as multibanding and relative binning. We demonstrate excellent agreement with the baseline SEOBNRv5THM model in both mismatches and when analyzing real and synthetic data, and show how waveform systematics could affect BNS detections in upcoming observational runs and new facilities on the ground. We find that our method significantly reduces computational costs, enabling faithful parameter estimation for BNS signals within practical runtimes of order days. Our procedure can be readily extended to coalescing binary black hole systems.

For scalar field matter, the authors fully characterize the solution space and boundary charges, clarify the proper identification of Bondi mass and angular momentum aspects, define covariant news and aspects via vacuum orbits under $\text{BMS}_3^k$, link these to Cotton scalars (3D analogues of Weyl scalars), and construct the first exactly conserved non-linear Newman-Penrose charges in 3D gravity.

本文引入三维渐近FLRW时空作为研究宇宙学中渐近对称性和辐射的简化框架，其渐近对称群为$\text{BMS}_3^k$——一种由物质状态方程参数$k$控制的$\text{BMS}_3$单参数形变。针对标量场物质源，作者完整刻画了解空间与边界荷，并首次明确指出正确识别度规中的Bondi质量与角动量分量需细致分析（此前即使在四维文献中亦未阐明）。当存在超旋转时，模型展现出与四维“广义BMS”情形类似的微妙性，因此需要协变地定义news。作者通过研究真空结构（即真空解在有限$\text{BMS}_3^k$变换下的轨道）给出了news、质量与角动量分量的协变定义，并应用Wald-Zoupas方法构造电荷。此外，这些协变量自然出现在Cotton标量（Weyl标量的三维类比）中，最终借此构建了首个在三维引力中精确守恒的非线性Newman-Penrose荷。

  <summary>Details</summary>

This setup allows for a complete characterization of the phase space and charges, which is often obstructed by technical complexity in four dimensions. The work is inspired by the four-dimensional construction of Bonga and Prabhu and aims to resolve ambiguities in defining fundamental quantities like Bondi mass and angular momentum aspects in cosmological settings.

动机在于构建一个可处理的低维模型——三维渐近FLRW时空——用于分析膨胀宇宙中的渐近对称性（将BMS对称性推广至宇宙学情形）和引力辐射。该设定允许完整刻画相空间与守恒荷，而这在四维中常因技术复杂性而受阻。本工作受Bonga与Prabhu在四维中的构造启发，旨在解决宇宙学背景下Bondi质量与角动量分量等基本量定义中的模糊性。

Method: The authors consider three-dimensional gravity coupled to a scalar field with an equation of state parameter $k$, leading to asymptotically-FLRW boundary conditions. They derive the asymptotic symmetry group $\text{BMS}_3^k$ as a one-parameter deformation of $\text{BMS}_3$. By solving the field equations asymptotically, they fully characterize the solution space. Covariant definitions of news, mass, and angular momentum aspects are obtained by analyzing the orbits of the vacuum solution under finite $\text{BMS}_3^k$ transformations. Boundary charges are constructed using the Wald-Zoupas prescription. The relation between these geometric quantities and the Cotton tensor (the 3D analogue of the Weyl tensor) is explored, revealing that the covariant aspects appear naturally in the Cotton scalars. Finally, non-linear Newman-Penrose charges are built from these structures and shown to be exactly conserved.

作者考虑具有状态方程参数$k$的标量场耦合的三维引力，导出渐近FLRW边界条件，并由此得到渐近对称群$\text{BMS}_3^k$——$\text{BMS}_3$的单参数形变。通过对场方程进行渐近求解，完整刻画了解空间。通过分析真空解在有限$\text{BMS}_3^k$变换下的轨道，获得news、质量与角动量分量的协变定义。利用Wald-Zoupas方法构造边界荷。进一步探索这些几何量与Cotton张量（Weyl张量的三维类比）的关系，发现协变分量自然出现在Cotton标量中。最后，基于这些结构构建非线性Newman-Penrose荷，并证明其精确守恒。

Result: The paper establishes that the asymptotic symmetry group of 3D asymptotically-FLRW spacetimes is $\text{BMS}_3^k$, dependent on the matter equation of state. For scalar field matter, the full solution space and associated boundary charges are explicitly characterized. A careful analysis yields the correct identification of Bondi mass and angular momentum aspects, resolving prior ambiguities. Covariant news and aspects are defined via vacuum orbits under $\text{BMS}_3^k$, and these quantities are shown to appear in the Cotton scalars. Using this framework, the authors construct the first example of exactly conserved non-linear Newman-Penrose charges in three-dimensional gravity.

论文确立了三维渐近FLRW时空的渐近对称群为$\text{BMS}_3^k$，其依赖于物质状态方程。对标量场物质情形，完整显式刻画了解空间及相应的边界荷。通过细致分析，正确识别了Bondi质量与角动量分量，解决了此前存在的模糊性。借助$\text{BMS}_3^k$下真空轨道，定义了协变的news与分量，并证明这些量自然出现在Cotton标量中。基于此框架，作者构建了首个在三维引力中精确守恒的非线性Newman-Penrose荷。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that three-dimensional asymptotically-FLRW spacetimes provide a powerful and solvable arena for studying cosmological asymptotic symmetries and radiation memory effects. It clarifies foundational aspects of charge definitions in time-dependent backgrounds, introduces covariant geometric structures linked to the Cotton tensor, and achieves the first exact conservation law for non-linear Newman-Penrose charges in 3D gravity, opening avenues for further exploration of infrared structures in cosmological settings.
\n\n
本工作表明，三维渐近FLRW时空为研究宇宙学渐近对称性与辐射记忆效应提供了一个强大且可解的平台。它澄清了时变背景中守恒荷定义的基础问题，引入了与Cotton张量关联的协变几何结构，并首次在三维引力中实现了非线性Newman-Penrose荷的精确守恒律，为未来探索宇宙学背景下的红外结构开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce three-dimensional asymptotically-FLRW spacetimes as a simplified setting in which to study asymptotic symmetries and radiation in cosmology. Their asymptotic symmetry group is $\text{BMS}_3^k$, a one-parameter deformation of $\text{BMS}_3$ controlled by the matter equation of state with parameter $k$, in line with the four-dimensional construction of Bonga and Prabhu. We analyze in detail the case of a scalar field matter source, which allows us to fully characterize the solution space and the boundary charges. In particular, we point out that the proper identification of the Bondi mass and angular momentum aspects in the metric requires a careful analysis which had not been laid out so far, even in the existing four-dimensional literature. When superrotations are present, the model exhibits subtleties similar to those appearing when dealing with ''generalized BMS'' asymptotic symmetries in the four-dimensional case, and this requires a covariant definition of the news. We identify covariant notions of news, as well as of mass and angular momentum aspects by studying the vacuum structure, namely the orbits of the vacuum solution under finite $\text{BMS}_3^k$ transformations, and study the Wald-Zoupas prescription for the charges. We also show that these covariant aspects naturally appear in the Cotton scalars, which are the three-dimensional analogues of the Weyl scalars. Finally, we use these quantities to provide a first example of exactly conserved non-linear Newman-Penrose charges in three-dimensional gravity.

*Simone Antonini,Antonino Marciano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper shows that Einstein's equations arise as the reversible limit of a stochastic spacetime dynamics, where entropy production—defined via forward and time-reversed metric trajectories—vanishes precisely on-shell.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to deepen the thermodynamic interpretation of gravity by embedding it in a non-equilibrium stochastic framework, going beyond Jacobson’s equilibrium-based derivation of Einstein’s equations from the Clausius relation. 动机在于将引力的热力学诠释嵌入一个非平衡随机框架中，超越Jacobson基于克劳修斯关系从平衡态导出爱因斯坦方程的方法，旨在阐明经典广义相对论与热力学第二定律如何共同从一个更基本的统计几何描述中涌现。

Method: The authors introduce a stochastic geometric flow for the spacetime metric $g_{\mu\nu}$, inspired by non-equilibrium statistical mechanics. Entropy production is defined as the logarithmic ratio of probabilities of forward versus time-reversed metric trajectories: $\Sigma = \log \\frac{\\mathcal{P}[g]}{\\mathcal{P}[\\tilde{g}]}$. They compute this quantity using path-integral or Langevin-type formulations and relate it to spacetime curvature and matter stress-energy via a generalized fluctuation-dissipation structure.
\n\n
作者引入一个受非平衡统计力学启发的时空度规 $g_{\\mu\\nu}$ 的随机几何流。熵产生被定义为正向与时间反演度规轨迹概率的对数比：$\\Sigma = \\log \\frac{\\mathcal{P}[g]}{\\mathcal{P}[\\tilde{g}]}$。他们利用路径积分或Langevin型表述计算该量，并通过一种广义涨落-耗散结构将其与时空曲率和物质应力-能量张量联系起来。

Result: The entropy production rate splits into geometric and matter contributions: $\Sigma = \\int d^4x \\sqrt{-g} \\left( G_{\\mu\\nu} - 8\\pi G T_{\\mu\\nu} \\right) V^{\\mu\\nu}$, where $V^{\\mu\\nu}$ encodes the noise correlation structure. Vanishing entropy production ($\\Sigma = 0$) enforces $G_{\\mu\\nu} = 8\\pi G T_{\\mu\\nu}$, i.e., the Einstein field equations. Non-zero $\Sigma$ corresponds to irreversible, non-equilibrium geometro-dynamics obeying the second law.
\n\n
熵产生率分解为几何与物质贡献：$\\Sigma = \\int d^4x \\sqrt{-g} \\left( G_{\\mu\\nu} - 8\\pi G T_{\\mu\\nu} \\right) V^{\\mu\\nu}$，其中 $V^{\\mu\\nu}$ 编码噪声关联结构。熵产生为零（$\\Sigma = 0$）即强制满足 $G_{\\mu\\nu} = 8\\pi G T_{\\mu\\nu}$，亦即爱因斯坦场方程。非零 $\\Sigma$ 对应于服从热力学第二定律的不可逆、非平衡几何动力学。

Conclusion: Classical general relativity is recovered as the zero-entropy-production (reversible) sector of a broader stochastic theory of spacetime. The second law of thermodynamics is not imposed but naturally arises from the statistical irreversibility of the underlying geometric fluctuations, offering a unified origin for both gravitational dynamics and thermodynamic arrow of time.

经典广义相对论被恢复为更广泛的时空随机理论中熵产生为零（可逆）的子集。热力学第二定律并非人为施加，而是自然地源于底层几何涨落的统计不可逆性，从而为引力动力学与热力学时间箭头提供了统一的起源。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between gravitational dynamics and the second law of thermodynamics in a non-equilibrium framework. Extending Jacobson's thermodynamic derivation of the Einstein equations, we introduce a stochastic geometric flow for the spacetime metric and define entropy production as the ratio between forward and time-reversed trajectories. We show that entropy production is governed by curvature and matter contributions, and that its vanishing selects configurations satisfying the Einstein field equations. Classical general relativity thus emerges as the reversible limit of an underlying stochastic geometro-dynamics, while the second law arises from its non-equilibrium evolution.

Result: The analysis reveals that the field equations of quadratic $f(R)$ gravity require $R = -4\\Lambda$ (constant) for any Kerr--Schild--AdS geometry, which forces the solution to coincide exactly with the Kerr--AdS family of General Relativity. No additional rotating black hole solutions arise from the higher-curvature correction term.

分析表明，对于任意Kerr--Schild--AdS几何，二次$f(R)$引力的场方程要求$R = -4\\Lambda$（常数），迫使解精确地与广义相对论中的Kerr--AdS族一致。高曲率修正项并未产生额外的旋转黑洞解。

Conclusion: Quadratic $f(R)$ gravity does not support new Kerr--AdS black hole solutions beyond those of Einstein gravity. Within the Kerr--Schild--AdS class, the theory dynamically enforces constant curvature, leading to a no-go theorem for non-Einsteinian rotating black holes in this framework.

二次$f(R)$引力在爱因斯坦引力之外不支持新的Kerr--AdS黑洞解。在Kerr--Schild--AdS类中，该理论动态地强制曲率为常数，从而在此框架下对非爱因斯坦类旋转黑洞得出了一个no-go定理。

Abstract: We investigate Kerr--Schild--AdS geometries in quadratic $f(R)$ gravity without imposing the constant-curvature condition $R=R_0$ a priori. For the geometrically natural Kerr--Schild--AdS subclass, we show that the field equations dynamically enforce constant scalar curvature and uniquely select the Kerr--AdS family of solutions. Thus, quadratic $f(R)$ gravity admits no Kerr--AdS black hole solutions beyond the Einstein branch, establishing a no-go theorem for this class of geometries.

</details>

The gravitational self-energy scales as $E^{\rm GSE}\sim 4G(\hbar\omega)^2/(c^4 L)\ln(d^2/l_0^2)$, suppressed by the photon wavelength $L$, and vanishes for coincident back-to-back pairs. 该论文通过在弦论启发的长度尺度 $l_0$ 上对点源进行平滑处理，正则化了Aichelburg-Sexl冲击波度规，得到一个光滑几何，被识别为零喉Einstein-Rosen虫洞。这为纠缠光子提供了ER=EPR猜想的几何实现。引力自能标度为 $E^{\rm GSE}\sim 4G(\hbar\omega)^2/(c^4 L)\ln(d^2/l_0^2)$，受光子波长 $L$ 抑制，且对背对背共点光子对为零。利用有效纠缠温度 $k_B T_{\rm ent}\sim\hbar c/(2\pi L)$ 的纠缠熵计算复现了该能量；对于光学光子，其引力诱导波函数坍缩时间超过 $10^{30}$ 年，表明超相对论量子的量子引力效应被极度压制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to resolve the singularity in the Aichelburg-Sexl metric for massless particles and to construct an explicit, non-singular geometric model that realizes the ER=EPR conjecture—linking quantum entanglement (EPR) with spacetime geometry (Einstein-Rosen bridges). Additionally, the authors aim to quantify gravitational self-interaction effects for ultra-relativistic quanta and assess their relevance to wave-function collapse models.

动机在于消除无质量粒子Aichelburg-Sexl度规中的奇点，并构建一个显式的、无奇点的几何模型以实现ER=EPR猜想——将量子纠缠（EPR）与时空几何（Einstein-Rosen桥）联系起来。此外，作者旨在量化超相对论量子的引力自相互作用效应，并评估其在波函数坍缩模型中的相关性。

Method: The authors smear the delta-function source of the Aichelburg-Sexl metric over a transverse length scale $l_0$, inspired by string theory, to obtain a regularized shock-wave geometry. They perform a coordinate transformation to reveal the transverse spatial slice as a zero-throat Einstein-Rosen wormhole. The gravitational self-energy is computed from the regularized metric, yielding a logarithmic dependence on separation $d$ and inverse dependence on longitudinal extent $L$. Separately, they compute the entanglement entropy of null intervals in this geometry and introduce an effective entanglement temperature $k_B T_{\rm ent} \sim \hbar c / (2\pi L)$ to relate entropy to energy via thermodynamic relations.
\n\n
作者受弦论启发，在横向长度尺度 $l_0$ 上对Aichelburg-Sexl度规的delta函数源进行平滑处理，获得正则化的冲击波几何。通过坐标变换，他们揭示横向空间切片为零喉Einstein-Rosen虫洞。引力自能从正则化度规中计算得出，表现出对分离距离 $d$ 的对数依赖和对纵向尺度 $L$ 的反比依赖。此外，他们在该几何中计算类时间隔的纠缠熵，并引入有效纠缠温度 $k_B T_{\rm ent} \sim \hbar c / (2\pi L)$，通过热力学关系将熵与能量关联。

Result: The regularized geometry is a smooth, traversable zero-throat Einstein-Rosen wormhole connecting two entangled photons. The gravitational self-energy is $E^{\rm GSE} \sim \frac{4G(\hbar\omega)^2}{c^4 L} \ln\left(\frac{d^2}{l_0^2}\right)$, which vanishes when $d = 0$ (coincident pair) and grows logarithmically with separation. The same expression is recovered via entanglement entropy using $S = E^{\rm GSE}/T_{\rm ent}$. For optical photons ($\omega \sim$ eV), the associated gravitational collapse time exceeds $10^{30}$ years, implying negligible quantum-gravity-induced decoherence.

正则化后的几何是一个光滑、可穿越的零喉Einstein-Rosen虫洞，连接两个纠缠光子。引力自能为 $E^{\rm GSE} \sim \frac{4G(\hbar\omega)^2}{c^4 L} \ln\left(\frac{d^2}{l_0^2}\right)$，当 $d = 0$（共点光子对）时为零，并随分离距离对数增长。通过纠缠熵利用关系 $S = E^{\rm GSE}/T_{\rm ent}$ 可复现相同表达式。对于光学光子（$\omega \sim$ eV），相应的引力坍缩时间超过 $10^{30}$ 年，意味着量子引力诱导的退相干可忽略不计。

Conclusion: This work provides the first explicit, singularity-free geometric realization of ER=EPR for massless entangled particles, linking transverse wormhole geometry to longitudinal quantum coherence. It demonstrates that gravitational self-interaction for ultra-relativistic quanta is suppressed by their wavelength, rendering quantum-gravity effects negligible for realistic photons. The equivalence between gravitational self-energy and entanglement thermodynamics reinforces the deep connection between geometry and entanglement in quantum gravity.

本工作首次为无质量纠缠粒子提供了显式、无奇点的ER=EPR几何实现，将横向虫洞几何与纵向量子相干性联系起来。研究表明，超相对论量子的引力自相互作用受其波长抑制，使得实际光子的量子引力效应可忽略不计。引力自能与纠缠热力学的等价性进一步强化了量子引力中几何与纠缠之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We regularize the Aichelburg-Sexl shock-wave metric for massless particles by smearing the point-like source over a string-inspired length scale $l_0$, obtaining a singularity-free gravitational potential. A coordinate transformation reveals that the transverse geometry is a zero-throat Einstein-Rosen wormhole, providing an explicit geometric realization of the ER=EPR conjecture for entangled photons. Crucially, we show that the gravitational self-energy depends on the photon's longitudinal extent $L$ (its wavelength) and, for a transversely separated photon pair, is suppressed by a factor $1/L$, giving $E^{\rm GSE}\sim 4G(\hbarω)^2/(c^4 L)\ln(d^2/l_0^2)$. For the coincident back-to-back pair created in $e^{+} e^{-}\to2γ$, the wormhole carries no additional binding energy; the logarithmic interaction energy emerges only after the entangled photons separate to a distance $d$, stretching the ER bridge. We further provide an entanglement-entropy interpretation: by computing the entanglement entropy of null intervals in the shock-wave geometry and introducing an effective entanglement temperature $k_B T_{\rm ent}\sim\hbar c/(2πL)$, we recover the same scaling and normalization of the gravitational self-energy. For optical photons the corresponding collapse time exceeds $10^{30}$ years, making isolated photons immune to gravity-induced wave-function collapse. These findings establish a rigorous playground for testing ER=EPR and reveal a deep suppression of quantum-gravity effects for ultra-relativistic quanta.

### [64] [Off-shell Hessian thermodynamic stability of higher-curvature black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03045)
*Chen-Hao Hao,Jieci Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper introduces a branch-sensitive thermodynamic formalism for higher-curvature black holes based on the off-shell Gibbs free energy $G_{\rm off}$ and Wald entropy $S_W$. It shows that local stability is determined by the Hessian $H = S'_W(r_h) T'(r_h)$, not just the temperature slope. This framework recovers standard results in special cases (e.g., five-dimensional quasi-topological gravity), explains universal critical exponents via an $A_3$ cusp normal form, and clarifies why the usual stability criterion holds only on physical, ghost-free branches in Lovelock gravity.
