本文提出了一种基于非壳层 Gibbs 自由能 $G_{\rm off}$ 和 Wald 熵 $S_W$ 的、对分支敏感的高阶曲率黑洞热力学框架。研究表明，局域稳定性由 Hessian 量 $H = S'_W(r_h) T'(r_h)$ 决定，而非仅由温度斜率决定。该框架在特定情形（如五维准拓扑引力）下复现标准结果，通过 $A_3$ 尖点正规形解释普适临界指数，并阐明为何在 Lovelock 引力中，常规稳定性判据仅在物理上可接受的无鬼且分支正则的解上成立。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard black hole thermodynamics often assumes that local stability is dictated solely by the sign of the temperature derivative with respect to horizon radius, i.e., $T'(r_h) > 0$. However, this rule fails in higher-curvature theories where multiple solution branches exist and Wald entropy may not be monotonic. The authors aim to construct a more robust, branch-aware thermodynamic framework that correctly captures stability and critical behavior across all physically admissible black hole solutions in such theories.

Near critical points, they analyze the catastrophe-theoretic normal form (specifically the $A_3$ cusp) derived from the off-shell structure to extract scaling exponents. (1) 高阶曲率黑洞的局域热力学稳定性由 $H = S'_W(r_h) T'(r_h)$ 决定，而非仅由 $T'(r_h)$ 决定。(2) 在五维准拓扑引力中，物理分支上的 $S_W$ 保持单调，因此传统斜率规则作为推论成立。(3) 非壳层框架在临界点附近导出 $A_3$ 尖点正规形，预言平均场分支分离指数为 $1/2$，该指数也支配如 Lyapunov 指数等光滑可观测量的标度行为。(4) 在 Lovelock 引力中，$S'_W$ 可在非平面或病态分支上变号，导致稳定性判据反转，但在无鬼且分支正则的外部区域保持正值——从而保证标准规则在物理分支上依然有效。

Conclusion: The off-shell thermodynamic formalism based on $G_{\rm off}$ and $S_W$ provides a unified, branch-sensitive criterion for black hole stability in higher-curvature gravity. Abstract: We develop a branch-sensitive thermodynamic framework for higher-curvature black holes using the off-shell Gibbs free energy $G_{\rm off}$ and the Wald entropy$S_W$ as the basic data. On fixed-parameter slices, equilibrium black holes are stationary points of $G_{\rm off}$, and their local stability is governed by the Hessian $H=S'_W(r_h)T'(r_h)$, rather than by the temperature slope alone. Method: The authors derive the geodesic equations for charged particles in the background of a Schwarzschild-like metric sourced by a Kalb-Ramond field, incorporating the Lorentz force from a uniform external magnetic field $\\mathcal{B}$. They compute the effective potential and epicyclic frequencies ($\\nu_r$, $\\nu_\\theta$) governing radial and vertical oscillations. Using observed QPO frequencies from GRO 1655-40, XTE 1550-564, and GRS 1915+105, they perform Monte Carlo Markov Chain (MCMC) parameter estimation to constrain $l$ and $\\mathcal{B}$, comparing models via Akaike (AIC) and Bayesian (BIC) information criteria.

Result: The pure Schwarzschild model is statistically disfavored for all three microquasars. For GRO 1655-40 and XTE 1550-564, joint inclusion of the magnetic field ($\\mathcal{B}\\sim 0.03$–$0.04$) and Lorentz violation ($l\\sim 0.08$–$0.10$) yields optimal fits. For the more massive GRS 1915+105, the KR-induced Lorentz violation alone provides a perfect fit ($ΔAIC = ΔBIC = 0$). Conclusion: The combined framework of Kalb-Ramond-induced Lorentz violation and external magnetic fields provides a statistically robust and physically motivated explanation for QPOs in microquasars. Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles around a Schwarzschild-like black hole sourced by a Kalb-Ramond field and immersed in a uniform external magnetic field. The Kalb-Ramond field introduces a Lorentz violation parameter $l$ that modifies the spacetime geometry, while the magnetic field profoundly influences the trajectories through the Lorentz force, leading to a rich variety of orbital behaviours including curled (epicyclic) motion and dramatic transitions between distinct energy boundary configurations. The pure Schwarzschild model is statistically ruled out for all three sources. For GRO 1655-40 and XTE 1550-564, only the combined effect of the magnetic field and Lorentz violation yields statistically robust models, with best-fit values $\mathcal{B}\sim 0.03$-$0.04$ and $l\sim 0.08$--$0.10$. Remarkably, for GRS 1915+105, the most massive object in our sample, the Lorentz violation parameter alone is sufficient to model the QPO frequencies, yielding an optimal fit with $ΔAIC = ΔBIC = 0$. Across all three objects, a clear trend emerges: the required value of $l$ decreases as the mass of the astrophysical object increases, suggesting a mass-dependent scaling of the Kalb-Ramond parameter. These findings establish the combined KR and magnetic field framework as a viable and statistically robust scenario for modeling QPOs in microquasars, and indicate that Lorentz-violating modifications to gravity may leave observable imprints in the strong-field regime, offering new avenues for testing quantum-gravity phenomenology with current and next-generation X-ray missions.

Rigorous asymptotic analysis as $L \\to \\infty$ yields $O(L^{-2})$ bounds on the imaginary parts of the mode frequencies, corresponding to localization estimates.

Result: The paper proves rigorous $O(L^{-2})$ localization estimates for the quasinormal mode frequencies in this compactly supported model. This quantifies how tightly the modes are confined near the effective cavity formed between the potential barrier and the inner Robin wall, confirming the expected scaling from heuristic echo models but now with explicit normalization and mathematical control.

该研究表明，即使在高度理想化但数学上严格的设定中，标准回声腔图像也能产生可定量预测的模式局域化。这为未来关于奇异致密天体（exotic compact objects）的理论工作提供了基准，并阐明了边界条件在修改黑洞铃宕（ringdown）信号中的作用。

Abstract: Echo models phenomenologically encode possible near-horizon structure by replacing the purely ingoing horizon-side condition with an effective reflecting inner boundary near the would-be horizon. We study this idea in a controlled transfer-function model consisting of a compactly supported one-dimensional barrier and a Robin wall at a large negative tortoise coordinate. The aim is not to propose a new echo mechanism or to make an observational claim, but to analyze the standard cavity denominator in a benchmark model with explicit normalizations. All rigorous $O(L^{-2})$ localization estimates are proved for this compactly supported model.

  <summary>Details</summary>

动机在于探索黑洞阴影——强引力区域中的可观测特征——是否能够编码关于相变的热力学信息，特别是在包含弦云和quintessence等奇异物质的复杂环境中。作者旨在将shadow thermodynamics推广到更复杂的黑洞系统，并检验在暗能量与拓扑缺陷影响下相变结构的普适性与鲁棒性。

Using this relation, they reformulate standard thermodynamic quantities (e.g., temperature, pressure, Gibbs free energy) in terms of $R_s$, thereby constructing a shadow-based thermodynamic framework. Traditional thermodynamic geometry (e.g., Ricci scalar of Ruppeiner metric) is employed to identify phase structures. Energy emission rates $\frac{d^2 E(\omega)}{d\omega dt}$ for both massless and massive particles are derived from the absorption cross-section approximated by the shadow area.
\n\n
作者计算了受弦云和quintessence场修正的四维Reissner–Nordström–AdS黑洞的光子球与阴影半径 $R_s$，并建立了 $R_s$ 与事件视界半径 $r_h$ 的一一对应关系。利用该映射，他们将温度、压强、Gibbs自由能等标准热力学量用 $R_s$ 表示，从而构建了基于阴影的热力学框架。通过传统的热力学几何方法（如Ruppeiner度规的Ricci标量）识别相结构。同时，基于阴影面积近似吸收截面，推导了无质量与有质量粒子的能量辐射率 $\frac{d^2 E(\omega)}{d\omega dt}$。

We present the energy emission rates for massless and massive particles and discover that the maximum emission frequency can also serve as a useful tool for thermodynamic analysis. We firstly study and systematically establish shadow thermodynamics under the background of string cloud and quintessence, and our results reveal the independent regulatory mechanism of dark components on phase transitions as well as the universal topological invariance of the phase transition structure.

### [68] [Model-Independent Search Discards Faint Lensed-Pairs of Gravitational Wave Events in the Sub-Threshold Candidates of GWTC-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03346)
*Aniruddha Chakraborty,Suvodip Mukherjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper presents the first model-independent search for gravitationally lensed gravitational wave (GW) events using the GLANCE cross-correlation method on the full GWTC-4 dataset, including both confident detections ("super-events") and sub-threshold candidates ("sub-events"). Despite examining ~11,000 event pairs and identifying four pairs with ≥2σ significance, no pair exceeded 3σ in both LIGO detectors, leading to a null detection.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gravitational lensing of GWs can produce multiple images with time delays, magnifications, and phase shifts. A model-independent approach avoids assumptions about lens or source models, making it more robust for initial discovery efforts.

引力波的引力透镜效应可产生具有时间延迟、放大因子和相位偏移的多重像。虽然透镜GW事件较为稀有（约千分之几），但引入次阈值事件可显著增加候选事件总数，从而提升探测透镜效应的可能性。采用模型无关的方法可避免对透镜或源模型的假设，使初步搜寻更为稳健。

Method: The authors apply the GLANCE (Gravitational Lensing Analysis via Normalized Cross-correlation Estimator) algorithm—a model-independent, cross-correlation-based technique—to the full strain data of GWTC-4. They construct ∼90 super-events and ∼800 sub-events, forming ∼11,000 event pairs. For promising pairs, they perform spectrogram checks for data quality, assess sky-map overlap, and conduct Bayesian parameter estimation on sub-events to validate lensing hypotheses.
\n\n
作者将GLANCE（Gravitational Lensing Analysis via Normalized Cross-correlation Estimator）算法——一种模型无关的交叉相关技术——应用于GWTC-4的完整应变数据。他们构建了约90个super-events和约800个sub-events，形成约11,000个事件对。对于有希望的事件对，他们进行频谱图检查以评估数据质量、检验天区定位重叠，并对sub-events进行贝叶斯参数估计以验证透镜假设。

Result: Four event pairs showed cross-correlation significance ≥2σ, but none reached ≥3σ simultaneously in both LIGO-Hanford and LIGO-Livingston detectors. Consequently, no statistically significant lensed GW event—especially among sub-threshold candidates—is detected in GWTC-4. This null result yields an upper bound on the lensing rate of ≤1.5 per year.
\n\n
四对事件显示出≥2σ的交叉相关显著性，但没有一对在LIGO-Hanford和LIGO-Livingston两个探测器中同时达到≥3σ。因此，在GWTC-4中未发现具有统计显著性的透镜GW事件（尤其是次阈值候选事件）。该零结果将透镜事件发生率的上限设为≤1.5/年。

Conclusion: While no lensed GW events are detected in GWTC-4—even when including sub-threshold candidates—the methodology demonstrates the feasibility of deep, noise-dominated searches using cross-correlation and Bayesian validation. Future observing runs with increased exposure time may enable the first detection of lensed GWs with current-generation detectors.
\n\n
尽管在GWTC-4中（即使包含次阈值候选事件）未探测到透镜GW事件，但该方法展示了利用交叉相关和贝叶斯验证在噪声主导区域进行深度搜索的可行性。未来随着观测时间的增加，当前一代探测器有望实现透镜GW的首次探测。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing of gravitational waves (GWs) can produce multiple images in the geometric optics limit. These lensed GW images arrive at different times, are amplified by different magnification factors, and are shifted by constant phases. With current understanding, the occurrence of lensed events stands at a few per thousand events, and the number of GW detections is a few hundred with the ground-based detector network. However, with the inclusion of the sub-threshold events, the total number of detections crosses a few thousand. Therefore, a search that includes both types of events yields a higher chance of lensing detection. In this work, we carry out the first model-independent lensing search using a cross-correlation-based technique GLANCE over the entire volume of the GWTC-4 strain data, containing $\sim 90$ super-events $\sim 800$ sub-events forming a total of $\sim 11,000$ event pairs with a higher False Alarm Rate (FAR) event rate allowing to search deep in the noise dominated regime. We further conduct their spectrogram checks to inspect data quality, sky-map overlap of the interesting pairs, and a Bayesian parameter exploration of the sub-event to make a robust lensing detection. Although the search indicated four pairs of potential events with cross-correlation significance $\geq 2σ$, none were above $3σ$ at both the LIGO-Hanford and LIGO-Livingston detectors. This makes it possible to strongly rule out the presence of any statistically significant sub-threshold lensed GW event in GWTC-4. The null detection translates to an upper bound on the lensing detection rate to be $\leq$ 1.5/yr with inclusion of the sub-threshold event candidates. In the future, with more observation time, the detection of lensed GW can be possible from the current generation of GW detectors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Horndeski's scalar-tensor theory is the most general single-field scalar-tensor theory with second-order equations of motion, making it a natural setting for exploring modified gravity and inflation. However, generic solutions often suffer from pathologies like ghost or gradient (Laplace) instabilities. The motivation is to systematically construct viable cosmological models—specifically inflationary ones—that are free of such pathologies by reverse-engineering the theory from a desired background evolution.
\n\n
Horndeski标量-张量理论是具有二阶运动方程的最一般的单场标量-张量理论，因此是探索修改引力和暴胀的自然框架。然而，一般的解通常存在鬼场或梯度（Laplace）不稳定性等病态问题。本文的动机是通过从期望的背景演化反向构造理论，系统地构建无此类病态的可行宇宙学模型（特别是暴胀模型）。

Method: The authors employ a designer approach: they start by prescribing a specific scale factor $a(t)$ corresponding to exponential or power-law inflation in a flat FRW universe. They then solve the Friedmann equations derived from Horndeski's action to determine the functional forms of the Horndeski Lagrangian functions $G_i(\phi, X)$ (where $X = -\\frac{1}{2}\\partial_\\mu\\phi\\partial^\\mu\\phi$) that yield this prescribed background evolution. Finally, they impose the conditions for the absence of ghost and Laplace instabilities on the resulting solution to constrain the allowed subclasses of the theory.
\n\n
作者采用一种设计者方法：首先设定平坦FRW宇宙中对应于指数或幂律暴胀的特定尺度因子$a(t)$；然后求解由Horndeski作用量导出的Friedmann方程，以确定能产生该背景演化的Horndeski拉格朗日函数$G_i(\\phi, X)$（其中$X = -\\frac{1}{2}\\partial_\\mu\\phi\\partial^\\mu\\phi$）的具体函数形式；最后，对所得解施加无鬼场和Laplace不稳定性的条件，以约束理论的允许子类。

Result: The paper successfully identifies specific subclasses of Horndeski's theory—defined by particular relations among the $G_i(\phi, X)$ functions—that admit stable, pathology-free exponential and power-law inflationary solutions in flat FRW spacetime. These solutions satisfy both the background equations and the linear stability conditions against ghost and Laplace instabilities.
\n\n
本文成功识别出Horndeski理论的特定子类——由$G_i(\\phi, X)$函数之间的特定关系所定义——这些子类在平坦FRW时空中允许稳定的、无病态的指数和幂律暴胀解。这些解同时满足背景方程以及针对鬼场和Laplace不稳定性的线性稳定性条件。

Conclusion: The designer method provides a powerful and systematic framework for constructing viable cosmological models within the broad landscape of Horndeski gravity. By starting from a desired cosmic history and working backward, one can efficiently pinpoint healthy theoretical sectors that would be difficult to find through forward-model building. This approach validates specific inflationary scenarios as consistent within a well-defined subclass of Horndeski's theory.

设计者方法为在Horndeski引力广阔的理论空间中构建可行的宇宙学模型提供了一个强大而系统的框架。通过从期望的宇宙演化历史出发进行逆向推导，可以高效地定位健康的理论区域，而这些区域通过正向模型构建难以发现。该方法验证了特定的暴胀情景在Horndeski理论的一个明确定义的子类中是一致的。

Abstract: We present a designer method for flat Friedman-Robertson-Walker space-times within the framework of Horndeski's scalar-tensor theory. As a result, one can find subclasses of Horndeski's scalar-tensor theory within which cosmological solutions exist without ghosts and Laplace instabilities. As an example, we found subclasses of Horndeski's theory for exponential and power-law inflation models of the Universe without pathologies.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding whether an observer can survive the plunge into a rotating (Kerr) black hole is crucial for both theoretical investigations of spacetime structure near singularities and potential observational implications in astrophysics. 理解观测者能否在落入旋转（Kerr）黑洞的过程中幸存，对于理论研究奇点附近的时空结构以及天体物理中的潜在观测意义至关重要。Kerr几何中的环状奇点相较于Schwarzschild黑洞带来了独特的挑战，这促使人们沿着极轴等安全轨迹对潮汐效应进行详细研究。

They evaluate the proper-time integrated tidal forces and derive a critical black hole mass $M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$—dependent on the dimensionless spin parameter $a$—above which tidal disruption does not occur before crossing the event horizon.

作者计算了Kerr时空中一般类时测地线的潮汐张量分量，并特别考虑了沿极轴（$\\theta = \\pi/2$）的轨迹，在该轨迹上观测者与位于$r=0$的环状奇点保持最大距离。他们评估了固有时积分的潮汐力，并推导出一个临界黑洞质量$M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$——其依赖于无量纲自旋参数$a$——当黑洞质量超过该值时，观测者在穿越事件视界前不会被潮汐撕裂。

Result: For any given spin $a \\in [0, M]$, there exists a minimum mass $M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$ such that observers falling along the polar axis experience sub-lethal tidal forces if $M > M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$. This critical mass increases with spin, but even for maximal spin ($a = M$), $M_{\\text{crit}} \\sim 10^5\\,M_\\odot$, well below typical supermassive black hole masses ($\\gtrsim 10^6\\,M_\\odot$). In contrast, stellar-mass black holes ($M \\sim 10\\,M_\\odot$) always lie below this threshold and would tidally disrupt infalling observers.
\n\n
对于任意给定的自旋$a \\in [0, M]$，存在一个最小质量$M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$，使得沿极轴下落的观测者在$M > M_{\\text{crit}}(a)$时经历的潮汐力低于致死水平。该临界质量随自旋增大而增加，但即使在最大自旋（$a = M$）情况下，$M_{\\text{crit}} \\sim 10^5\\,M_\\odot$，远低于典型超大质量黑洞的质量（$\\gtrsim 10^6\\,M_\\odot$）。相比之下，恒星级黑洞（$M \\sim 10\\,M_\\odot$）始终低于此阈值，会将下落的观测者潮汐撕裂。

Conclusion: Safe passage through the interior of a Kerr black hole is theoretically possible for sufficiently massive black holes, particularly supermassive ones, when following polar trajectories that avoid the ring singularity. This result underscores the qualitative difference between supermassive and stellar-mass black holes regarding their tidal environments and has implications for thought experiments involving black hole interiors and cosmic censorship.

Abstract: We investigate the fate of an observer falling towards a Kerr black hole. The tidal forces are computed for arbitrary trajectories of an observer, and we specify them along the polar axis in order to remain as far as possible from the ring-shaped singularity. Our analysis shows that an observer is not tidally disrupted during the fall provided that the black hole mass exceeds a critical value, which depends on its spin. In practice, any supermassive black hole represents a suitable candidate to allow an observer to traverse the black hole without severe deformation. In contrast, stellar-mass rotating black holes do not satisfy the mass condition and are expected to subject the observer to extreme tidal forces leading to its destruction during the plunge.

### [71] [Quantum Matter Makes Lightcones Quantum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03671)
*Tomohiro Fujita,Misao Sasaki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that when spacetime's causal structure is sourced by quantum matter—specifically a non-relativistic quantum particle generating a Newtonian potential—the resulting light cones become operator-valued due to a quantum Shapiro delay. This leads to three key effects: (i) causal boundaries become noncommuting observables with intrinsic quantum uncertainty, (ii) fixed spacetime points can exist in superpositions of timelike and spacelike separations, and (iii) tracing out the quantum source smooths the Wightman function’s light-cone singularity, introducing an effective UV cutoff. Crucially, this quantization of causal structure occurs without requiring quantized gravitons.
\n\n
本文表明，当时空的因果结构由量子物质（具体为产生牛顿势的非相对论性量子粒子）决定时，光锥因量子Shapiro延迟而成为算符值。这导致三个关键效应：(i) 因果边界变为不可对易的可观测量，具有内在的量子不确定性；(ii) 固定时空点之间的因果关系可处于类时与类空间隔的叠加态；(iii) 对量子源做迹运算会平滑Wightman函数在光锥上的奇异性，从而引入一个有效的紫外截断。关键在于，这种因果结构的量子化无需引力子的量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the foundational question in quantum gravity: how does quantum matter influence the causal structure of spacetime? In classical general relativity, matter determines the metric and hence the light cones, but when matter is in a quantum state, it is unclear whether and how causality becomes quantum. The authors aim to explore this in a controlled, low-energy regime where full quantum gravity is not required, yet quantum features of causal structure emerge explicitly.
\n\n
动机源于量子引力中的一个基础问题：量子物质如何影响时空的因果结构？在经典广义相对论中，物质决定度规，从而决定光锥；但当物质处于量子态时，因果性是否以及如何变得量子化尚不清楚。作者旨在一个可控的低能框架下探索这一问题——该框架无需完整的量子引力理论，却能明确展现出因果结构的量子特征。

Method: The authors consider a massless scalar field propagating on a background spacetime whose metric includes a Newtonian gravitational potential $\\Phi = -Gm/|\\vec{x} - \\hat{\\vec{X}}|$, where $\\hat{\\vec{X}}$ is the position operator of a non-relativistic quantum particle of mass $m$. This makes the metric—and thus the light cones—operator-valued. They compute the retarded and Wightman propagators of the scalar field, treating the gravitational potential as a perturbation. The quantum nature of the source induces an operator-valued Shapiro time delay $\\hat{\\Delta t} \\sim Gm/|\\vec{x}|$, which modifies the causal propagation. By analyzing commutators and two-point functions, they extract consequences for quantum causality.
\n\n
作者考虑一个无质量标量场在背景时空上传播，该时空的度规包含牛顿引力势 $\\Phi = -Gm/|\\vec{x} - \\hat{\\vec{X}}|$，其中 $\\hat{\\vec{X}}$ 是质量为 $m$ 的非相对论性量子粒子的位置算符。这使得度规（从而光锥）成为算符值。他们将引力势视为微扰，计算标量场的推迟传播子和Wightman传播子。源的量子性诱导出算符值的Shapiro时间延迟 $\\hat{\\Delta t} \\sim Gm/|\\vec{x}|$，从而修正因果传播。通过分析对易子和两点函数，他们提取出量子因果性的若干后果。

Result: Three main results are derived: (i) The causal boundary—normally a sharp hypersurface—is promoted to a quantum observable represented by a noncommuting operator, implying an irreducible uncertainty in causal relations; (ii) For two fixed spacetime points, the causal relation becomes indefinite, existing in a quantum superposition of timelike and spacelike configurations; (iii) When the quantum source is traced out, the Wightman function’s light-cone singularity is smeared over a region of size $\\sim (Gm)^{2/3}$, effectively acting as a UV regulator without modifying the underlying field theory.
\n\n
得出三个主要结果：(i) 因果边界（通常是一个尖锐的超曲面）被提升为由不可对易算符表示的量子可观测量，意味着因果关系存在不可消除的不确定性；(ii) 对于两个固定的时空点，其因果关系变得不确定，处于类时与类空间隔的量子叠加态；(iii) 当对量子源做迹运算后，Wightman函数在光锥上的奇异性被抹平，抹平尺度约为 $\\sim (Gm)^{2/3}$，有效起到紫外截断的作用，而无需修改底层场论。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that quantum matter alone—without quantizing the gravitational field—can render spacetime’s causal structure fundamentally quantum. This challenges the notion that quantum gravity effects require Planck-scale physics or graviton quantization, showing instead that even in low-energy, semi-classical settings, quantum sources induce nontrivial quantum features in causality, such as operator-valued light cones, causal superpositions, and UV regularization via entanglement with the source.

本文得出结论：仅靠量子物质（无需对引力场进行量子化）即可使时空的因果结构本质上成为量子的。这挑战了“量子引力效应必须依赖普朗克尺度物理或引力子量子化”的传统观点，表明即使在低能、半经典框架下，量子源也能通过与源的纠缠诱导出非平凡的量子因果特征，例如算符值光锥、因果叠加态，以及通过纠缠实现的紫外正则化。

Abstract: In gravitational physics, matter does not merely move within spacetime; it also determines the light cones that define causal relations. What happens when the matter that determines these light cones is itself in a quantum state? We address this question in a controlled low-energy setting: a massless scalar field propagating in the spacetime with the Newtonian gravitational potential sourced by a non-relativistic quantum particle. We show that the light cones are affected by an operator-valued Shapiro delay, with the three consequences: (i) causal-boundary shifts are promoted to noncommuting observables, giving the causal structure an irreducible quantum uncertainty; (ii) the causal relation between two fixed spacetime points can become a superposition of timelike and spacelike configurations; and (iii) tracing out the source smears the Wightman light-cone singularity, producing an effective UV cutoff. Thus, quantum matter does not merely fluctuate within spacetime; it makes the causal structure itself quantum, even without quantized gravitons.

</details>


### [72] [Critical collapse of a self-interacting scalar field in asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03751)
*Li-Jie Xin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates type II critical gravitational collapse of a spherically symmetric massless scalar field in asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime with a potential $V \propto 1/\ell^2$. Despite varying the AdS curvature radius $\ell$, the echoing period ($\Delta \approx 3.4$) and critical exponent ($\gamma \approx 0.37$) remain consistent with Choptuik’s original results in asymptotically flat spacetime, indicating universal critical behavior insensitive to the AdS scale or the specific form of this potential.
\n\n
本文研究了在渐近反德西特（AdS）时空中，具有势能 $V \propto 1/\ell^2$ 的球对称无质量标量场的II型临界引力坍缩。尽管改变AdS曲率半径 $\ell$，回声周期（$\Delta \approx 3.4$）和临界指数（$\gamma \approx 0.37$）仍与Choptuik在渐近平直时空中的经典结果高度一致，表明临界行为具有普适性，对AdS尺度或该势能形式不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to test the universality of Choptuik's critical phenomena—specifically the echoing period $\Delta$ and critical exponent $\gamma$—in asymptotically AdS spacetimes, where boundary conditions differ significantly from asymptotically flat cases. The authors aim to determine whether introducing an AdS-scale-dependent potential alters the well-established type II critical collapse behavior.
\n\n
动机在于检验Choptuik临界现象（特别是回声周期 $\Delta$ 和临界指数 $\gamma$）在渐近AdS时空中的普适性，其中边界条件与渐近平直情形显著不同。作者旨在确定引入依赖于AdS尺度的势能是否会改变已确立的II型临界坍缩行为。

Method: The authors perform numerical simulations of spherically symmetric gravitational collapse of a massless scalar field with potential $V = \text{const.}/\ell^2$ in asymptotically AdS spacetime. They use two independent coordinate systems—polar coordinates and double null coordinates—to evolve distinct families of initial data and fine-tune the scalar field amplitude near the critical threshold. The critical exponent $\gamma$ and echoing period $\Delta$ are extracted via standard scaling and discrete self-similarity analyses.
\n\n
作者对具有势能 $V = \text{const.}/\ell^2$ 的球对称无质量标量场在渐近AdS时空中的引力坍缩进行数值模拟。他们采用两种独立坐标系——极坐标和双零坐标——来演化不同的初始数据族，并在临界阈值附近对标量场振幅进行精细调节。通过标准的标度律分析和离散自相似性分析提取临界指数 $\gamma$ 和回声周期 $\Delta$。

Result: For multiple values of the AdS radius $\ell$ (including $\ell=8$), both coordinate systems yield consistent results: the critical exponent remains $\gamma \approx 0.37$ and the echoing period $\Delta \approx 3.4$, matching Choptuik’s original findings in asymptotically flat spacetime. This demonstrates that the $1/\ell^2$ potential does not qualitatively affect type II critical collapse.
\n\n
对于多个AdS半径 $\ell$（包括 $\ell=8$），两种坐标系均给出一致结果：临界指数保持为 $\gamma \approx 0.37$，回声周期 $\Delta \approx 3.4$，与Choptuik在渐近平直时空中的原始发现相符。这表明 $1/\ell^2$ 势能在定性上不影响II型临界坍缩。

Conclusion: The critical behavior of type II gravitational collapse is robust and universal, persisting unchanged in asymptotically AdS spacetimes even when a curvature-scale-dependent potential is introduced. The echoing period and critical exponent are insensitive to the AdS length scale $\ell$, reinforcing the idea that Choptuik scaling is a fundamental feature of general relativity near the black hole threshold.
\n\n
II型引力坍缩的临界行为具有鲁棒性和普适性，即使在引入依赖曲率尺度的势能后，其在渐近AdS时空中依然保持不变。回声周期和临界指数对AdS长度尺度 $\ell$ 不敏感，进一步支持了Choptuik标度律是广义相对论在黑洞形成阈值附近的基本特征这一观点。

Abstract: We study the critical gravitational collapse of a spherically symmetric massless scalar field in asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime. The scalar field potential adopted here is inversely proportional to the square of the AdS curvature radius $\ell$, and the system admits a well-known exact static solution. Working in polar coordinates, we first confirm that type II critical collapse occurs for a range of distinct initial configurations when $\ell=8$, where the measured echoing period and critical exponent are in excellent agreement with Choptuik's classic results. We then fine-tune the initial amplitude of the scalar field for a series of AdS radii $\ell$, performing calculations in both polar coordinates and double null coordinates to cross-validate our results. We find that the form of the potential does not alter the critical behavior of gravitational collapse in any meaningful way: in particular, both the echoing period ($Δ\approx 3.4$) and critical exponent ($γ\approx 0.37$) remain essentially unchanged across all tested values of $\ell$.

</details>

Abstract: We show that the remaining diagonal Einstein equations in the Carter-projective sector of stationary axisymmetric spacetimes are equivalent to a single sourced master equation.   =16πΣ\left( T_{\hat0\hat0}+T_{\hat3\hat3} \right), \] where \(Δ(r)\) and \(Y(x)\) are the radial and angular structure functions. The reduction is accompanied by two geometric diagonal identities of the Einstein tensor, which become algebraic compatibility conditions on admissible matter sources. In the homogeneous limit, the vacuum--\(Λ\) Kerr--Carter and Plebański--Demiański families are recovered as solutions of the same master operator. We also show the projective covariance of the construction and discuss compatible sources, including the aligned Maxwell field and separable anisotropic examples.

A sixth-order WKB approximation is employed near the peak of the effective potential as an independent local check. Hawking spectra are then constructed using these greybody factors combined with thermal distributions based on different temperature prescriptions: one tied to the black hole event horizon and another based on an effective static-patch temperature.

作者将黑洞视界与宇宙学视界之间的静态 patch 建模为双向散射区域。他们通过对中性标量场和无质量 Dirac 场的径向 Teukolsky 型方程进行直接数值积分，求得透射系数（即 greybody 因子）。同时，在有效势垒顶部附近采用六阶 WKB 近似作为独立的局域验证。随后，结合这些 greybody 因子与基于不同温度约定的热分布（一种关联黑洞事件视界，另一种基于有效静态 patch 温度）构建 Hawking 谱。

Result: Increasing the Euler–Heisenberg coupling parameter strengthens the effective potential barriers for both scalar and Dirac fields, shifting the half-transmission frequency $\\omega_{1/2}$ to higher values. For fixed charge and coupling, raising the cosmological constant $\\Lambda$ shrinks the static patch and reduces the threshold frequencies for greybody emission. The total luminosity $L \\propto \\int d\\omega\\, \\omega\\, |T(\\omega)|^2 n(\\omega)$ exhibits strong dependence on the temperature choice: event-horizon-based temperatures yield increasing luminosity with stronger nonlinear corrections, while static-patch temperatures produce suppressed and even decreasing emission rates. This demonstrates that the evaporation rate is not invariant under temperature redefinition in $\\Lambda > 0$ spacetimes.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that Hawking evaporation in black holes with $\\Lambda > 0$ and nonlinear electromagnetic corrections is highly sensitive to the adopted temperature convention. Greybody factors alone do not determine observable emission; the thermal distribution's temperature prescription—whether anchored to the event horizon or the static patch—plays an equally critical role. Abstract: We compute neutral scalar and neutral massless Dirac greybody factors and Hawking spectra for the positive-cosmological-constant branch of the Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg black hole. The finite region between the black-hole and cosmological horizons is treated as a two-sided scattering problem, with direct numerical integrations providing the transmission coefficients and a sixth-order WKB calculation used as a local check near the barrier top. Along the reference family studied here, increasing the Euler--Heisenberg coupling raises the dominant scalar and Dirac barriers and shifts the half-transmission frequencies upward. At fixed charge and nonlinear coupling, increasing the cosmological constant contracts the static patch and lowers the dominant greybody thresholds. The luminosity is controlled as much by the de Sitter temperature prescription as by the greybody factors: event-horizon prescriptions brighten the emission as the nonlinear correction grows, whereas effective static-patch temperatures give much smaller rates and can reverse the trend. Thus the evaporation interpretation is not unique unless the temperature convention is specified explicitly.

Result: The constructed theories produce singularity-free black hole spacetimes with vanishing curvature and force at large $r$. Generic solutions exhibit two horizons, and a subclass ensures geodesic completeness through the origin. A minimum allowed black hole mass emerges as a prediction.

所构建的理论产生了无奇点的黑洞时空，在大$r$处曲率和力消失。一般解表现出双视界结构，一个子类确保通过原点的测地线完备性。理论预言了黑洞的最小允许质量。

Abstract: Generalizing the gravitational potential first proposed in [1] we derive a large class of relativistic singularity free theories of gravity, which reduce to flat spacetime at large distances. We verify that for the chosen gravitational potential the force and the space-time curvature resolve the singularity and vanish at large distances. We show that those singularity free black hole solutions generically have a two horizon structure. Furthermore, we show that there is a subclass of potentials which produce a geometries geodesically complete through the origin. We discuss the implications of the effects resulting from such theories and show that black holes solutions are predicted to have minimum allowed mass.

### [76] [Constraints on the gravitational potential from DESI DR2 BAO and its implications for the local void scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02712)
*Indranil Banik,José Antonio Nájera,Harry Desmond*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The paper investigates whether a local void—motivated by galaxy number counts and the Hubble tension—can explain cosmological observations via a gravitational redshift offset $z_0$. Using high-redshift datasets (BBN, CMB, BAO, CC), the authors constrain $z_0 = -0.4 \pm 0.9\%$ ($z > 0.5$) or $0.0^{+0.6}_{-0.7}\%$ ($z > 0.29$), consistent with zero but not excluding the void-motivated value of $0.84\%$. While $\Lambda$CDM shows a $2.81\sigma$ BAO anomaly in $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$, fixing $z_0 = 0.84\%$ reduces it to $2.39\sigma$, suggesting such a void remains viable.
\n\n
本文研究了一个由星系计数和哈勃张力所启发的局部空洞（local void）是否能通过引力红移偏移量 $z_0$ 解释宇宙学观测。作者利用高红移数据集（BBN、CMB、BAO、CC），限制出 $z_0 = -0.4 \pm 0.9\%$（$z > 0.5$）或 $0.0^{+0.6}_{-0.7}\%$（$z > 0.29$），结果与零一致，但并未排除空洞模型所预期的 $0.84\%$ 值。虽然 $\Lambda$CDM 在 $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$ 参数空间中存在 $2.81\sigma$ 的 BAO 异常，但固定 $z_0 = 0.84\%$ 可将其降低至 $2.39\sigma$，表明足以解决哈勃张力的局部空洞仍不能被高红移数据排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the Hubble tension—the discrepancy between early- and late-Universe measurements of $H_0$—and observational hints from galaxy number counts suggesting we reside in a local underdensity (void). If true, this void would induce a gravitational redshift (GR) contribution $z_0$ due to our location on a potential hill relative to distant sources at $z \gtrsim 0.3$. This effect could mimic a higher local $H_0$, potentially resolving the tension. Previous work estimated $z_0 \approx 0.84\%$ from void models fitted to local data, but its consistency with higher-redshift cosmological probes had not been fully assessed, especially given its subtle impacts like altering the CMB temperature and sound horizon $r_{\rm d}$.
\n\n
动机源于哈勃张力——即早期与晚期宇宙对 $H_0$ 测量之间的不一致性——以及来自星系计数的观测线索，暗示我们可能位于一个局部低密度区（空洞）。若属实，该空洞会因我们相对于 $z \gtrsim 0.3$ 处遥远源处于引力势“山丘”上而产生一个引力红移（GR）贡献 $z_0$。这一效应可能模拟出更高的局域 $H_0$，从而缓解张力。先前工作基于拟合局域数据的空洞模型估计 $z_0 \approx 0.84\%$，但其与高红移宇宙学探针的一致性尚未被充分评估，尤其是考虑到其微妙影响，如改变 CMB 温度和声视界 $r_{\rm d}$。

Method: The authors construct a modified cosmological model incorporating a constant gravitational redshift offset $z_0$, which effectively rescales observed redshifts as $1 + z_{\rm obs} = (1 + z_{\rm true})(1 + z_0)$. They analyze combined datasets including Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN), Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB), Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO), and Cosmic Chronometers (CC) at $z > 0.5$, later extending to $z > 0.29$. The analysis accounts for secondary effects of $z_0$: a hotter CMB (since $T_{\rm CMB} \propto 1 + z_0$) and a reduced sound horizon at drag epoch $r_{\rm d}$ due to altered expansion history. They perform likelihood analyses in the $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$ parameter space and compute $\Delta \chi^2$ relative to $\Lambda$CDM to assess viability of $z_0 = 0.84\%$.
\n\n
作者构建了一个包含恒定引力红移偏移 $z_0$ 的修正宇宙学模型，该模型将观测红移有效重标度为 $1 + z_{\rm obs} = (1 + z_{\rm true})(1 + z_0)$。他们分析了 $z > 0.5$ 的联合数据集，包括大爆炸核合成（BBN）、宇宙微波背景（CMB）、重子声学振荡（BAO）和宇宙计时器（CC），随后扩展至 $z > 0.29$。分析考虑了 $z_0$ 的次级效应：更热的 CMB（因 $T_{\rm CMB} \propto 1 + z_0$）以及由于膨胀历史改变而导致拖曳时期的声视界 $r_{\rm d}$ 减小。他们在 $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$ 参数空间中进行似然分析，并计算相对于 $\Lambda$CDM 的 $\Delta \chi^2$ 以评估 $z_0 = 0.84\%$ 模型的可行性。

Result: The joint analysis yields $z_0 = -0.4 \pm 0.9\%$ for $z > 0.5$, consistent with zero, and $z_0 = 0.0^{+0.6}_{-0.7}\%$ when including data down to $z > 0.29$. The standard $\Lambda$CDM model exhibits a $2.81\sigma$ tension between BAO and non-BAO probes in the $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$ plane. Imposing $z_0 = 0.84\%$ reduces this anomaly to $2.39\sigma$. Although the best-fit favors $z_0 = 0$, the model with $z_0 = 0.84\%$ fits the data nearly as well as $\Lambda$CDM, with $\Delta \chi^2 < 2$, indicating it cannot be ruled out by current high-redshift data despite its non-standard effects on CMB temperature and $r_{\rm d}$.
\n\n
联合分析得出：对于 $z > 0.5$，$z_0 = -0.4 \pm 0.9\%$，与零一致；当包含 $z > 0.29$ 的数据时，$z_0 = 0.0^{+0.6}_{-0.7}\%$。标准 $\Lambda$CDM 模型在 $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, \Omega_{\rm m})$ 平面上表现出 BAO 与非 BAO 探针之间 $2.81\sigma$ 的张力。施加 $z_0 = 0.84\%$ 后，该异常降至 $2.39\sigma$。尽管最佳拟合倾向于 $z_0 = 0$，但 $z_0 = 0.84\%$ 的模型与数据的拟合程度几乎与 $\Lambda$CDM 相当（$\Delta \chi^2 < 2$），表明尽管其对 CMB 温度和 $r_{\rm d}$ 有非标准影响，当前高红移数据仍无法排除该模型。

Conclusion: A local void large enough to resolve the Hubble tension via a gravitational redshift offset of $z_0 = 0.84\%$ remains consistent with existing high-redshift cosmological data. Although the data prefer $z_0 = 0$, the alternative model is only marginally disfavored ($\Delta \chi^2 < 2$) and alleviates a significant BAO anomaly in $\Lambda$CDM. Thus, such a void scenario cannot be excluded and warrants further investigation with improved datasets and more detailed modeling of its relativistic and nonlinear effects.
\n\n
一个通过引力红移偏移 $z_0 = 0.84\%$ 足以解决哈勃张力的局部空洞，仍与现有高红移宇宙学数据一致。尽管数据更倾向于 $z_0 = 0$，但替代表模型仅被轻微排除（$\Delta \chi^2 < 2$），并缓解了 $\Lambda$CDM 中显著的 BAO 异常。因此，此类空洞情景尚不能被排除，值得借助更优数据集和更精细的相对论及非线性效应建模进一步研究。

Abstract: We constrain the difference in gravitational potential between our location and sources at $z \gtrsim 0.3$ using datasets at those redshifts. Our motivation is that the Hubble tension might be caused by a local void, as suggested by galaxy number counts. This would increase the redshift through outflow and gravitational redshift (GR). Only the latter is important at high redshift, where a void contributes a fixed additional GR contribution of $z_0$ due to our location on a potential hill. This $z_0$ model has various subtle effects that were not previously considered, including a hotter CMB and reduced BAO $r_{\rm d}$. We test whether $z_0$ can have the previously expected value of 0.84\%, which was based on fitting void parameters to galaxy number counts and local $H_0$ measurements. Combining BBN, CMB, BAO, and CC datasets at $z > 0.5$, we find that $z_0$ = $-0.4 \pm 0.9\%$, which rises to $0.0^{+0.6}_{-0.7}\%$ when extending our analysis down to $z > 0.29$. Although the results prefer the standard value of $z_0 = 0$, the best-fitting model with $z_0 = 0.84\%$ fits the data almost as well as $Λ$CDM, with $Δχ^2 < 2$. We find that $Λ$CDM faces a $2.81σ$ BAO anomaly in the standard $(H_0 r_{\rm d}, Ω_{\rm m})$ parameter space, where different regions are preferred by BAO and non-BAO datasets from $z > 0.29$. Fixing $z_0 = 0.84\%$ reduces this to $2.39σ$. This suggests that a local void large enough to solve the Hubble tension cannot be ruled out by higher-redshift datasets despite its novel impact on them.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Observed X-ray scaling relations in galaxy clusters systematically deviate from the predictions of the self-similar model, especially in low-mass systems. These discrepancies suggest that non-gravitational physics (e.g., feedback, cooling) plays a significant role. The motivation is to develop a physically interpretable, minimal-parameter framework that captures these deviations efficiently and improves the predictive power of scaling relations for cosmological and astrophysical applications.
\n\n
观测到的星系团X射线标度关系系统性地偏离自相似模型的预测，尤其在低质量系统中更为显著。这些偏差表明非引力物理过程（如反馈、冷却）起着重要作用。本文旨在构建一个物理上可解释、参数最少的框架，高效刻画这些偏离，从而提升标度关系在宇宙学和天体物理应用中的预测能力。

Method: The author models departures from self-similarity using two dimensionless physical quantities: the gas mass fraction $f_g \sim T^{f_1} E_z^{f_z}$ and the temperature variation $f_T \sim T^{t_1} E_z^{t_z}$, where $T$ is the X-ray temperature and $E_z = H(z)/H_0$. A Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) meta-analysis is performed on a large compilation of published X-ray scaling relations to jointly constrain the exponents $\{f_1, f_z, t_1, t_z\}$. The calibrated model is then used to predict various scaling laws and assess tension levels compared to the standard self-similar model.
\n\n
作者使用两个无量纲物理量建模对自相似性的偏离：气体质量分数$f_g \sim T^{f_1} E_z^{f_z}$和温度变化$f_T \sim T^{t_1} E_z^{t_z}$，其中$T$为X射线温度，$E_z = H(z)/H_0$。通过对大量已发表的X射线标度关系进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）元分析，联合约束指数参数$\{f_1, f_z, t_1, t_z\}$。校准后的模型用于预测多种标度律，并与标准自相似模型对比评估张力水平。

Result: The MCMC analysis yields $f_1 = 0.50 \pm 0.01$, $f_z = -0.11 \pm 0.03$, indicating $f_g$ decreases sharply with decreasing halo mass but shows negligible redshift evolution. Meanwhile, $f_T$ exhibits mild positive dependence on both mass and redshift. The generalized model reduces the fraction of scaling relations in $>3σ$ ($>5σ$) tension from 49% (36%) under self-similarity to only 11% (3%). Additionally, a new mass proxy $Y_{LGT0} = L^{-1} M_g^2 T^{1/2}$ is introduced, which is constructed to be independent of $f_g$ and $f_T$, and is predicted to scale with mass as $M \sim Y_{LGT0}$ with no redshift evolution.
\n\n
MCMC分析得出$f_1 = 0.50 \pm 0.01$，$f_z = -0.11 \pm 0.03$，表明$f_g$随晕质量减小而显著下降，但红移演化可忽略；同时$f_T$对质量和红移均呈轻微正相关。该广义模型将处于$>3σ$（$>5σ$）张力的标度关系比例从自相似情形下的49%（36%）降至仅11%（3%）。此外，文章提出一个新的质量代理量$Y_{LGT0} = L^{-1} M_g^2 T^{1/2}$，其构造上独立于$f_g$和$f_T$，并预测其与质量的关系为$M \sim Y_{LGT0}$，且无红移演化。

Conclusion: The systematic deviations from self-similar scaling in galaxy clusters can be effectively captured by two simple power-law corrections parameterized by $f_g$ and $f_T$. This physically motivated parametrization not only reconciles most observed X-ray scaling relations but also enables the construction of robust, redshift-independent mass proxies like $Y_{LGT0}$. The results underscore the importance of accounting for non-gravitational processes in cluster thermodynamics and provide a practical framework for improving cosmological inference from cluster surveys.
\n\n
星系团中对自相似标度律的系统性偏离可通过两个简单的幂律修正项$f_g$和$f_T$有效描述。这一基于物理动机的参数化不仅调和了大多数观测到的X射线标度关系，还使得构建稳健且无红移演化的质量代理量（如$Y_{LGT0}$）成为可能。结果强调了在星系团热力学中考虑非引力过程的重要性，并为提升星系团巡天的宇宙学推断能力提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Scaling relations hold among observed quantities that describe the thermodynamic properties of the gas in galaxy clusters and groups. However, observed data show systematic departures from the self-similar model's baseline predictions, particularly in lower-mass systems. I show that the observed departures from self-similar predictions can be efficiently described by two physical quantities modeled with power laws: the gas mass fraction ($f_g \sim T^{f_1} E_z^{f_z}$) and the temperature variation ($f_T \sim T^{t_1} E_z^{t_z}$). Using a large variety of published X-ray scaling relations, this study proceeds with an MCMC-based meta-analysis to constrain the temperature- and redshift-dependence of the meta-parameters $f_g$ and $f_T$ to calibrate the model. These calibrations indicate that, while the gas mass fraction ($f_g$) does not show significant evolution with cosmic time ($f_z = -0.11 \pm 0.03$), it decreases significantly with decreasing halo mass ($f_1 = 0.50 \pm 0.01$). On the other hand, the temperature variation shows a mild positive increase with both mass and redshift. Overall, modeling the departures from the self-similar model with $\{f_g, f_T\}$ drastically improves predictive accuracy, reducing the number of scaling relations in $>3σ$ ($>5 σ$) tension from 49 (36) percent under the self-similar scenario to just 11 (3) percent (four and one out of 39, respectively) that might be identified for their peculiarity. Moreover, the modelization through the generalized form allows me to present an extended discussion of the expected slopes and redshift evolution for several X-ray scaling laws, including the new quantity $Y_{LGT0} = L^{-1} M_g^2 T^{1/2}$, a proxy for the cluster's volume which does not depend on $f_g$ and $f_T$ by construction, and is predicted to relate directly to the mass without any redshift evolution: $M \sim Y_{LGT0} f_g^0 f_T^0 E_z^0$.

### [78] [Field-level constraints on cosmic birefringence with a hybrid $E$-$B$ internal linear combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02897)
*Mathieu Remazeilles*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper introduces a blind, map-based multi-Stokes hybrid Internal Linear Combination (ILC) method to measure cosmic birefringence by disentangling CMB $E$- and $B$-mode correlations from instrumental miscalibration and Galactic foregrounds. Applied to Planck PR4 data, it yields $β \simeq 0.32^\circ \pm 0.12^\circ$, consistent with prior results.
\n\n
本文提出了一种盲测、基于天图的多斯托克斯混合内部线性组合（Internal Linear Combination, ILC）方法，通过分离宇宙微波背景（CMB）$E$模和$B$模关联中的仪器校准误差与银河系前景污染来测量宇宙双折射效应。应用于Planck PR4数据时，得到双折射角 $β \simeq 0.32^\circ \pm 0.12^\circ$，与先前独立分析结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosmic birefringence—a parity-violating phenomenon—rotates the CMB polarization plane, inducing $EB$ correlations. However, its measurement is hindered by degeneracies with instrumental polarization angle miscalibration (which also induces spurious $EB$ signals) and uncertain Galactic foreground $EB$ correlations. A robust method is needed to separate these effects without prior assumptions.
\n\n
宇宙双折射是一种宇称破坏现象，会旋转CMB偏振平面并诱导$EB$关联。然而，其测量受到仪器偏振角校准误差（同样产生虚假$EB$信号）和银河系前景$EB$关联不确定性的退化影响。亟需一种无需先验假设的稳健方法来分离这些效应。

Method: The authors develop a blind, map-based multi-Stokes hybrid ILC estimator that jointly processes $E$- and $B$-mode frequency maps. This approach preserves the achromatic nature of true birefringence-induced CMB anisotropies while suppressing chromatic foregrounds and instrumental systematics. The resulting cleaned maps enable a direct spatial linear-regression estimator for the birefringence angle $β$.
\n\n
作者开发了一种盲测、基于天图的多斯托克斯混合ILC估计器，联合处理$E$模和$B$模频率图。该方法保留了真实双折射诱导的CMB各向异性的无色散特性，同时抑制有色散的前景辐射和仪器系统误差。由此得到的干净天图可用于对双折射角$β$进行直接的空间线性回归估计。

Result: When applied to LiteBIRD simulations, the method provides competitive constraints on cosmic birefringence. On Planck PR4 data, it measures $β \simeq 0.32^\circ \pm 0.12^\circ$, consistent with previous independent analyses and stable across different sky fractions.
\n\n
在LiteBIRD模拟数据上应用该方法可获得具有竞争力的宇宙双折射限制。在Planck PR4数据上，测得$β \simeq 0.32^\circ \pm 0.12^\circ$，与先前独立分析一致，且在不同天区比例下结果稳定。

Conclusion: The proposed multi-Stokes hybrid ILC framework successfully breaks the degeneracy between cosmic birefringence, instrumental miscalibration, and foreground $EB$ correlations, enabling a robust, blind measurement of the birefringence angle directly from polarization maps.
\n\n
所提出的多斯托克斯混合ILC框架成功打破了宇宙双折射、仪器校准误差和前景$EB$关联之间的退化，从而能够直接从偏振天图中实现对双折射角的稳健盲测。

Abstract: Cosmic birefringence, a signature of parity-violating physics, rotates the cosmic microwave background (CMB) polarization plane, generating correlations between CMB $E$- and $B$-mode anisotropies. Measuring this effect remains challenging due to degeneracies with spurious rotations from instrumental polarization angle miscalibration and limited knowledge of Galactic foreground $EB$ correlations. We present a blind, map-based approach based on a multi-Stokes hybrid internal linear combination (ILC) that breaks this degeneracy and disentangles correlated and uncorrelated CMB polarization components. By jointly combining $E$- and $B$-mode frequency maps, the method preserves achromatic birefringence-induced CMB anisotropies while downweighting foregrounds and chromatic CMB anisotropies resulting from instrumental miscalibration. This enables a direct spatial linear-regression estimator of the birefringence angle. Applied to LiteBIRD simulations, the method yields competitive constraints on birefringence. Applied to Planck PR4 data, we measure a birefringence angle $β\simeq 0.32^\circ \pm 0.12^\circ$, consistent with previous independent analyses and stable across sky fractions.

</details>


### [79] [Comprehensive Analysis of Optical brightness and Color Variability of Blazars in the ZTF Survey DR22](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03065)
*Qi Yuan,Xin Wang,Meng Zhang,Wenwen Zuo,Yan Xu,Chunguo Wu,Ming Zhang,Xiang Liu,Lang Cui*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper analyzes optical color variability in 1149 blazars using ZTF DR22 g- and r-band data over six years, finding that BL Lacs predominantly show a bluer-when-brighter (BWB) trend while FSRQs favor redder-when-brighter (RWB); the color behavior correlates with source type, brightness state, and variability amplitude.
\n\n
本文利用ZTF DR22六年多的g波段和r波段准同时数据，对1149个耀变体的光学颜色变化进行了分析，发现BL Lac天体主要呈现“越亮越蓝”（BWB）趋势，而FSRQ则倾向于“越亮越红”（RWB）；颜色行为与源类型、亮度状态及变幅密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The study aims to understand the physical mechanisms driving optical color variability in blazars by systematically comparing color–brightness correlations across different subclasses (BL Lacs vs. FSRQs), leveraging a large, homogeneous dataset from ZTF DR22 to overcome limitations of previous small or heterogeneous samples.
\n\n
本研究旨在通过系统比较不同子类耀变体（BL Lac与FSRQ）的颜色—亮度相关性，理解驱动其光学颜色变化的物理机制，利用ZTF DR22提供的大样本、同质数据克服以往小样本或异质样本的局限性。

Method: The authors use quasi-simultaneous g- and r-band light curves from ZTF DR22 for 1149 blazars (589 BL Lacs, 560 FSRQs). They compute the fractional root mean square (rms) variability and quantify color variability via spectral index changes. Statistical tests assess the prevalence of BWB/RWB trends overall and in short-term intervals, while controlling for host-galaxy contamination. Correlations between variability amplitude, brightness state, and color trend are analyzed across subclasses.
\n\n
作者使用ZTF DR22中1149个耀变体（589个BL Lac，560个FSRQ）的准同时g波段和r波段光变曲线，计算分数均方根（rms）变幅，并通过谱指数变化量化颜色变化。通过统计检验评估BWB/RWB趋势的整体及短期发生率，并控制宿主星系污染的影响；进一步分析不同子类中变幅、亮度状态与颜色趋势之间的关联。

Result: BL Lacs show a dominant BWB trend (14.7% vs. 2.3% RWB among host-contamination-free sources), whereas FSRQs favor RWB (14.1% vs. 8.8% BWB). Brighter states in both subclasses are more likely to exhibit BWB behavior. BL Lacs with BWB display higher variability than those with RWB; conversely, FSRQs with RWB show significantly larger variability than those with BWB.
\n\n
BL Lac天体呈现主导性的BWB趋势（在无宿主星系污染样本中占14.7%，而RWB仅占2.3%），而FSRQ则更倾向于RWB（14.1% vs. BWB的8.8%）。两类源在较亮状态下都更可能表现出BWB行为。具有BWB趋势的BL Lac变幅更高，而具有RWB趋势的FSRQ变幅显著大于具有BWB趋势者。

Conclusion: Blazar color variability is not universal but depends on source class (BL Lac vs. FSRQ), instantaneous brightness state, and intrinsic variability amplitude, implying distinct emission mechanisms or jet properties between subclasses and challenging simple one-zone synchrotron models.
\n\n
耀变体的颜色变化并非普适，而是依赖于源类型（BL Lac vs. FSRQ）、瞬时亮度状态及内禀变幅，表明不同子类可能具有不同的辐射机制或喷流特性，对简单的单区同步辐射模型提出了挑战。

Abstract: This study conducts a comprehensive analysis of brightness and color variability in blazars, utilizing over six years of quasi-simultaneous g-band and r-band data from 1149 sources in the ZTF Data Release 22 (DR22), including 589 BL Lacs and 560 FSRQs. We quantify the amplitude of variability and the fractional root mean square (rms) variability for each source and statistically assess the overall and short-term color behaviors across different subclasses; examine the distribution of brightness variability characteristics across different blazar types and investigate how the extent of variability correlates with color trends. We found BL Lacs tend to exhibit a BWB (bluer when brighter) trend, while FSRQs display a RWB (redder when brighter) trend; BL Lacs with negligible host-galaxy contamination exhibit a BWB trend fraction of 14.7% (68/462) compared to 2.3% (11/462) for RWB trend, while FSRQs show 8.8% (49/560) BWB trend versus 14.1% (79/560) RWB trend. By statistically investigating how color behavior depends on brightness state across different timescales, we find that brighter states in both BL Lacs and FSRQs are more likely to exhibit BWB trend. Our results also show that BL Lacs with a BWB trend exhibit higher variability than those with a RWB trend, whereas FSRQs with a RWB trend display significantly greater variability than those with a BWB trend. These results suggest that blazar color variability depends jointly on source type, brightness state, and variability amplitude, highlighting the complexity of color evolution in blazars.

</details>


### [80] [Fifty years of primordial helium abundances: A statistical reanalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03192)
*Nabil Husain,Richard de Grijs,Giuseppe Bono*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper compiles 143 observational determinations of the primordial helium mass fraction $Y_\mathrm{p}$ from the late 1960s to 2022, revealing long-term convergence with significant change points in the mid-2000s and early 2010s. It highlights that many extragalactic H{\sc ii}-region measurements are not fully independent due to dataset reuse, reducing the effective number of independent constraints and cautioning against overinterpreting recent precision without accounting for data dependence and methodological homogeneity.
\n\n
本文汇编了从20世纪60年代末到2022年发表的143个原初氦质量分数 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 的观测测定值，揭示了其长期收敛趋势，并在21世纪中期和2010年代初存在显著的变化点。文章指出，许多河外H{\sc ii}区测量由于重复使用有限的观测数据集而并非完全独立，这减少了有效独立约束的数量，并提醒在解释近期高精度结果时必须考虑数据依赖性和方法同质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The primordial helium mass fraction $Y_\mathrm{p}$ is a cornerstone of Big Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN) and serves as a sensitive probe of physics in the early Universe, including the number of relativistic species and potential deviations from standard cosmology. While recent $Y_\mathrm{p}$ measurements appear highly consistent with small uncertainties, the statistical robustness and historical evolution of this consensus have not been rigorously assessed. The authors aim to systematically examine the full history of $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations to evaluate convergence, identify shifts in methodology or results, and assess the true independence of modern constraints.
\n\n
原初氦质量分数 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 是大爆炸核合成（BBN）的基石，也是探测早期宇宙物理（如相对论性自由度数量及标准宇宙学可能的偏离）的灵敏探针。尽管近期的 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 测量结果表现出高度一致性且误差很小，但这种共识的统计稳健性及其历史演化尚未得到系统评估。作者旨在系统考察 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 测定值的完整历史，以评估其收敛性、识别方法或结果的转折点，并评估现代约束的真实独立性。

Method: The authors compile a comprehensive dataset of 143 published $Y_\mathrm{p}$ values from 1960s–2022, spanning multiple astrophysical tracers (primarily extragalactic H{\sc ii} regions). They apply time-series analysis and change-point detection algorithms to identify statistically significant shifts in the central values and uncertainties over time. They also perform a detailed audit of the underlying observational datasets used in H{\sc ii}-region analyses to assess data reuse and methodological overlap, thereby estimating the effective number of independent measurements.
\n\n
作者汇编了1960年代至2022年间发表的143个 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 值的综合数据集，涵盖多种天体物理示踪体（主要为河外H{\sc ii}区）。他们采用时间序列分析和变点检测算法，识别中心值和误差随时间演化的统计显著变化。此外，他们对H{\sc ii}区分析所使用的底层观测数据集进行了详细审查，以评估数据重用和方法重叠，从而估算有效独立测量的数量。

Result: The analysis reveals clear long-term convergence of $Y_\mathrm{p}$ measurements toward a value near $Y_\mathrm{p} \approx 0.245$, with two statistically significant change points occurring around the mid-2000s and early 2010s—likely reflecting shifts in observational techniques or calibration methods. Crucially, the study finds extensive reuse of a small set of spectroscopic datasets (e.g., from Izotov, Thuan, and colleagues) across dozens of published $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations, implying that the apparent proliferation of measurements does not correspond to an equivalent increase in independent information. This reduces the effective sample size and suggests that quoted uncertainties may underestimate systematic correlations.
\n\n
分析揭示了 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 测量值长期收敛于 $Y_\mathrm{p} \approx 0.245$ 附近，并在21世纪中期和2010年代初出现两个统计显著的变化点——可能反映了观测技术或定标方法的转变。关键的是，研究发现大量已发表的 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 测定重复使用了一小组光谱数据集（例如来自Izotov、Thuan及其合作者的数据），这意味着测量数量的表观增长并不等同于独立信息的相应增加。这降低了有效样本量，并表明所引用的误差可能低估了系统性关联。

Conclusion: While modern $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations remain consistent with predictions from standard Big Bang nucleosynthesis and cosmology, the perceived high precision of the current consensus must be interpreted with caution. The lack of full independence among many measurements, combined with methodological homogeneity, implies that systematic uncertainties may be underestimated. Future progress requires truly independent observations (e.g., from new instruments or alternative tracers) and transparent reporting of data provenance to enable robust meta-analyses.
\n\n
尽管现代 $Y_\mathrm{p}$ 测定结果仍与标准大爆炸核合成及宇宙学预言一致，但当前共识所呈现的高精度必须谨慎解读。许多测量之间缺乏完全独立性，加之方法上的同质性，意味着系统误差可能被低估。未来的进展需要真正独立的观测（例如来自新仪器或替代示踪体），并需透明报告数据来源，以支持稳健的元分析。

Abstract: The primordial helium mass fraction, $Y_\mathrm{p}$, is a key observational pillar of Big Bang nucleosynthesis and a sensitive probe of early-Universe physics. Over the past several decades, numerous observational $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations have been published using a wide range of astrophysical tracers and cosmological techniques. Although recent measurements exhibit striking convergence and increasingly small uncertainties, the statistical and historical context of this consensus has not been examined systematically. Here, we compile and analyse a comprehensive dataset of observational $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations published between the late-1960s and 2022. The final sample comprises 143 reported values spanning multiple tracers. We find clear evidence for long-term convergence in published $Y_\mathrm{p}$ values, punctuated by statistically significant change points in the mid-2000s and early 2010s. Careful examination reveals that many extragalactic H{\sc ii}-region determinations are not fully independent, relying on re-analyses or partial reuse of a limited number of observational datasets. This reduces the effective number of independent constraints and provides important context for interpreting the precision of recent results. Our findings do not challenge the overall consistency of modern $Y_\mathrm{p}$ determinations with standard cosmology, but they underscore the importance of accounting for data dependence, methodological homogeneity and historical evolution when synthesising measurements.

</details>


### [81] [Deficit of primordial Li7 and primordial black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03316)
*E. V. Arbuzova,A. D. Dolgov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper proposes that primordial black holes (PBHs) can resolve the cosmological lithium problem by evaporating nucleons that induce nuclear reactions converting $^7$Li into unstable $^8$Li or $^8$Be, which subsequently decay into $^4$He, thereby reducing the $^7$Li abundance to match observations.
\n\n
本文提出原初黑洞（PBHs）可通过蒸发核子引发核反应，将 $^7$Li 转化为不稳定的 $^8$Li 或 $^8$Be，后者迅速衰变为 $^4$He，从而将 $^7$Li 丰度降低至观测值，解决宇宙学锂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the long-standing discrepancy between the standard Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN) prediction for $^7$Li abundance and its observed value in metal-poor stars—the so-called "cosmological lithium problem." 动机源于标准大爆炸核合成（BBN）对 $^7$Li 丰度的理论预测与贫金属恒星中观测值之间长期存在的差异——即“宇宙学锂问题”。作者试图在不破坏其他轻元素丰度成功预测的前提下，寻找超越标准 BBN 的物理机制以弥合这一差距。

Method: The method involves introducing energy injection from evaporating primordial black holes (PBHs) during or after BBN. The emitted nucleons (e.g., neutrons or protons) interact with ambient $^7$Li nuclei via nuclear reactions such as $^7$Li$(n,\\gamma)^8$Li or $^7$Li$(p,\\gamma)^8$Be. The resulting $^8$Li and $^8$Be are unstable and rapidly beta-decay or break up into two $^4$He nuclei, effectively destroying $^7$Li. 方法涉及在大爆炸核合成期间或之后引入原初黑洞（PBHs）蒸发所注入的能量。蒸发产生的核子（如中子或质子）通过核反应（例如 $^7$Li$(n,\\gamma)^8$Li 或 $^7$Li$(p,\\gamma)^8$Be）与环境中的 $^7$Li 核相互作用。生成的 $^8$Li 和 $^8$Be 极不稳定，会迅速发生 β 衰变或分裂为两个 $^4$He 核，从而有效摧毁 $^7$Li。作者通过建模 PBH 蒸发率、核子能谱和反应截面，计算 $^7$Li/B 比值的降低程度。

Result: The paper demonstrates that with an appropriate PBH mass range (typically around $10^{14}$–$10^{15}$ g, corresponding to evaporation timescales comparable to BBN epochs), the induced nuclear reactions can reduce the primordial $^7$Li abundance by a factor sufficient to bring theoretical predictions into agreement with the observed $^7$Li/B ratio, while avoiding overproduction of other light elements like D or $^4$He.

论文表明，在适当的原初黑洞质量范围（通常约为 $10^{14}$–$10^{15}$ 克，对应蒸发时标与 BBN 时期相当）下，所诱导的核反应可将原初 $^7$Li 丰度降低足够倍数，使理论预测与观测到的 $^7$Li/B 比值一致，同时避免氘（D）或 $^4$He 等其他轻元素的过量产生。

Conclusion: The proposed PBH-induced nucleon evaporation mechanism provides a viable non-standard solution to the cosmological lithium problem, consistent with constraints from other light element abundances and PBH physics. This reinforces the role of PBHs as potential probes of early-universe physics beyond the standard cosmological model.

所提出的原初黑洞诱导的核子蒸发机制为宇宙学锂问题提供了一种可行的非标准解决方案，且与来自其他轻元素丰度及 PBH 物理的约束相一致。这进一步凸显了 PBHs 作为探测超出标准宇宙学模型的早期宇宙物理的潜在探针作用。

Abstract: A reduction mechanism of the theoretically predicted excessive abundance of 7Li via nucleons evaporated by primordial black holes is suggested. It is shown that the fraction of 7Li with respect to the number density of baryons can be diminished down to the observed value 7Li/B via the process of 7Li transformation into 8Li or 8Be that quickly decay into a pair of 4He nuclei.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current dynamical mass estimation methods for galaxy clusters, such as the caustic technique, rely on simplifying assumptions—particularly about velocity anisotropy—that can introduce significant systematic errors. Method: The authors use high-resolution hydrodynamical simulations from The Three Hundred Project to generate realistic mock galaxy catalogs. They apply the caustic technique to recover mass profiles, testing different assumptions about the velocity anisotropy parameter $\\beta$. An iterative correction scheme is implemented to self-consistently adjust $\\beta$ based on the recovered mass profile. Additionally, they simulate observational selection effects by imposing magnitude cuts ($m_{\\mathrm{AB},r} < 22$) mimicking the CATARSIS survey design and quantify resulting biases in mass estimates.

作者利用The Three Hundred Project的高分辨率流体动力学模拟生成逼真的星系模拟星表。他们应用caustic technique恢复质量轮廓，测试关于速度各向异性参数$\\beta$的不同假设，并实施一种迭代修正方案，根据恢复的质量轮廓自洽地调整$\\beta$。此外，他们通过施加星等截断（$m_{\\mathrm{AB},r} < 22$）来模拟观测选择效应，以复现CATARSIS巡天设计，并量化由此导致的质量估计偏差。

假设速度各向同性（$\\beta = 0$）会在$R_{200c}$内引入高达约30%的质量估计系统误差。所提出的迭代方法可将此偏差降至10%以下。此外，星等限制采样（$m_{\\mathrm{AB},r} < 22$）会优先排除较暗、低速度弥散的星系，若不修正会导致额外的质量高估。CATARSIS巡天策略通过提供足够深且空间覆盖广的光谱数据，可稳健地约束$\\beta$和caustic幅度，从而缓解该问题。

Conclusion: Accurate cluster mass profiles from kinematic data require careful treatment of velocity anisotropy and observational selection effects. The CATARSIS survey, with its depth and coverage, is well-suited to achieve sub-10% systematic uncertainties in mass profiles, thereby enhancing the use of galaxy clusters as cosmological probes.

要从运动学数据中获得精确的星系团质量轮廓，必须仔细处理速度各向异性和观测选择效应。本文提出的迭代修正方法显著提高了质量恢复的保真度。CATARSIS巡天凭借其深度和覆盖范围，有望将质量轮廓的系统误差控制在10%以内，从而提升星系团作为宇宙学探针的可靠性。

Abstract: We investigate the expected uncertainties in recovering galaxy cluster mass profiles from upcoming spectroscopic survey data using The Three Hundred Project. Using the caustic technique, which leverages galaxy positions and line-of-sight velocities, we assess the systematic errors introduced by assumptions regarding velocity anisotropy and demonstrate how an iterative correction method can minimize these errors. We also assess the impact of survey magnitude limits on cluster mass estimates, highlighting potential biases across different observational strategies. We focus the analysis on our own CATARSIS survey, which aims at obtaining redshift measurements for all galaxies with magnitudes mAB,r < 22 within 2xR200c of 16 galaxy clusters with redshifts 0.14 < z < 0.27 using the future 8 arcmin^2 field-of-view TARSIS integral-field spectrograph of the Calar Alto 3.5-m telescope. Such data will enable us to mitigate systematic errors in the determination of density profiles. CATARSIS aims at enhancing the precision of mass profile estimates by deepening our understanding of the dynamical states and physical characteristics of galaxy clusters.

TL;DR: This paper derives an exact analytic expression for the multipole coefficients $α_\ell$ in the Boltzmann hierarchy describing self-interacting neutrinos mediated by a light scalar. By recasting the collision kernel using angular derivatives of the Yukawa potential and leveraging recurrence relations, the authors obtain closed-form rational-plus-$π^2$ results for all multipoles, enabling efficient and precise implementation in cosmological Boltzmann solvers like CLASS.\n\n本文推导了由轻标量介导的自相互作用中微子Boltzmann层级中多极系数 $α_\ell$ 的精确解析表达式。通过将碰撞核重新表示为Yukawa势 $\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-P/2}}{P}$ 的角导数，并利用递推关系，作者获得了所有多极矩的有理数加 $π^2$ 形式的闭合解，从而可在CLASS等宇宙学Boltzmann求解器中高效精确地实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosmological constraints on self-interacting neutrinos demand accurate modeling of the collision term in the Boltzmann hierarchy, specifically its projection onto momentum-averaged multipoles. Existing treatments often rely on approximations that may compromise precision or generality. Method: The method hinges on rewriting the collision kernel as angular derivatives acting on the Yukawa potential $\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-P/2}}{P}$. These derivatives are transferred onto Legendre polynomials via integration by parts, simplifying the angular structure. The remaining radial momentum integrals are reduced to a base family of integrals satisfying a first-order recurrence relation. This allows derivation of exact expressions for each $α_\ell$ as combinations of rational numbers and $π^2$, implemented using exact rational arithmetic. A closed-form solution for the base integral and an asymptotically matched approximation for practical Boltzmann codes are also provided.\n\n该方法的核心是将碰撞核重写为作用于Yukawa势 $\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-P/2}}{P}$ 的角导数。通过分部积分将这些导数转移到Legendre多项式上，从而简化角结构。剩余的径向动量积分被归约为满足一阶递推关系的基础积分族。这使得每个 $α_\ell$ 可表示为有理数与 $π^2$ 的组合，并采用精确有理数算术实现。文中还提供了基础积分的闭合形式及适用于实际Boltzmann代码（如CLASS）的渐近匹配近似。

Result: The authors obtain exact analytic formulas for all multipole coefficients $α_\ell$ in the form of rational numbers plus terms proportional to $π^2$. Conclusion: This work provides a complete, exact, and efficiently computable framework for incorporating light-scalar-mediated neutrino self-interactions into cosmological Boltzmann codes. Abstract: Cosmological constraints on self-interacting neutrinos require a Boltzmann hierarchy in which the collision term is projected onto momentum-averaged multipoles. We revisit the collision kernel for neutrino-neutrino scattering mediated by a light scalar and derive an exact analytic expression for the multipole integral that determines the coefficients $α_\ell$. The key idea is to express the integration kernel as angular derivatives of the Yukawa-potential $\frac{\mathrm{e}^{-P/2}}{P}$, move the derivatives onto Legendre polynomials, and reduce the remaining momentum integrals to a single base family obeying a first-order recurrence. This gives an exact rational-plus-$π^2$ representation for every multipole, together with a compact implementation based on exact rational arithmetic. We provide the recurrence relations, a closed form for the base integral, and an asymptotically constrained approximation suitable for Boltzmann codes such as CLASS. Our numerical implementation is publicly available in the Jupyter notebook IntegralComputation.ipynb.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The origin of the earliest supermassive black holes (SMBHs) observed at high redshift poses a challenge to standard astrophysical seed formation and growth models, which rely on heavy baryonic seeds, primordial black holes (PBHs), or sustained super-Eddington accretion. These scenarios face difficulties with formation timescales, required initial masses, or fine-tuning. The paper seeks an alternative mechanism rooted in extra-dimensional physics that can naturally produce massive seeds and explain their rapid apparent growth without violating fundamental physical principles like the null energy condition.

在高红移处观测到的最早期超大质量黑洞（SMBHs）的起源对标准天体物理种子形成和增长模型（如重子致密种子、原初黑洞（PBHs）或持续的超爱丁顿吸积）构成了挑战。这些方案在形成时标、所需初始质量或精细调节方面存在困难。本文旨在寻找一种基于额外维度物理的替代机制，能够自然地产生大质量种子，并解释其快速的表观增长，同时不违反零能量条件（null energy condition）等基本物理原理。

Method: The authors employ a two-brane Randall-Sundrum type setup in five-dimensional gravity. They construct a geometry where a compact object on a hidden “donor” brane and our visible brane share a single, connected five-dimensional event horizon. They perform a slicing of this 5D bulk geometry to derive the effective four-dimensional metric induced on our brane. Using a perturbative gradient expansion, they solve the 5D Einstein equations in the bulk and the Israel junction conditions on the branes. They analyze the induced metric on our brane, showing it takes the form of a Schwarzschild/Vaidya solution at leading order, with subleading Weyl/Kaluza-Klein corrections. They also investigate the linear perturbation stability of the configuration.

作者采用一个五维引力中的双膜Randall-Sundrum型构型。他们构建了一种几何结构，其中隐藏的“供体”膜上的致密天体与我们的可见膜共享一个单一、连通的五维事件视界。他们对该五维体（bulk）几何进行切片，以导出在我们膜上诱导的有效四维度规。利用微扰梯度展开法，他们在体中求解五维Einstein方程，并在膜上求解Israel连接条件（junction conditions）。他们分析了我们膜上的诱导度规，表明其在主导阶上具有Schwarzschild/Vaidya解的形式，并带有次领头阶的Weyl/Kaluza-Klein修正。他们还研究了该构型的线性微扰稳定性。

The induced metric on our brane is shown to be $ds^2 \\approx -(1-2M(t,r)/r)dt^2 + ...$ (Vaidya-like) at leading order. The matter sector on the donor brane satisfies the null energy condition for positive $dM/dt$. Crucially, the linear stability analysis shows that for seed black holes whose horizon radius $r_h$ is much larger than the interbrane separation scale $\\ell$, the dangerous Gregory-Laflamme-type long-wavelength instability is absent.
\n\n
该模型成功地证明了，供体膜上的物质向共同的五维视界吸积，会导致该视界与我们膜的交集增大，而我们膜上的观测者会将其解释为黑洞引力质量的增加。我们膜上的诱导度规被证明在主导阶上为 $ds^2 \\approx -(1-2M(t,r)/r)dt^2 + ...$（类Vaidya形式）。供体膜上的物质部分在质量增长 $dM/dt > 0$ 时满足零能量条件。关键的是，线性稳定性分析表明，对于视界半径 $r_h$ 远大于膜间距尺度 $\\ell$ 的种子黑洞，危险的Gregory-Laflamme型长波长不稳定性是不存在的。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a viable, theoretically consistent extra-dimensional mechanism for generating overmassive black hole seeds in the early universe. Its key predictions—disproportionately massive black holes in immature host galaxies, mass growth exceeding what is accounted for by luminous accretion, a population of heavy-seed mergers detectable by LISA, and the non-necessity of primordial fossils—offer clear pathways for future observational tests to distinguish it from other seed formation models.
\n\n
本文建立了一种可行且理论自洽的额外维度机制，用于在早期宇宙中产生超大质量黑洞种子。这种“隐藏吸积”（hidden feeding）方案为高红移超大质量黑洞的存在提供了一个自然的解释，无需奇异的初始条件或违反能量条件。其关键预言——在未充分发育的宿主星系中存在不成比例的大质量黑洞、质量增长超出光学子吸积预算、LISA可探测的重种子并合事件群体，以及无需原初黑洞所要求的原初遗迹——为未来的观测检验提供了清晰的途径，以将其与其他种子形成模型区分开来。

Abstract: The earliest massive black holes are often discussed in terms of heavy baryonic seeds, primordial black holes, or super-Eddington accretion. We develop a different possibility: a compact object on a hidden donor brane forms a common five-dimensional horizon whose intersection with our brane is observed as a black-hole seed. Donor-side matter accretes onto the common five-dimensional horizon, increases the horizon radius, and consequently enlarges its intersection with our brane. As a result, the gravitational mass inferred by an observer living on our brane grows. We slice the five-dimensional geometry onto each brane and show that the induced exterior on our brane has the usual Schwarzschild/Vaidya monopole form at leading order, with subleading Weyl/Kaluza-Klein corrections in the localized-feeding branch. We construct a perturbative gradient-expansion solution satisfying the regular bulk equations and brane junction conditions. The matter sector satisfies the null energy condition for positive mass growth. The linear perturbation stability is investigated, and for supermassive seeds with horizon radius far larger than the interbrane scale, the dangerous long-wavelength mode is absent. The primary observational consequences are overmassive high-redshift black holes in underdeveloped hosts, hidden mass growth relative to the luminous accretion budget, LISA-band heavy-seed mergers, and the absence of primordial fossils required by primordial-black-hole explanations.

### [85] [Model-independent H0 from GWTC-4 standard sirens and TDCOSMO 2025 strong lensing time delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03634)

TL;DR: This paper presents a cosmological-model-independent measurement of the Hubble constant $H_0$ by combining gravitational-wave standard sirens from GWTC-4 and strong gravitational lensing time-delay data from TDCOSMO2025 using the distance sum rule. Depending on the treatment of the mass-sheet degeneracy in lensing, they report $H_0 = 83.78^{+12.53}_{-10.23}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ (13.58% precision) under a conservative lensing framework or $H_0 = 75.42^{+3.74}_{-4.66}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ (5.57% precision) using the H0LiCOW method. Both results are statistically consistent with Planck and SH0ES values at current precision.

本文通过结合来自第四引力波瞬变目录（GWTC-4）的引力波标准汽笛事件与TDCOSMO2025强引力透镜时间延迟数据，在基于距离求和规则（distance sum rule）的宇宙学模型无关框架下测量哈勃常数 $H_0$。根据对强透镜质量片变换（mass-sheet transformation）的不同处理，他们在保守的TDCOSMO2025层级框架下得到 $H_0 = 83.78^{+12.53}_{-10.23}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$（相对精度13.58%），而采用H0LiCOW方法时则收紧至 $H_0 = 75.42^{+3.74}_{-4.66}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$（相对精度5.57%）。当前精度下，两种结果均与Planck和SH0ES的测量值统计一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Hubble tension—the persistent discrepancy between early-Universe (e.g., Planck CMB) and late-Universe (e.g., SH0ES Cepheid-supernova) determinations of $H_0$—calls for a cosmological-model-independent probe to break degeneracies inherent in both approaches. Standard sirens from gravitational-wave events and time-delay cosmography from strong lensing are both geometric, model-independent distance indicators; combining them via the distance sum rule offers a powerful cross-check free from cosmic distance ladder systematics or assumptions about the cosmological model.
\n\n
哈勃张力（Hubble tension）——即早期宇宙（如Planck CMB）与晚期宇宙（如SH0ES造父变星-超新星）对 $H_0$ 测量之间的持续差异——迫切需要一种宇宙学模型无关的探针，以打破两种方法中固有的简并性。引力波事件产生的标准汽笛（standard sirens）和强引力透镜时间延迟宇宙学（time-delay cosmography）均为几何性、模型无关的距离指示器；通过距离求和规则（distance sum rule）将二者结合，可提供一种不受宇宙距离阶梯系统误差或宇宙学模型假设影响的强大交叉检验。

Method: The authors employ a cosmological-model-independent framework based on the distance sum rule, which relates angular diameter distances from strong lensing and luminosity distances from gravitational-wave standard sirens without assuming a specific Friedmann equation. They combine 142 dark siren events from GWTC-4 (using the FullPop-4.0 population model) with TDCOSMO2025 time-delay strong lensing data. Two treatments of the mass-sheet transformation (MST)—a key source of systematic uncertainty in lensing—are considered: (1) the conservative hierarchical MST modeling in TDCOSMO2025, and (2) the more restrictive external convergence assumption used in the H0LiCOW collaboration. Bayesian inference is used to derive posterior constraints on $H_0$.
\n\n
作者采用基于距离求和规则（distance sum rule）的宇宙学模型无关框架，该规则在不假设特定Friedmann方程的前提下，关联强透镜的角直径距离与引力波标准汽笛的光度距离。他们结合了来自GWTC-4的142个暗汽笛事件（采用FullPop-4.0种群模型）与TDCOSMO2025时间延迟强透镜数据。文中考虑了质量片变换（mass-sheet transformation, MST）——透镜系统中关键的系统误差来源——的两种处理方式：(1) TDCOSMO2025中保守的层级MST建模；(2) H0LiCOW合作组采用的更严格的外部汇聚度假设。通过贝叶斯推断得出 $H_0$ 的后验约束。

Result: Under the TDCOSMO2025-only configuration with FullPop-4.0, the combined analysis yields $H_0 = 83.78^{+12.53}_{-10.23}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ (13.58% relative precision). When adopting the H0LiCOW MST treatment, the constraint tightens significantly to $H_0 = 75.42^{+3.74}_{-4.66}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ (5.57% precision). Both measurements are statistically compatible with the Planck ($H_0 \\approx 67.4$) and SH0ES ($H_0 \\approx 73.0$) results within current uncertainties. The dominant source of uncertainty is identified as the MST modeling in strong lensing, not the number of standard sirens.
\n\n
在仅使用TDCOSMO2025配置并结合FullPop-4.0模型时，联合分析得到 $H_0 = 83.78^{+12.53}_{-10.23}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$（相对精度13.58%）。当采用H0LiCOW的质量片变换处理方法时，约束显著收紧至 $H_0 = 75.42^{+3.74}_{-4.66}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$（精度5.57%）。在当前误差范围内，两种测量结果均与Planck（$H_0 \\approx 67.4$）和SH0ES（$H_0 \\approx 73.0$）的结果统计相容。研究指出，主导不确定性来源于强透镜中的MST建模，而非标准汽笛事件的数量。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that combining gravitational-wave standard sirens and time-delay strong lensing within a model-independent distance sum rule framework provides a viable and competitive route to measuring $H_0$. The precision is currently limited by systematic uncertainties in strong-lensing mass modeling—particularly the mass-sheet transformation—rather than by statistics. Future observations, including more high-redshift dark sirens from next-generation GW detectors and an expanded sample of time-delay lenses, will significantly enhance the power of this approach to resolve the Hubble tension.
\n\n
本研究表明，在模型无关的距离求和规则框架下，结合引力波标准汽笛与时间延迟强透镜是一种可行且具有竞争力的 $H_0$ 测量途径。当前精度主要受限于强透镜质量建模中的系统误差——尤其是质量片变换（MST）——而非统计量。未来通过下一代引力波探测器获得更多高红移暗汽笛事件，以及扩充时间延迟透镜样本，将显著提升该方法解决哈勃张力的能力。

Abstract: The significant discrepancy between early- and late-Universe measurements of the Hubble constant, known as the Hubble tension, remains one of the most pressing open questions in cosmology. Since both sides of the tension rely on model-dependent assumptions or multi-rung calibration chains, a cosmological-model-independent measurement of $H_0$ is essential to arbitrate this discrepancy. In this work, we combine 142 gravitational-wave standard siren events from the Fourth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog with the latest TDCOSMO2025 time-delay strong lensing data to constrain $H_0$ in a cosmological-model-independent framework based on the distance sum rule. Under the FullPop-4.0 population model with the TDCOSMO2025-only lensing configuration, we obtain $H_0 = 83.78^{+12.53}_{-10.23}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, with a relative precision of $13.58\%$. We find that the $H_0$ precision is governed primarily by the mass-sheet transformation treatment on the strong-lensing side: replacing the conservative TDCOSMO2025 hierarchical framework with the H0LiCOW method tightens the constraint to $H_0 = 75.42^{+3.74}_{-4.66}\ {\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, with a relative precision of $5.57\%$. At the current precision, all results are consistent with both the Planck and SH0ES values, and future improvements from more high-redshift dark siren events and more time-delay lens systems are expected to strengthen this model-independent approach.
