### [86] [Constraining Scale-Dependent Growth in $f(R)$ Gravity with Future 21 cm Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03729)
*Apurba Samanta,Bhuwan Joshi,Jess Worsley,Peter Dunsby,Saikat Chakraborty*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether upcoming 21 cm intensity mapping surveys can constrain scale- and redshift-dependent modifications to the growth of structure predicted by $f(R)$ modified gravity models, which aim to explain late-time cosmic acceleration without a cosmological constant. The authors find that such surveys offer moderate but meaningful constraints on the growth index and the combined HI bias–growth rate parameter.
\n\n
本文研究了未来的21 cm强度映射巡天能否对$f(R)$修正引力模型所预言的、依赖于尺度和红移的结构增长修正进行限制。这些模型试图在不引入宇宙学常数的情况下解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀。作者发现，此类巡天能对增长指数以及中性氢（HI）偏置与增长率参数的组合提供适度但有意义的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivated by recent DESI observations hinting at dynamical dark energy, the paper explores $f(R)$ gravity as a theoretically well-motivated alternative that replaces dark energy with a modification of General Relativity. A key prediction of such models is a scale- and redshift-dependent linear growth index $\\gamma(k,z)$, which deviates from the approximately constant value in $\\Lambda$CDM. Constraining this deviation provides a direct test of modified gravity, and 21 cm cosmology offers a promising high-redshift probe complementary to optical galaxy surveys.
\n\n
受近期DESI观测暗示动力学暗能量的启发，本文探讨了$f(R)$引力作为一种理论动机充分的替代方案——它通过修改广义相对论来取代暗能量。这类模型的一个关键预言是线性增长指数$\\gamma(k,z)$依赖于尺度$k$和红移$z$，这与$\\Lambda$CDM中近似恒定的值形成偏离。对这一偏离的限制可直接检验修正引力理论，而21 cm宇宙学提供了一种有前景的高红移探针，可与光学星系巡天互补。

Method: The authors adopt a phenomenological parametrization of the growth factor $f_g(z,k) = \\Omega_m(z)^{\\gamma(k,z)}$, where $\\gamma(k,z)$ is allowed to vary with scale and redshift as predicted by specific $f(R)$ models (e.g., Hu–Sawicki). They compute the expected 21 cm power spectrum, focusing on the combination $b_{\\rm HI}(z) f_g(z,k)$, where $b_{\\rm HI}$ is the neutral hydrogen bias. Using Fisher matrix forecasts for future 21 cm experiments (e.g., SKA), they quantify the constraining power on $\\gamma(k,z)$ and $b_{\\rm HI} f_g$ by comparing against $\\Lambda$CDM and viable $f(R)$ scenarios.
\n\n
作者采用唯象参数化方法描述增长率$f_g(z,k) = \\Omega_m(z)^{\\gamma(k,z)}$，其中$\\gamma(k,z)$根据具体的$f(R)$模型（如Hu–Sawicki模型）允许随尺度和红移变化。他们计算了预期的21 cm功率谱，重点关注中性氢偏置$b_{\\rm HI}(z)$与增长率$f_g(z,k)$的组合$b_{\\rm HI} f_g$。通过针对未来21 cm实验（如SKA）的Fisher矩阵预测，他们量化了对$\\gamma(k,z)$和$b_{\\rm HI} f_g$的限制能力，并与$\\Lambda$CDM及可行的$f(R)$情景进行比较。

Result: Future 21 cm intensity mapping surveys are projected to constrain the effective growth index and the $b_{\\rm HI} f_g$ parameter with sufficient precision to distinguish certain $f(R)$ models from $\\Lambda$CDM at moderate significance. While not definitive, these constraints provide non-negligible support for or against specific modified gravity scenarios, particularly due to the unique access to small scales and high redshifts ($z \\gtrsim 1$) where deviations are amplified.
\n\n
预计未来的21 cm强度映射巡天能够以足够精度限制有效增长指数和$b_{\\rm HI} f_g$参数，从而以中等显著性区分某些$f(R)$模型与$\\Lambda$CDM。尽管并非决定性证据，但这些限制对特定修正引力情景提供了不可忽略的支持或反驳，尤其是因为21 cm观测能独特地覆盖小尺度和高红移（$z \\gtrsim 1$）区域，在这些区域修正效应被放大。

Conclusion: 21 cm cosmology represents a valuable complementary probe for testing modified gravity theories like $f(R)$ through measurements of the scale- and redshift-dependent growth of structure. Although current constraints are modest, next-generation surveys will significantly enhance our ability to test deviations from General Relativity on cosmological scales, offering a pathway to validate or rule out popular dark-energy-free explanations for cosmic acceleration.
\n\n
21 cm宇宙学是通过测量依赖于尺度和红移的结构增长来检验$f(R)$等修正引力理论的一种宝贵且互补的探针。尽管当前限制尚属温和，但下一代巡天将极大提升我们在宇宙学尺度上检验广义相对论偏离的能力，为验证或排除流行的无暗能量宇宙加速解释提供途径。

Abstract: Recent observations, particularly from DESI, have provided intriguing hints of dynamical behaviour in late-time dark energy. Modified gravity theories offer a compelling framework for interpreting such phenomena, with $f(R)$ gravity emerging as one of the most extensively studied examples. A central challenge in these models, however, lies in determining the precise functional form of $f(R)$. Nevertheless, several viable models have been proposed that successfully reproduce the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology at high red shifts while generating late-time cosmic acceleration without an explicit dark energy component. Within this framework, the evolution of the linear matter density contrast becomes scale dependent, leading to a growth index that varies with both scale and redshift. In this work, we explore the capability of forthcoming 21 cm observations to constrain the growth index, as well as the combined neutral hydrogen (HI) bias and growth-rate parameter. Our results indicate that future 21 cm surveys can provide meaningful, though moderate, support for these modified gravity scenarios.

These mocks reproduce the observed cross-correlation within large statistical scatter, though the peak correlation scale is offset by ~10 cMpc. Current data cannot strongly constrain ionising source models; larger samples and larger-volume simulations (>250 cMpc) are needed.\n\n詹姆斯 Webb 太空望远镜（JWST）对红移 $z \\sim 6$ 处 [O III] 发射星系的观测显示其与 Ly$\\alpha$ 森林透射率的互相关对现有模型构成挑战。作者构建了一个基于暗晕的经验模型，并纳入 JWST 巡天的几何结构和深度以生成模拟星表。这些模拟在较大的统计散射范围内重现了观测到的互相关，尽管其一维相关峰的位置比观测值偏小约 10 cMpc。当前数据尚不足以严格限制电离源模型；未来需要更大的观测样本和更大体积（>250 cMpc）的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent JWST measurements of the cross-correlation between [O III]-emitting galaxies and Ly$\\alpha$ forest transmission at $z \\sim 6$ appear inconsistent with predictions from numerical simulations, suggesting a gap in our understanding of how high-redshift galaxies trace the underlying dark matter haloes and influence the intergalactic medium (IGM). This motivates the development of a more observationally grounded theoretical framework.\n\n近期 JWST 对红移 $z \\sim 6$ 处 [O III] 发射星系与 Ly$\\alpha$ 森林透射率互相关的测量结果似乎与数值模拟预测不一致，表明我们对高红移星系如何追踪底层暗物质晕并影响星系际介质（IGM）的理解存在不足。这促使作者发展一个更贴近观测的理论框架。

Method: The authors construct an empirical model linking dark matter haloes to observed [O III] emitters using observational constraints. They generate mock galaxy catalogues that incorporate the actual geometry and depth of JWST surveys. These mocks are then used to compute clustering statistics and the 1D/2D cross-correlation functions between galaxies and Ly$\\alpha$ forest transmission, which are compared directly to recent observational data.\n\n作者利用观测约束构建了一个将暗物质晕与观测到的 [O III] 发射体联系起来的经验模型。他们生成了包含 JWST 巡天实际几何结构和深度的模拟星系星表，并用这些模拟计算星系成团性统计量以及星系与 Ly$\\alpha$ 森林透射率之间的一维和二维互相关函数，并将其直接与最新观测数据进行比较。

Result: The mock surveys exhibit large sample variance in the galaxy–Ly$\\alpha$ cross-correlation, allowing for statistically acceptable agreement with observations despite a systematic offset: the peak of the 1D cross-correlation in mocks occurs at a scale ~10 cMpc smaller than observed. The large scatter implies current data cannot robustly discriminate among different ionising source scenarios.\n\n模拟巡天在星系–Ly$\\alpha$ 互相关中表现出较大的样本方差，尽管存在系统性偏移——模拟中一维互相关的峰值尺度比观测值小约 10 cMpc——但仍能与观测数据达成统计上可接受的一致性。这种大散射意味着当前数据无法可靠地区分不同的电离源模型。

Conclusion: Current JWST galaxy–IGM cross-correlation measurements, while groundbreaking, are limited by cosmic variance and small sample sizes. Abstract: James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) surveys of [O III]-emitting galaxies are offering fresh insight into the connection between galaxies and the intergalactic medium at redshift z ~ 6. Recent measurements of the cross-correlation between [O III]-emitting galaxies and Ly $α$ forest transmission present an apparent challenge to numerical models. Here we improve upon previous theoretical work by constructing an empirical model that connects haloes with the observed population of [O III] emitters and incorporates the geometry and depth of the JWST surveys into mock galaxy survey catalogues. We compare these mocks to recent measurements of [O III] emitter clustering and the one and two dimensional galaxy-Ly $α$ transmission cross-correlation. The large scatter in our mock survey measurements of the cross-correlation enable a statistically good match to the observational data, albeit the peak of the one dimensional correlation in our mocks occurs at a scale $\approx$10 cMpc below that observed. The large scatter implies that, at present, current galaxy-IGM observations may struggle to rule out a broad range of ionising source models. We anticipate that further progress will strongly benefit from increased observational sample sizes, as well as simulations performed in box sizes >250 cMpc that use a variety of source models.

### [88] [GraphShed: a parameter-free Graph-based waterShed group finder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03790)
*P. Ghafour,S. Ansarifard,M. H. Jalali Kanafi,S. M. S. Movahed*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: GraphShed is a novel parameter-free galaxy group finder using top-down watershed segmentation on Voronoi-induced graphs, yielding cosmologically consistent yet structurally refined galaxy group catalogs compared to Friends-of-Friends. \n\n GraphShed 是一种新颖的无参数星系群识别方法，通过对 Voronoi 诱导图应用自上而下的分水岭分割，在与 Friends-of-Friends 方法保持宇宙学统计一致性的同时，提供了结构上更精细的星系群星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional group-finding algorithms like Friends-of-Friends rely on tunable parameters or density thresholds, which can bias structural and dynamical interpretations of galaxy systems; thus, a parameter-free method is needed to robustly extract intrinsic properties while preserving large-scale cosmological statistics. \n\n 传统的星系群识别算法（如 Friends-of-Friends）依赖于可调参数或密度阈值，可能对星系系统的结构和动力学解释引入偏差；因此，需要一种无参数的方法，在保留大尺度宇宙学统计量的同时稳健地提取内在性质。

Method: GraphShed applies top-down watershed segmentation to a set of separated Voronoi-induced graphs constructed from the discrete galaxy distribution, enabling direct identification of overdensities in the density field without any free parameters or density cuts. The resulting galaxy groups are compared with those from a Friends-of-Friends (FoF) algorithm using the IllustrisTNG100-1 simulation across multiple structural and statistical diagnostics. \n\n GraphShed 对由离散星系分布构建的分离 Voronoi 诱导图应用自上而下的分水岭分割，无需任何自由参数或密度截断即可直接从密度场中识别过密度区域。利用 IllustrisTNG100-1 模拟，将所得星系群与 Friends-of-Friends (FoF) 算法的结果在多种结构和统计诊断指标上进行比较。

Result: While $M_{200}$ distributions and large-scale statistics like the two-point correlation function and mass function agree between GraphShed and FoF, structural properties—such as $R_{200}$, sphericity, compactness, spin, and centroid shift—differ significantly. GraphShed better resolves nearby overdensities as distinct systems in velocity space, improving the identification of interacting pairs. \n\n 尽管 GraphShed 与 FoF 在 $M_{200}$ 分布以及两点相关函数和质量函数等大尺度统计量上一致，但在 $R_{200}$、球形度、致密度、自旋和质心偏移等结构属性上存在显著差异。GraphShed 在速度空间中能更好地区分邻近的过密度区域，从而改进了相互作用星系对的识别。

Conclusion: GraphShed provides a parameter-free alternative that maintains cosmological fidelity while enhancing the resolution of internal structure and dynamical interactions in galaxy group catalogs, making it a promising tool for precision studies of galaxy formation and environment. \n\n GraphShed 提供了一种无参数的替代方案，在保持宇宙学保真度的同时，提升了星系群星表中内部结构和动力学相互作用的分辨能力，使其成为星系形成与环境精密研究的有力工具。

Abstract: In this study, a parameter-free group-finding method named GraphShed is introduced and evaluated using the IllustrisTNG100-1 simulation. The method utilizes top-down watershed segmentation applied to the set of separated Voronoi-induced graphs, facilitating the recognition of aggregations directly from the density field without tunable parameters or density thresholds. A galaxy group catalog constructed with GraphShed is compared with a Friends-of-Friends catalog generated from the same dataset. The $M_{200}$ distributions of the two catalogs are statistically consistent; nevertheless, other structural properties, including $R_{200}$, sphericity, compactness, spin, and centroid shift show significant differences, suggesting that GraphShed could improve several internal characteristics of the identified systems. Conversely, the two-point correlation function and the mass function of the identified galaxy systems, derived from the aforementioned methods, show consistency. A velocity-based classification of interacting pairs indicates that GraphShed provides improved separation of nearby over-densities which might otherwise be considered as components of a single larger system in position-only methods due to their positional proximity. These results demonstrate that GraphShed effectively preserves cosmological statistics while offering a more refined detection of galaxy systems and their dynamical interactions.

</details>


### [89] [Biased tracers, Hybrid Effective Field Theory and Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03860)
*Guilherme Brando,Baojiu Li,Kazuya Koyama*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper extends the Hybrid Effective Field Theory (HEFT) framework for modeling biased tracer power spectra to $f(R)$ modified gravity cosmologies, addressing challenges posed by scale-dependent growth and chameleon screening. It derives analytical loop-corrected power spectra within local Lagrangian bias and proposes a method to generalize existing $Λ$CDM emulators like \texttt{bacco} and \texttt{Aemulus} to beyond-$Λ$CDM scenarios.
\n\n
本文将用于建模偏置示踪剂功率谱的 Hybrid Effective Field Theory (HEFT) 框架扩展至 $f(R)$ 改良引力宇宙学，应对由尺度依赖增长和 chameleon screening 所带来的挑战。文章在 local Lagrangian bias 方案下推导了包含圈修正的解析功率谱，并提出了一种将现有 $Λ$CDM 仿真器（如 \texttt{bacco} 和 \texttt{Aemulus}）推广到 beyond-$Λ$CDM 宇宙学的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurate modeling of biased tracer statistics is essential for extracting cosmological information from galaxy surveys. While perturbative approaches in both Eulerian and Lagrangian frameworks are well-established in $Λ$CDM, their extension to modified gravity theories—particularly $f(R)$ gravity with its nonlinear chameleon mechanism and scale-dependent linear growth—is nontrivial. Existing HEFT-based emulators are limited to $Λ$CDM, necessitating a generalization to test gravity on cosmological scales.
\n\n
从星系巡天中提取宇宙学信息需要对偏置示踪剂统计量进行精确建模。尽管 Eulerian 和 Lagrangian 框架下的微扰方法在 $Λ$CDM 中已成熟，但将其推广到改良引力理论（尤其是具有非线性 chameleon 机制和尺度依赖线性增长的 $f(R)$ 引力）并非易事。现有的基于 HEFT 的仿真器仅限于 $Λ$CDM，因此有必要将其推广以在宇宙学尺度上检验引力理论。

Method: The authors formulate the perturbative biased expansion using the local Lagrangian bias scheme within Lagrangian Perturbation Theory (LPT), adapted to $f(R)$ gravity. They compute the necessary growth functions accounting for scale dependence and chameleon screening, derive one-loop power spectra for biased tracers analytically, and validate these against N-body simulations in $f(R)$ cosmologies. They then outline a strategy to augment existing HEFT emulators trained on $Λ$CDM simulations by incorporating modified-gravity-specific responses or retraining with targeted simulation suites.
\n\n
作者在 Lagrangian Perturbation Theory (LPT) 框架内采用 local Lagrangian bias 方案构建适用于 $f(R)$ 引力的微扰偏置展开。他们计算了考虑尺度依赖性和 chameleon screening 的增长函数，解析推导了偏置示踪剂的一圈功率谱，并与 $f(R)$ 宇宙学中的 N-body simulations 结果进行对比验证。随后，他们提出通过引入改良引力特有的响应函数或使用针对性模拟数据集重新训练，来扩展现有基于 $Λ$CDM 模拟训练的 HEFT 仿真器。

Result: The paper demonstrates that analytical one-loop HEFT predictions in $f(R)$ gravity, when properly accounting for modified growth and screening, agree well with fully nonlinear N-body simulation results for biased tracers. This validates the feasibility of perturbative methods in such modified gravity scenarios. Furthermore, the proposed extension strategy shows how existing high-precision $Λ$CDM emulators can be adapted to beyond-$Λ$CDM cosmologies without requiring full re-simulation.
\n\n
论文表明，在正确计入改良增长和 screening 效应后，$f(R)$ 引力下的一圈 HEFT 解析预测与偏置示踪剂的完全非线性 N-body simulations 结果吻合良好，验证了在此类改良引力场景中使用微扰方法的可行性。此外，所提出的扩展策略展示了如何在无需完全重新模拟的情况下，将现有高精度 $Λ$CDM 仿真器适配到 beyond-$Λ$CDM 宇宙学。

Conclusion: Perturbative modeling of biased tracers via HEFT can be successfully generalized to $f(R)$ modified gravity, despite its theoretical complexities. The work provides a practical pathway to extend current emulation frameworks beyond $Λ$CDM, enabling efficient and accurate inference from upcoming large-scale structure surveys in tests of gravity.
\n\n
尽管存在理论复杂性，通过 HEFT 对偏置示踪剂的微扰建模仍可成功推广至 $f(R)$ 改良引力。本工作为将当前仿真框架扩展到 $Λ$CDM 之外提供了实用路径，使得未来大尺度结构巡天在引力检验中能够实现高效且精确的参数推断。

Abstract: The modelling of the power spectrum of biased tracers has become a central topic in the analysis of modern cosmological galaxy surveys. Perturbative templates formulated in both Eulerian and Lagrangian frameworks have been extensively developed over the last decades, with their implementation in $Λ$CDM thoroughly investigated and validated. In parallel, approaches combining perturbation theory with the output of dark-matter-only simulations have emerged as powerful tools for modelling the nonlinear regime, most notably the Hybrid Effective Field Theory (HEFT) framework~\cite{Modi:2019qbt}. In this work, we discuss the perturbative biased expansion within the local Lagrangian bias scheme and its implementation in the HEFT framework for modified gravity cosmologies. We focus on $f(R)$ gravity, a theory characterized by scale-dependent growth and chameleon screening, making it one of the most challenging scenarios for the computation of Lagrangian Perturbation Theory growth functions and for the generation of accurate numerical simulations. We present a detailed overview of the ingredients required to compute loop-corrected biased power spectra analytically and compare these predictions against fully non-perturbative simulation results. Finally, we propose a strategy to extend existing HEFT-based $Λ$CDM emulators, such as \texttt{bacco} and \texttt{Aemulus}, to beyond-$Λ$CDM cosmologies.

</details>
